# Remediation round 2026-09-20 — batching charter (final state, condensed 2026-10-06; extended through call 37, 2026-11-04; condensed to final state 2026-10-31 and at every call since; tiers per §12 since call 33)

**This is the INPUT for `/create-plan`, not a plan.** Each open group in §3 becomes one `/create-plan` invocation; point it at this file plus the group's issue numbers. The plan author re-reads every issue (bodies AND comments — rulings live in comments), pins a sha, and takes its own measurements with `sharpyc run`; the per-issue notes here summarize what was read, grepped and probed during batching and are hypotheses until the plan re-measures.

**Predecessor:** `remediation-round-2026-08-30-batching.md` (same directory) — its §2 done ledger, §4 rulings R-A…R-AV and §6 cross-cutting notes remain authoritative and are NOT copied here. Rulings in this round continue the sequence at R-AW.

**Condensed 2026-10-06 and 2026-10-14:** the call-by-call history (every census line, the per-call order extensions, the per-call cross-cutting notes, the full contracts of DONE groups and DONE chores) is folded into the state line, §1, §2, §3.1, §6 and §7. **Standing round rules** (from CLAUDE.md / `docs/design/verification-contract.md` — restate in every plan):
- Fix the class, not the cell (Rule 11); every batch names its violated contract and cell matrix; an issue's repro list is a symptom report.
- Every new guard is mutation-tested with red/green recorded in the commit body (Rule 12); absence assertions need a positive control; refusals are verified by direction with `run` against the prior commit (SPY0908 surfaces only under `run`).
- Commit gate = whole solution via `.claude/scripts/dotnet-serialized test --filter "Category!=Benchmark" --logger "console;verbosity=normal"` (`dangerouslyDisableSandbox: true`); filtered runs are the edit loop only. Every fixer's pre-report filter includes `DispatchSiteInventoryTests|StaticStateConformanceTests|EmitterCarrierOnly|FrontEndParity|SymbolFactMirrorConformanceTests|SinkPushingTotalityTests|LayoutFactConsumerScanTests` and the three GapDiscovery sweeps (`InteropConformance`, `MetamorphicCorpus`, `DifferentialExecution`) — component filters missed cross-cutting guards in the 2026-09-19, plan-791d3a and plan-bf0244 rounds.
- Verify Python behavior first with `python3 -c` (Rule 6; python3 here is 3.12.13; python3.14 at `/opt/homebrew/bin/python3.14` for PEP 750).
- Spec is authoritative (Rule 7); spec edits ship with executed examples (the spec-block sweep is a push gate). New SPY codes need `DiagnosticExplanations` entries (Rule 10).
- Regen spy-stdlib C#, spy-test C#, stdlib docs and the oracle ledger EARLY after Semantic/CodeGen changes; the `check_*_staleness.sh` scripts are the gate.
- Any new node-keyed `SemanticInfo` dictionary joins `SemanticInfo.MergeFrom` (Rule 2); new symbol-keyed facts freeze at `MaterializeCodeGenInfo`.
- Standing rule R-BG: every plan that touches a spec file drains that file's `spec_blocks_allowlist.txt` rows FIRST (prelude where genuine define-once/use-many, `fragment` only after review, else a real issue); P20 is the sweep for whatever no plan owns. **Extended by R-FY (2026-10-08):** once #2286's `output` marker has landed, a plan that touches a spec file also converts that file's output-stating blocks (prose "prints …", `# Output:`, trailing `# 3` comments) so the gate RUNS them.

## 1. Execution order (authoritative; owner-ruled through 2026-11-05, R-LJ; tiers backfilled per contract §12 at call 33)

Only OPEN work is listed. Everything done is in §2. "FIRST" = the first issue inside the group. "Alone" = lands on its own measured gate (printed output, emitted layout or a Stdlib C# surface moves). "Gen FIRST" = a generative/differential harness is the group's first task (contract §11).

**Tier** (contract §12; R-JR): `band` = no plan, one commit each, the band's gate verifies; `light` = `/create-plan --light` → `/verify-plan --adequacy` (matrix arms, guard mutations, design predicates only) → `/implement-plan` → `/verify-implementation` (Agents 2, 3, 4; completeness by the lead); `full` = all four stages in full. A group is `full` only for a named letter: (a) open decision / unpromoted DEVIATION surface at planning, (b) `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`, (c) seam in `Semantic/`·`Lowering/`·`CodeGen/` or `alone`, (d) residue of a group that already passed a verify round, (e) owner's call. Tiers move only upward after planning begins. Done rows carry `—`; a group in flight keeps the tier its stages have run at.

**Ordering rationale (owner-confirmed, cumulative; the per-call reasoning lives in the rulings named, §4):** bands — silent-wrong first (P34/P33/P35/P56/P13b, then P11g/P21d/P25), ICEs on common shapes next (P52/P51, P72, P21e, P26, P28, P30), refusal parity after, and the groups that turn silent acceptance into refusals (P18, P48, P18b, P27, P29, P31) late on measured gates; P45 last. P54 sits first so every later gate runs on a determinism-checked suite; the instrument groups P63 and P68 (R-GC, R-GR), the two short keyword-respelling gates P69 → P70 (R-GW: no users and no stability claim before 1.0; P68's C7 roster guard lands just before) and the pulled-forward P37 → P16b (R-GX: `T !E` usable in every position early; inverts the old 'P16 before P16b' preference) sit directly behind it. Editor and tooling residues queue BEHIND P34 and P13b: the P22 family is capped at P22i (R-GF; the 'warm harness' slotting argument is retired after R-FT, R-GC, R-GE), and the rule was applied to P47's residues (R-GO), P53's (R-GU, R-GZ) and the REPL (R-HC) — only a regression by direction is remediated in-round. Design issues are SCHEDULED, not parked (R-FJ): P57 after P51, P58 after P37, P59 after P17d. P60 directly after P57 rather than as a P17e (every #2277 refusal cell lands on an existing code — SPY0335, SPY0486, SPY0277 — or R-FW's new one, so P17's sentinel adds nothing); P61 directly after P60 so the ledger exists before P36 moves files and P38 learns whether `collections` is promotable; P62 before P45, after the syntax-adding groups. P41 (throughput) landed, so each remaining gate is cheaper. **R-HK (2026-10-15):** P21e, re-scoped to declaration-position totality, sits directly behind the chores band because its generator already exists (P54's `TypeDefinitionOwnSymbolMatrixTests`), every verdict it needs is ruled (R-CS, R-HJ, SPY0212's rule) and it drains an ICE on an ordinary shape plus two spec-block allowlist rows — the premise that held it behind P21c (#2148's B6 arms) moved with #2148 to the bindings plan. **R-HQ (2026-10-20):** P63b (the typed-generator injection-reach residue) sits directly after P21e's gate, before P69 — the instrument argument that placed P63 early (R-GC) was about the instrument existing and being honest, which P63 delivered; #2324 widens its reach, so the chores band and the #2063 ICE drain are not delayed by a second tests-only plan, and the generator still precedes every group from P69 on. **R-HY (2026-10-21):** P22c moves from row 31 to directly after P63b — it is a COMPILER group whose every cell is program-reach (R-CO's `rf"…"`/`F"…"` refusals, #2129's `\"` fill, #2044's `{{` cell that prints `{{{5|`, #2337's `df"""` cells that run and print altered text — silent-wrong in effect), so R-GF/R-GO's "editor residues behind Core silent-wrong" reasoning does not apply; it lands behind the #2339 chore (same literal reader) and before P69/P70's keyword codemods, on the harnesses P68 just built. **R-JQ (2026-10-28):** P74 sits directly after P22c, before P69 — an ICE (a lambda in a later overload's slot reaches Roslyn), a false refusal (overloads narrowed by a non-lambda argument) and an order-dependent verdict (overloads differing only by the lambda's parameter type) on the ordinary `apply(x, lambda …)` shape; the keyword codemods have no stake in it and the #2391 theory is its warm control. **R-JR (2026-10-28):** tiers are backfilled per §12 — the light groups are the LSP/CLI/formatter/Core-twin groups and, by (e), the two keyword codemods and the two tests-only instruments. **R-JU (2026-10-29):** P75 sits directly after P21i, before P22c — a silent-wrong on an ordinary class shape (a `@static` field or `const` derived from a `@static` property runs `0`), and the only cure for P21h's temporary refusal, which otherwise becomes the false refusal users meet first. **R-JW (2026-10-29):** the P21h residue goes BY CLASS — one residue plan (P21j, after P74) instead of R-JF's three; the P30 and P16 cells wait for the generators those rows were ruled under, since every reach-program ICE among them is on a C#-habit spelling (explicit interface implementation, bare `N()` where Python writes `C.N()`). **R-KI (2026-11-03):** the name-resolution block (P77a → P77 → P78 → P79 → P80 → P52) sits directly after P76, before P22c/P74/P21k — silent-wrong on ordinary shapes (module initializers run AFTER `main` starts, A11; a module var reading a later computed const prints `1 7`, E01; a cross-type chain `0 0 5`, D01) and the common false refusal (a function reading a module var declared below it, B01/M03), and the only cure for P75's SPY0618, which is otherwise the first refusal users meet on a forward static read — R-JU's reasoning applied to the rule that replaces it; the ICE groups P74/P21k wait about six gates.

**Hard dependencies still binding (done groups removed; superseded orderings struck in §4):**
- Instruments and chores: P54 (DONE) before P63; P21e's generator is P54's `TypeDefinitionOwnSymbolMatrixTests` (landed @ 7ae6ed761); P63's close-out (the #2288 record post) before the #2169 test-position chore (R-HP: the chore deletes the pin the roster RAW control and `ParsePins_StillFire` tolerate — landing it under the hardening agent would move the RAW control counts); P21e's gate before P63b (R-HQ); P63b before P69 (its generator is part of the codemod corpus); the §3.0 chores ride P21e's gate (seam-free); #2182 before #2322 before #2183 inside P42 (R-DN's diagnostic is #2322's door); P54 before #2261 part 2 (R-FF), before P55's `http.client` cell (`socket._DefaultTimeout` is one of P54's five globals) and before P42's door widening (R-HI: every SPY0306 refuses at every door once P54 removes the already-loaded-from-another-path leak that made fa7e8aa63 filter SPY0306 out — P42 re-measures those run-route cells after P54); #2289's next component runs after the group it scores has shipped; the #2313 cell-0 chore before P46b (its `ReferenceDeploymentMatrixTests` rows become P46b's honoured controls); #2282 → #2283 → #2284 → #2285 inside P61; P61 before P36 and P38. **Name-resolution block (R-KD, R-KI): P77a before every rule change (the owner's FIRST, Rule 13); P77 before P78 (forward reads need N3); P79 and P80 independent of P77/P78 but after P77a (their rows are its allowlist); P52 after P80 (same oracle; P77a gains its axis) and still before P51; P78 before P21b/P21c's SPY0611 text (its steer gains `global`); the #2365 draw-filter chore rides P76's gate; P77 lands ALONE.**
- Syntax band: P68 (the C7 roster guard; DONE) before P69/P70; **the #2339 chore before P22c (both rewrite `Lexer.Literals.cs`'s number reader; R-HX); P22c before P69 (R-HY: the prefix table lands before the keyword codemods walk the lexer)**; P69/P70 before P62; P59 and P21c before P62; P37 before P16b (#2307's per-position gate needs the #2303 cell drained); P16b BEFORE P16 (R-GX; P16's nested-under-constructed-generic carrier extends P16b's reader); P16 before P17 (the constructor-ring vocabulary reads the alias and nested-type carriers); **P21g before P22c (R-JA); the call-31 set-only setter chore before P70 (its fixtures join the `prop` codemod corpus).** **P22c before P74 before P69 (R-JQ; order only — P74 shares no seam with either).** **P21i before P75 before P22c (R-JU); P75 retires P21h's temporary SPY0200 arm, so a plan touching `variable_scoping.md:384-388` waits for it; P74 before P21j before P69 (R-JW; order only); the #2399 chore rides P22c's gate, never P21i's (both touch `CodeGenInfoComputer`); the #2395 chore rides P21i's gate (property emission is not a P21i seam).**
- Core/semantic band: P13b's #2240 before P51's #2246 (R-EU); P52 before P51 (the binder's Unknown source would appear in P51's generator as noise); P51 before P57 (P57's position axis reuses P51's generator positions); P57 before P60; P37, P23 and P13b before P58 (P37's generator gains the `.X` expression value; enum members are P58's v1 payload; `case .RED:` is a P13b pattern head); P11g before P23 and P32 (both read the python-name channel); #2105 (P11g, R-DG) owns a function's python name, so the thread-name ` (target)` suffix lands there (R-FD); P38 before P49; P40 (done) before P22d.

**Lessons from the done rounds that still bind:**
- Meta-class **cold-path construction inline on a hot path** (P76, #2404 is its tracker): an interpolated/allocating throw inside an accessor body defeats the JIT inline budget; standard cure = return-the-exception `[NoInlining]` helpers at ONE seam + a static IL-shape guard that reads inlinability itself (the method's own `NoInlining`, which `NoInlining` callees are allowed, `newobj` targets) — a shape-only scan stayed green while N1 re-created the measured defect.
- P14c retired `#1919`'s merge rule: a generic class named like its module stays REFUSED uniformly; the general cure landed as module-as-namespace (`module_system.md`).
- "Any CLR `IFormattable` owns its spec" hid CLR types python classifies as NUMBERS — when a rule keys on an interface, check which types python classifies differently.
- `"…".format(…)` is a `FunctionSymbol` from `BuiltinRegistry.DiscoverStringExtensionMethods`; `ParameterSignature.FormatSpecOf` goes through the overload-index cache (bump it); a registry type change needs BOTH cache bumps (`OverloadIndexCache` AND `IncrementalCompilationCache`).
- Compiler.Tests alone is ~1.5 h — run the gate in the background as its own call; laptop sleep skews trx spans; verify agents stall on the lock unless backgrounded.
- The charter's "T2 skip set drains to EMPTY" acceptance for P40 was refuted at `/create-plan` (8 of 10 escaped twins unparseable) — a charter acceptance is a hypothesis until the plan measures it.
- P22e's contract ("when the lexer has lost a literal, no indent-only route edits") was REFUTED at verify by three abort-free loss mechanisms its generator could not build (S5 always aborts) — a generator that samples one failure shape per state guards one shape; the residue builds the twin FIRST (P22f).
- The gate peak is GC-elastic (18.5 GB idle / 8.9 GB loaded; Lsp's peak is one test's garbage → `HeapHardLimit` 3 GB); the documented gate command carries the console logger (#2257).
- A first restore with `shutil.copy2` reuses the mutant build (mtime kept) — restore with `shutil.copy`.
- `/verify-implementation` closes issues with evidence; a closed issue is not a finished issue (#1939 stays open until P20's reviewed pass; #1893 by design).

**Numbers cited only in the folded texts (2026-10-31 condense; bodies in `remediation-round-2026-09-20-batching.pre-call35-2026-10-31.md`):** #1036

### 3.2b P67 — node end-position authority (Parser + LSP position readers; R-GN; generator FIRST; directly after P13b per R-GO)

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract (#2295's, with #2297 folded):** a node's `LineEnd`/`ColumnEnd` is the position just past its OWN last token — for every token kind (a multi-line triple-quoted, f-string, raw or bytes literal) and every node kind (a stub-bodied `def …: ...`, an interface property/event stub, a field closing a block) — never a line of the statement that follows. **Matrix:** last-token kind {single-line token, triple-quoted, f-string, raw, bytes} × node kind {expression, simple statement, inline-stub def, interface property/event stub, field, block} × followed-by {statement at the same indent, dedent, EOF} × consumer {folding range, document symbol, selection range, hover/highlight, each refactoring}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program (every interface stub — 66 fixtures carry the shape; any statement ending in a multi-line string). **Fidelity-scope row:** n/a (not a Python surface). **Standing harness:** `ParserSuiteSeamConformanceTests` + the #1736 suite-extent matrix in `ParserPositionTests` — both keyed on Dedent sites, blind to both axes; the plan says so. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — parse the fixture corpus and, for every node, assert `LineEnd`/`ColumnEnd` equals the end of its last own token reconstructed from the token stream (tokens AND node ends; the same instrument is #2300's C2 row — whichever of P67/P68 lands first builds it, the other extends it); allowlist ratchet; the consumer matrix is the hand axis (the generator cannot enumerate consumers). **Class tracker:** #2295.
- **#2295** (FIRST; tracker; R-GN) — silent-wrong · program; cells measured by P47 @ 7f0502de3 (Wrap/Extract leave a multi-line string's tail line behind — compensated in P47 by `Extent.StatementEndLine`) and, folded from #2297, @ e4a24985f over stdio: documentSymbol of `def name(self) -> str: ...` ending an interface ends at `(3, 0)` (the next `class A:` line), foldingRange `0–3` swallows `class A:`; positive controls: a class ending in a field ends `(4, 15)`, a def with a real body `(7, 12)`. **Ruling R-GN (a):** one class, one group; #2297 folded. Rejected: (b) two groups on the same sites and consumer matrix. Internals are in the ruling (§4), read grep-only — the plan measures. The "class field ending a block" sentence in the four source comments was NOT reproduced by the #2297 probe; re-measure with a following statement at the same indent before counting it.
- **#2338** (R-HX; filed by the P68 implementation round, extended by its verify round) — silent-wrong · program. **Contract (the token half of P67's):** a token's (line, column) is the position of its first character in the SOURCE — a line break consumed inside a literal advances the line counter exactly as one outside it. **Measured (the issue @ 96d7a2792):** `FStringEnd` stamped at L5:C24 in fixture `strings/d_string_fstring.spy` (the line break inside the literal was consumed without advancing `_line`); `ProcessEscapeSequence` consumes `\n` with no `_line++`, so an SPY0015 after it reports (2:5) instead of (3:5); verify-round cells: backslash+CRLF consumes only `\r` → an extra SPY0026 under CRLF; a `d"""` cell reports SPY0001 one line short on LF and CR. Five `lexer-position-allowlist.txt` rows (lines 20, 25–28) cite it — the issue-state gate keeps #2338 open until they drain. Cells: consumer {escape processor, `df` pre-close path, backslash-continuation, dedent prescan} × line ending {LF, CRLF, CR} × following diagnostic; generator = `TokenPositionReconstruction` (P68's; every row above is one of its allowlist entries). **Why P67, not a chore:** `TokenPositionReconstruction` and P67's node-end differential assert ONE contract (a position maps to its own source slice) and P67's "line of the statement that follows" cells and #2338's "line short" cells are the same off-by-a-line class on two producers; a `_line` fix without the node-end half leaves the LSP consumers reading compensated values. The #2338 cells land FIRST inside P67 (lexer before parser), each with a direction record on its allowlist row. Rejected (R-HX): a P73 residue group; P22c (a position defect, not the prefix table). Two-way.

By design NO snapshot moves (positions, not layout). Measured gate: Compiler.Tests + Lsp.Tests + the three sweeps.

### 3.2c P65 — Extract synthesized-declaration inference (LSP only; generator EXISTS; after P67, BEFORE P66)

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract (#2294's):** an Extract action's synthesized declaration names exactly the region's free variables that are bound OUTSIDE the region and read inside it, each spelled so the document resolves it; builtins and names first bound inside the region are never parameters; the call site passes only names defined there; an Extract variable annotation is a spelling the document parses and resolves, or no annotation. **Matrix:** name kind {builtin function, builtin type, module-level name, enclosing-function local bound before the region, local first bound inside, local bound inside and read after} × use {read, write, read-then-write} × type shape {int, function type, generic `T`, tuple initializer, lambda, Optional}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Fidelity-scope row:** n/a. **Standing harness / generator:** EXISTS — P47's `CodeActionShapeSweepTests` `diagnostics` + `parse` buckets (16 of 55 pins cite #2294; `extract-method identity diagnostics 2872` stems, `parse 532`, `extract-variable diagnostics 220`, `parse 56`, ×4 twins) — its **apertures widen FIRST** (recorded on #2232 comment 6131002949): pins are per (action, twin, bucket, cite) over STEMS, so a new failing cell on a pinned stem is invisible; `CiteOf` judges Extract method's FIRST parse error (cite-by-position narrowed this for Extract variable only); `DiagnosticQuickFixProvider.NameToken` finds the name as the first quoted word of the diagnostic MESSAGE (carry the span in `Data`); two indent-unit sources (`DefaultIndentWidth` vs `SourceLines.IndentUnit`); `SweepText.EndLine` is one line lenient on block-ending statements; a Rewrite with a declared removal is unjudged by `DecoratorVerdict`. **Class tracker:** #2294.
- **#2294** (FIRST; tracker) — silent-wrong · program; cells @ 7f0502de3 / 0c0470a35: `print` read in the region → `print: Callable[[object | None], None]` parameter (SPY0202 `Callable` not found) and `x` first assigned inside → passed at the call (SPY0200); a function-typed local → `Callable[[list[int32], int32], int32]` (parse error); a generic function's `T` → `[T]` (SPY0114); Extract variable on `a = 1, 2` → `result: tuple[int32, int32] = 1,2` (SPY0103); lambda / `T`-typed initializers. `int32` is a documented spelling (`generics.md`, `for_statement.md`, …) so `int` → `int32` is a spelling-fidelity cell on R-DL's footing, not a resolution defect; Sharpy's function-type spelling is the arrow form (`function_types.md`), never `Callable[...]`. No seam hypothesis — the plan measures (`FormatTypeAnnotation` and the data-flow analysis are the lead's reading).
- **Why before P66:** 2,872 `diagnostics` stems are pinned under #2294 by cite-by-first-error; #2296's cells on those stems surface only after P65 drains them. By design NO snapshot moves. Blast radius: Lsp.Tests only (+ the FULL sweep).

### 3.2d P66 — code-action meaning preservation (LSP only; R-GQ, R-GS; chore R-GP lands first; generator EXISTS; after P65, before P52)

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract (#2296's):** an LSP code action's applied text has the same error-diagnostic code multiset as the document it was applied to — or the action is not offered; a refactoring that changes what the program binds, infers or resolves is not a refactoring. **Matrix:** action {Wrap in try/except, Remove type annotation, Inline variable, Inline function, Rename to (SPY0200 and SPY0453 quick fixes), Remove unused import / Organize Imports, Convert to match, Convert to if/elif/else, Implement interface} × binding fact {a name declared in the region and read after it, inferred type ≠ annotation, a reference in another scope, a suggestion out of scope, an import flagged unused that is used, a comparand whose source spelling is lost, an Optional scrutinee (`case None:`), a read-write interface property} × position {function body, module level, nested def, type body}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Fidelity-scope row:** n/a. **Standing harness / generator:** EXISTS — the sweep's `diagnostics` bucket (39 of 55 pins cite #2296: `wrap-try 2320`, `remove-annotation 516`, `inline-variable 135`, `implement 46`, `inline-function 45`, `rename 38`, `organize-imports`/`remove-import 17`, `prefix-underscore 7`, `to-if 4` stems, ×4 twins) — drains after P65 unmasks the shared stems; the `offer` bucket gains a POSITION axis (the same statement inside an `elif`/`else`/`except`/`finally`/`case` body vs an `if`/`for` body as the positive control; a parenthesized condition / negative-literal comparand / or-pattern for Convert forms) so the seven offer-gap cells on #2296's tooling thread become allowlist rows instead of prose. **Class tracker:** #2296.
- **#2296** (FIRST; tracker) — silent-wrong · program; cells @ 0c0470a35 / 5a5ad3e63 (body + three comments): Wrap binds `x: int = 1` inside the `try` and `print(x)` after it is SPY0200 (≈3.7k rows); Remove type annotation where the inferred type differs (SPY0227 ×728) and at module level (`k = 1` → SPY0340); Inline across scopes (SPY0200 ×339); Rename to an out-of-scope suggestion; the SPY0453 naming fix renames the declaration only (`MathHelper.PiValue` left behind); import removal adds SPY0200 ×14 / removes an erroring import's error (may be an analyzer fact — SPY0452 disagreeing with use — the plan measures which); Convert to match `r"\d"` → `case r"\\d":` (SPY0120 under a typed scrutinee; doubled value under an untyped one) and `1_000` → `1000`, `'a'` → `"a"`, `"""tq"""` → `"tq"`; Convert to if on an Optional scrutinee `case None:` → `if x == None:` (SPY0222; `is None` expected); Implement interface on `property name: str` emits a getter stub only (`properties_inheritance.md`: both accessors). **Ruling R-GS (a):** Wrap is NOT OFFERED when a name declared in the region is read after it (a local cannot be declared without an initializer, `variable_declaration.md:198`, so no hoisted legal text exists). **Ruling R-GQ (a):** convert-forms header literals are the SOURCE TOKEN verbatim when legal in the target form; a raw-string comparand under a typed scrutinee re-spells as its cooked equivalent (`case "\\d":`, measured to parse by the verify round); else not offered. **Chore R-GP (a)** restores base's not-offered verdict on the nested-def cell first (§3.0). Rejected alternatives in §4.
- **#2232 leftovers (posted on #2296 at call 20):** cell 1 — if→match with a multi-line-string COMPARAND `if x == """a\nb""":` → `case "a\\nb":` @ 5a5ad3e63 (parses, same value, the tail line vanishes; sweep-blind because the `line` oracle exempts re-spelled headers) — a row of R-GQ, same root as the raw-string row; cell 2 — Remove unused import on a mixed run `from m import (\n    a, b,  # ab\n    c,  # c\n)` with `b`, `c` unused, UNMEASURED (by reading `ImportNames.RemovalSpans` the run `b..c` is deleted as one span taking the kept name `a`'s comment) — measure first, then a cell of the import row or a pin.
- **#2232 (REOPENED 2026-10-29 by the P21h `/verify-implementation` remediation; call 34 — by class, no ruling):** tooling · program; Inline variable on a module variable `x: int = 5` whose name a class-body initializer reads bare (`class C: @static x: int = x + 1`; the read binds the MODULE variable — owner ruling 2026-10-29 (a), the same binding as @ 9aae96977) produces text that does not parse — the inline also rewrites the same-named member declaration; the inherited-read-bound-to-a-module-variable shape (ruling R1 (b)) the same. Pinned as `inline-variable identity parse … # #2232` in `code-action-shape-allowlist.txt` from the fixture `classes/class_body_inherited_read_outer_binding_2384`; drains with this issue. P47's contract ("a rewrite code action never turns a parsing document into one that does not parse") is this group's rule in its parse arm; the sweep's `parse` bucket is the generator. Pre-existing (same binding at 9aae96977), newly spelled by the corpus.

### 3.4b P53b — editor analysis-input & route × state residue (P53 residue; Compiler `Semantic/` + LSP; R-GU; generator FIRST; directly after P66)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (P53's, widened):** the editor's per-file diagnostic set after ANY route equals the build's per-file set on the editor's document set — every re-analysis is the same door as the open analysis (path, options, import closure), every phase reads a file's open buffer over its disk text, and a route that changes the project's file set or validity republishes from the new state. **Matrix:** route {didOpen, BodyOnly edit, structural edit, didClose, watched Created/Changed/Deleted, `.spyproj` change} × state {single-file vs project, listed vs unlisted, buffer ≡ disk vs buffer ≠ disk, `.spyproj` valid vs invalid} × edited file {entry, imported, importer} × phase {parse, import resolution, cycle check, type check}. **Lane / reach:** false-refusal · program (#2301, #2304); tooling · program (#2309). **Standing harness:** P53's E2E `ProjectDiagnosticRoutesTests` + the `FrontEndParity` project arm — neither has the BodyOnly-edit route, the buffer≠disk state on the IMPORTED file, the invalid-project state or the edited-file axis. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** (residue group; ≥2 axes × ≥3 values) — the route driver enumerates route × state × edited file with the overlaid `sharpyc project` as the oracle, allowlist ratchet. **Blast radius:** the two compiler seams reach Compiler.Tests and the three sweeps, not only Lsp.Tests. No seam hypothesis beyond what the issues measured — the plan re-measures.
- **#2301** (FIRST; tracker) — false-refusal · program; `ScopedTypeChecker.RecheckFunction` → `api.Analyze(text)` with no path/options/closure, so every BodyOnly edit in a file importing a sibling publishes SPY0300 + SPY0451 (7-row route table measured @ c245ece62 over stdio; `sharpyc` clean); project mode hides it since P53 made the project route the sole publisher, but the hover/completion cache still stores it.
- **#2304** (tracker) — false-refusal · program; `ModuleLoader.LoadModule` / `CreateStubModuleInfo` / `PackageResolver` read `.spy` from disk and the buffer overlay reaches `ProjectCompiler.ReadSource` only — a buffer-only add/rename/privatize of an export gives dependents a false SPY0301 or a missing refusal (@ 202a78e72, same @ 8be4576fd); the verify round's axis: the IMPORTED file's own import/cycle diagnostics are disk-stale (`SPY0300 nosuch` after the buffer removed it). Standard cure the issue names: one source-reader seam (overlay first, then disk) + a census guard keeping `File.ReadAllText` on `.spy` paths out of `Semantic/`.
- **#2309** (tracker; #2242's successor) — tooling · program; a watched Deleted of a listed file publishes SPY0903 on the deleted URI until a `.spyproj` reload (stale `<Compile>` list); an invalid `.spyproj` keeps the old project sticky (code-read, not probed: `InitializeProjectAsync`'s catch nulls only the workspace config); its cell 4 (definition on the `from m import x` name token) is posted on #2308 (P71). Ride-along: `ProjectModel.UnattributedDiagnosticTarget` re-implements `ProjectCompiler.IsEntryPointFileForTypeCheck` — one shared rule.
- **Call 23 cell on #2309 (P46 Phase 3 lane @ 0afdad634; silent-wrong · program):** a `.spyproj` `<Reference>` whose `HintPath` file appears or vanishes on disk with no `.spy`/`.spyproj` event leaves SPY0305 as last published — the watcher registers `**/*.spy` and `**/*.spyproj` only, while the analysis cache key already covers reference mtimes (a later `main.spy` change clears/restores it). The watched-input set ≠ the analysis-input set: the generator's state axis gains {reference file present, absent} with "no editor event" as the route.

### 3.4c P71 — navigation request × spelling × position totality (LSP only; R-GZ; generator EXISTS; directly after P53b)

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract:** every symbol-identity request (definition, implementation, type-definition, references, document highlight, rename, call-hierarchy prepare) resolves the symbol the checker bound, for every spelling of the use {bare, `w.m`, `m.x`, `pkg.m.x`, `import m as n` alias} and every position {use site, type annotation (local / parameter / return), base class, type argument, import-statement name token}, against every target {same file, compiled unit, extracted module}; a use the checker bound is a recorded reference. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program (#2302: rename from the declaration leaves `w.render()` calling a method that no longer exists); tooling · program (#2308). **Standing harness:** P53's `LSP-project-nav` parity pairs (248/248, allowlist 0 @ 27e261684) cover definition/implementation/hover on imported-name spellings only — the generator gains the request and position axes. **Cure shape (the issues', read not measured):** ONE symbol-resolution entry point for every identity request (P53's `MemberAccessSymbolResolver` shape — one resolver reading one recorded fact; `ISemanticQuery.GetTypeAnnotation` already carries the resolved type) + `SemanticInfo.RecordReference` reached from member-access and type-annotation bindings; routing references through the resolver alone is not enough (rename from the declaration would still miss the uses). Shares the resolver with P66's rename-quick-fix cell — whichever lands first builds it.

### 3.4d P72 — REPL snippet totality (Compiler `Services/ReplSession` + Cli `ReplCommand` + one semantic verdict; R-HB, R-HC; generator FIRST; directly after P71, before P52)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`).

**Contract:** the REPL's two output channels read the NEW snippet only — an expression statement at the prompt runs its expression and is echoed only when the expression has a value (Python's `displayhook`: a `None`/void result echoes nothing), and a diagnostic is shown once, when the snippet that causes it is entered. **Matrix:** snippet kind {void call (function, `-> None`, method, `xs.append`), valued call, literal, `None`, name, assignment, `def`, `class`, `import`, syntax error} × session position {first, after a `def` with a warning, after a class, after an error} × channel {stdout echo, diagnostics}. **Lane / reach:** ice · program (#2310 — `xs.append(2)` at the prompt is SPY0908 CS1503, the most common prompt statement after `print`); message · program (#2312). **Python twin (verified at batching, python3 3.12.13 `-i`):** `g()` prints `x` and echoes nothing; `h()` with `-> int` echoes `3`; `None` echoes nothing; `3` echoes `3`. **Standing harness:** the cross-front-end parity probe (`ReplSession.ProbeFrontEndDiagnostics`) takes ONE input, not a session — blind to both by construction. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** (2 axes × ≥3 values) — a transcript corpus over the matrix fed to `sharpyc repl` (stdin) and to `python3 -i`; the stdout channel is a differential (Sharpy-only snippets carry an expected literal), the diagnostics channel asserts each (code, position) appears exactly once per transcript; allowlist ratchet. **Class trackers:** #2310, #2312. **Rule 2 note:** the echo verdict (void vs valued expression statement) is a semantic fact produced in analysis and read by the session — the plan decides node-keyed `SemanticInfo` (→ `MergeFrom`) versus a pre-analysis pass; the emitter decides nothing. Blast radius: Compiler.Tests (`ReplSessionTests`, `FrontEndParity`), Cli.Tests. By design no snapshot moves.
- **#2310** (R-HB R-HC; FIRST; tracker) — ice · program; six cells measured @ d6388d42f (void function, `-> None`, void method, `xs.append(2)` all CS1503; `print("y")` and a valued `h()` are the controls). Internals read at batching (grep-only @ 3f16f261e): the session rewrites a lone expression statement to `print(<input>)` as TEXT before analysis (`ReplSession.cs:228`), so the echo is decided by syntax; `print` works only because it is itself the wrapper. Cure shape the issue names: the echo decision reads the expression's semantic type at the one place the session builds the echo — not a per-callee-kind patch. **R-IY (call 30, 2026-10-24): the REPL mixed-submission split (eb9884bb4, P21e lead ruling under R-CS) is CONFIRMED** — imports, from-imports and type definitions of a mixed submission register at module level (through `UnwrapDecorated`), `def`/`type`/declarations/executable statements stay in `main()` in source order, both halves commit together and only on success; the corpus's snippet-kind axis gains {mixed submission}. Rejected: revert.
- **#2312** (tracker) — message · program; `def f():` with an unused local, then two `print`s → SPY0451 printed 3 times @ 739ae5416 (same on the pre-P46 binary c245ece62). Internals (grep-only): every snippet re-runs the pipeline on the accumulated session and carries every warning forward (`ReplSession.cs:354`). Cure shape: diagnostics are filtered to the new snippet (by line range of the appended text, or by de-duplication against the shown set — the plan measures which survives a redefinition of `f`).

### 3.6b″ P63c — injection instrument residue (Compiler.Tests only; R-IX; P63b residue; generator EXISTS; directly after P72, before P52)

### 3.6d P69 — variance respelled `produces T` / `consumes T` (Lexer + parser + Validation + formatter + LSP + TextMate + spec + generators; R-GW; codemod; directly after P68 and the #2261 part-2 chore)

**Tier:** light (e: owner's call (R-JR: a keyword codemod / a tests-only instrument with no door) — the §12 letters would say full).

**Contract (#2305's, owner-ruled 2026-10-13 on #1974):** a type parameter's variance has exactly ONE spelling — `produces T` (covariant), `consumes T` (contravariant), hard keywords, interfaces and delegates only (the CLR rule; `class Source[produces T]` is invalid) — and every surface that reads or prints variance uses it: lexer, parser, `VarianceValidator` (SPY0417–SPY0419) messages, formatter/unparser, LSP hover/completion/semantic tokens (a CLR type displays as `IEnumerable[produces T]`), the VS Code TextMate grammar, the spec (`generic_variance.md`, `delegates.md`, `function_types.md`), the CsCheck generators. C# emission stays `out`/`in`. `in T`/`out T` on a type parameter becomes a refusal naming the replacement, with an LSP quick-fix; no dual spelling. **Measured @ fce79bf92 (owner):** 26 `.spy`/`.md` files (29 `.spy` lines); `produces`/`consumes` appear only in comments. **Lane / reach:** tooling · program. **Standing harness:** P68's C7 keyword-roster test (lands directly before) — cite it, don't widen it (the #2300 C7 / #2048 class). **Acceptance (the issue's):** the roster changes in one commit guarded by the roster test; every variance fixture migrated by a codemod with byte-identical regenerated C# (empty diff) + a positive control; the old spelling refused, mutation-tested in both directions; direction record against the prior commit. No snapshot moves by design (C# emission unchanged).
- **#2305** (FIRST; tracker) — tooling · program.

### 3.6e P70 — `property` → `prop` (Lexer + diagnostics + formatter + LSP + TextMate + spec + stdlib sources + generators; R-GW; lands ALONE; directly after P69)

**Contract (#2306's, owner-ruled 2026-10-13 on #1974):** the property keyword has exactly ONE spelling, `prop` (hard keyword): `prop get name: T`, `prop init name: T = v`, `prop set name: T`, `prop get name(self) -> T:`, `@static prop get …` — accessor words and C# emission unchanged; every surface uses it: lexer, parser, validator messages (incl. the SPY0148 `@property` steer), formatter/unparser, LSP (completion, hover, semantic tokens, code actions that synthesize properties — Implement interface), TextMate, spec, the stdlib `.spy` sources and generated docs, CsCheck generators; `property` becomes a refusal naming `prop` with a quick-fix; no long-lived alias. ### 3.7 P34 — ordering engine parity (Core + one checker arm and one lowering since R-IP; R-CK, R-IP)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** every ordering surface (`sorted`, `list.sort`, `min`/`max`, `heapq`, `bisect`) — and, since R-IP, the `<` `<=` `>` `>=` operators on tuple operands, lowered onto the same engine — compares with python's operator through ONE comparer that discovers `__lt__` as `operator <` (the dunder table, rung 2) as well as `IComparable`, evaluates `key=` ONCE per element (COMPARED, R-CK), and refuses a non-comparable pair with python's catchable `TypeError` at the first comparison on every surface; WHICH operand the refusal names is a deviation (R-CK, ledger row `sort-refusal-operand-order`). **Matrix:** surface × element kind {IComparable, user `__lt__`, list-of-lists, non-comparable struct, non-comparable class, `T | None`} × `key=` {absent, side-effecting} × observation {order, key call count, exception type}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Standing harness:** the #2085 refusal matrix (Core.Tests). **Generator:** not required (closed surface × kind axes; the plan enumerates them). **Class tracker:** #2090.
- **#2090** (R-CK; FIRST) — silent-wrong · program; `sorted(xs, key=k)` calls `k` 18 times for 5 elements (python 5). **Ruling R-CK (D3 b, 2026-09-27):** key call count COMPARED (decorate-sort-undecorate in Core; both `Sorted<T,TKey>` and `List.Sort<TKey>` through the one engine); operand order stays DEVIATION (#2089 closed to the ledger row).
- **#2091** — runtime-throw · program; `xs.sort()` on non-comparable structs crashes with `InvalidOperationException` (the value-type fast path calls the CLR default comparer) while `sorted(xs)` raises python's `TypeError`; the fast path applies only to `IComparable<T>` elements.
- **#2103** — false-refusal · program (runtime): `sorted([V(2), V(1)])` with a user `__lt__` throws `TypeError` (`ComparerAdapter` reads `IComparable` only — the #1933 meta-class); `sorted([[2], [1, 0]])` likewise although `[2] < [1, 0]` compiles; static side inconsistent (`heapq`/`bisect` refuse SPY0237 while `sorted` compiles and throws).
- **#2356** (R-IP; after #2103) — false-refusal · program (COMPARED, fidelity row 1). **Measured @ 6c95019b8 (built CLI, lock-free):** `sorted([(2, 1), (1, 2), (1, 1)])`, `max([(1, 2), (1, 3)])`, `min([("b", 1), ("a", 2)])`, `sorted([(1, "b"), (1, "a")])` all equal python3 (the sort family orders tuples through `Comparer<ValueTuple>.Default`); `(1, 5) < (2, 3)`, `(1, 2) <= (1, 2)`, `((1, 2), 3) < ((1, 3), 0)` → SPY0222 where python prints `True`. **Ruling R-IP (a):** the operator family on tuple operands is a checker arm plus a lowering onto the one comparer engine, so an operator and a sort can never disagree (a user element `__lt__`, #2103, included); `System.ValueTuple` is not a Sharpy type (rung 2 unavailable — the dunder table's `__lt__` = `operator <`, which ValueTuple lacks), so the engine is the rung; python semantics kept (elementwise `==` then `<` at the first difference; prefix then length); an unorderable element pair is refused STATICALLY with SPY0222 naming the element position (python raises lazily — Axiom 2). Matrix: operator {4} × shape {flat, nested, mixed lengths, `str` element, user element with/without `__lt__`} × position {expression, `if`, sort key, `max`/`min` argument}. Rejected: (b) P15's `IComparable<T>` ⇒ `__lt__` rule (row 32); (c) a chore lowering to `CompareTo(...) < 0` (a second engine; `ArgumentException` where python says `TypeError`). Two-way.

### 3.7c P81 — Core sequence protocol (Core only; R-KS; **row 7b: directly after P34**)

**Tier:** light (Core-only with a measured seam; no D-number open after R-KS; the promoted surfaces have Core cures at rung 2 of the layer ladder). **Promoted in flight to full (c)** if cell 1 or 2 turns out to need a checker arm (SPY0220 / SPY0320 are checker refusals — the plan measures whether the Core overload alone flips them).

**Contract (#2436's, after R-KS):** `list`, `range` and `slice` are COMPARED surfaces for indexing, slicing and repr — a `slice` value indexes a list (`xs[slice(1, -1)]` → `[2, 3, 4]`), `range` is a sequence (`range(10)[-1]` → `9`, `range(10)[-3:]` → `range(7, 10)`, `len`), and `print(slice(0, 1))` → `slice(0, 1, None)`; a slice STORE (`ys[1:-1] = [7]`, SPY0225 today) stays a `docs/deviations.yaml` row until its own card. **Lane / reach:** false-refusal (cells 1–3: SPY0220 `Index must be 'int', got 'slice'`, SPY0320 `'RangeIterator' does not support indexing`) + silent-wrong (cell 5 prints `Sharpy.Slice`) · program; measured @ 5f8a4c4e2 and 694ab619d by the filer, none a regression. **Rung:** (2) Core C# in the dunder table's spelling — `List<T>.this[Slice]` beside the existing `GetSlice(Slice)`, a `Range` sequence type with `this[int]` / `this[Slice]` / `ISized` (today `RangeIterator : Iterator<int>, ISized`, no indexer), `Slice.ToString()`; rung 1 cannot (Core types), rungs 3–4 are not needed unless the checker's index-type check is name-keyed (then the plan says so and the tier promotes). **Matrix:** receiver {list, range, slice value} × operation {index, negative index, slice, repr, len} — one receiver axis × 4 ops, hand (no generator). **Spec:** `collection_types.md` ~605–640 (`slice` type, the slicing section's list/str/bytes/array list gains `range`); fidelity row 1 gains `slice`. **Standing harness:** `DifferentialExecution` (python twin) for the promoted cells. **Class tracker:** #2436.
- **#2436** (R-KS; the group) — false-refusal + silent-wrong · program. **Ruling R-KS (a):** promote cells 1, 2, 3, 5; record cell 4. Rejected: (b) promote all five (a slice store is a Semantic + CodeGen feature — an assignment-target arm, a `SetSlice` lowering, `del xs[a:b]` beside it — on its own card; P81 would be full (c)); (c) record all five (`range(n)[-1]` and a `slice` value as an index are ordinary programs with Core-only cures). Promotion is one-way per row; (a) then cell 4 later stacks.

### 3.7b P73 — stdlib math numerics accuracy (Stdlib only; R-IQ; generator FIRST; directly after P56, before P13b)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`).

**Contract (PROMOTED by R-IQ — a new COMPARED row in `python-fidelity-scope.md`):** every Stdlib `math` function returns python3's value within a STATED per-function ulp bound (libm's accuracy is CPython's), with special values (`±0.0`, `±inf`, `nan`, subnormals) python's; the bound and the special values are written in `docs/stdlib/math.md` per function. **Matrix:** function × argument band {tiny, the 1e-5…1e-2 band, unit, large, special values} × observation {ulp distance to python3, special-value identity}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Standing harness:** none — the plan builds the **differential numerics corpus FIRST** (python3 computes the expected values at run time; a per-function tolerance table; an allowlist ratchet that cites an issue per row and drains on fix; mutation = restore the naive `Math.Exp(x) - 1` branch → its rows red). **Class tracker:** #2349. **Blast radius:** Stdlib.Tests + `DifferentialExecution` (fixture `.expected` hits counted FIRST; own gate only if one moves). **Regen:** `Math/Math.cs` is GENERATED from `spy/math_module.spy` (`MODULES`) — edit the spy source, run the regen, the staleness check is the gate; `docs/stdlib/math.md` regenerated.
- **#2349** (R-IQ; the group) — silent-wrong · program; the filer @ 0c90a73 (python 3.13.16 as oracle): `math.expm1(2e-5)` 22,000 ulp off, `expm1(1e-4)` 3,200, `expm1(0.001)` 200, `log1p(1e-4)` 800, `log1p(0.001)` 500 — a 3-/4-term Taylor series below a tiny cutoff (`|x| < 1e-5` / `1e-4`), the naive formula above it (`Math.cs:243–270`, generated); .NET 10's `double.ExpM1`/`LogP1` return the same digits and Core multi-targets netstandard2.1 — no lower rung. **Ruling R-IQ (a):** PROMOTE; P73 = #2349 alone; #2350 stays P61's `math` ledger row (R-HL) and each function P61 lands gets a corpus row. Cure shape (the plan measures): port fdlibm `s_expm1.c` / `s_log1p.c` (Sun, MIT-compatible; musl carries the same code; Mojo 1.2's `_expm1_accurate` is the branch-free form) into the spy source; sibling cells the corpus sweeps once it exists: `tanh`/`sinh`/`cosh` small x, `hypot` over/underflow, `fsum`. Rejected: (b) P73 = {#2349, #2350} (departs from R-HL; two classes in one plan, §11); (c) RECORD as deviation rows. Promotion is additive; the printed values change once (count first).

### 3.9 P35 — culture invariance + datetime fidelity (Core + Stdlib; R-CL, R-DB)

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract:** every Core/Stdlib text path is culture-INVARIANT (COMPARED, R-CL: `str(dt) ≡ f"{dt}"`, matching CPython's C locale), and the datetime family's values and parsers are python's: `timedelta` components read the one normalized triple, python's full range is representable or refused with a python-named `OverflowError`, `strptime` parses by python's directive grammar and raises `ValueError`, `%Z` prints the abbreviation. **Matrix:** text path {`str`, `repr`, f-string hole, `format`, `strftime`, `strptime`, number formatting} × culture {invariant, a host culture with different separators} × type {DateTime, decimal, float, timedelta}; datetime cells per issue. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Fidelity-scope row:** culture/locale/platform text (COMPARED, R-CL). **Standing harness:** none for culture — the plan adds a source scan over Core/Stdlib for culture-sensitive calls (`ToString()` on IFormattable, `Parse`, `string.Format` without a provider) with a positive control, plus a test host running under a non-invariant culture. **Generator:** the scan is the generator; datetime cells are hand rows. **Class tracker:** #2056.
- **#2056** (R-CL; FIRST) — silent-wrong · program; `Builtins.Str` of a CLR `IFormattable` is current-culture text while the plain hole is invariant after R-CC. **Ruling R-CL (D4 b, 2026-09-27):** COMPARED — invariant everywhere, one rule, no per-call opt-out; `str(dt)` moves to the invariant spelling (direction record).
- **#2080** — silent-wrong · program; `timedelta(hours=-1).days/.seconds` → `0 -3600` (python `-1 82800`); the accessors share the normalized triple #2043's `ToString` computes.
- **#2109** — runtime-throw · program; `timedelta(days=999999999)` throws `System.OverflowException` (python's max); a wider representation, or a python-named `OverflowError` + deviation row — the plan measures `TimeSpan`'s range against python's before choosing.
- **#2133** — runtime-throw · program; `strptime` translates to `ParseExact` (`%j`, `%z`, non-padded fields, lowercase `%b`/`%p` fail) and a mismatch escapes as an uncatchable `FormatException`; parse by python's directive grammar (the `Strftime.cs` twin), raise `ValueError` with python's text.
- **#2134** (R-DB) — silent-wrong · program; `time.strftime("%Z")` prints `Eastern Daylight Time` (python `EDT`). **Ruling R-DB (option a):** libc `strftime`/`tzname` via interop on Unix; the display name + a deviation row on Windows. Rejected: a deviation row everywhere (strftime is COMPARED under R-CL).

### 3.9b P56 — `Popen` process-state fidelity (Stdlib `Subprocess/` only; R-FL; python-twin table; directly after P35)

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract (#2266's, widened by the batching read):** after every `Popen` operation {`communicate(timeout=)` timing out, `wait(timeout=)` timing out, `kill()`, `terminate()`, `send_signal(n)`, normal exit, `run`/`check_output` timeout kill}, `poll()` / `returncode` / a further `communicate()` give python's values, and the child receives the signal python sends. **Measured @ 90f7ff9ff on the issue:** `communicate(timeout=0.1)` KILLS the child before raising `TimeoutExpired` (python leaves it running; a second `communicate()` then returns and `returncode` is `0`); a SIGKILLed child's `returncode` is `137` (python `-9`). **Read @ a08d35fcf (`Subprocess/Popen.cs`):** `Returncode` is `Process.ExitCode` (128+N for a signalled child — .NET cannot distinguish a signal exit from `exit 137`); `Terminate()` and `SendSignal(n)` both call `Kill()` (SIGKILL; the managed API has no SIGTERM), so two more cells: `terminate()` → python `-15` and the child's SIGTERM handler runs; `send_signal(n)` → `-n`. python3 3.12.13 at batching: terminate `-15`, kill `-9`, `sh -c 'exit 137'` → `137`. **Matrix:** operation (7 above) × observation {`poll()` right after, `returncode` after `wait()`, a further `communicate()`, the child's own observation (a `sh -c 'trap … TERM'` twin)} × pipe state {no pipe, stdout pipe}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Standing harness:** `StdlibTimeoutMatrixTests` (#2263) covers the timeout VALUE class only and kills/reaps every child after the cell. **Generator:** the #2263 shape — one program per cell, a committed python twin, its output as the expected file (python is the generator; contract §11's differential form). **Class tracker:** #2266.
- **#2266** (R-FL; the group) — silent-wrong · program. **Ruling R-FL (option a):** Sharpy records the signal it sent — `kill()` → `-9`, `terminate()` → `-15`, `send_signal(n)` → `-n`, the timeout kill inside `run`/`check_output` → `-9` — and sends the REAL signal on Unix through a libc `kill(2)` P/Invoke (the first P/Invoke in Stdlib — none today, grep-only @ a08d35fcf; `netstandard2.1`-safe `DllImport`; Windows keeps `Process.Kill` for all three and reports python's Windows values); a child signalled by someone else keeps .NET's `128+N` as ONE `docs/deviations.yaml` row `external-signal-returncode` (the .NET exit code cannot name the signal — Axiom 1). `communicate(timeout=)` leaves the child running (COMPARED; no ruling needed). Rejected: (b) map every exit code 129–192 to `-(code−128)` (a genuine `exit 137` misreports; python prints `137`); (c) a deviation row for every signal returncode, SIGKILL-only `terminate` (a child trapping SIGTERM never runs its cleanup — a behaviour, not a message). (a) changes printed values once (one-way for output); (c) → (a) would have been additive, (a) → (c) is not. Spec: the subprocess stdlib doc (generated) + the ledger row.

P51's #2246 lands AFTER this so the guard the narrowing engine reads is the one that ran.

- **#2418** (call 36) — false-refusal · program; `run` @ df3eed258 (the issue; same at the 0.22.1 proxy): `@dataclass class D: a: int / @static s1: int = 9 / b: int = 3`; `match D(1, 2): case D(x, y): print(x, y)` → SPY0369 + SPY0363 "type 'D' has 3 fields" + SPY0200 (python3 `1 2` — `__match_args__` excludes `ClassVar`s); a struct with a `const` and a `@static` → "has 5 fields". **Contract (Rule 11):** a positional class pattern binds the type's INSTANCE fields — the ones its constructor takes — on every host; the constructor call `D(1, 2)` already counts only those, so the pattern side reads the SAME field list (one fact, two consumers). Cells: host {class, `@dataclass`, struct} × non-instance sibling {`@static` field, `const`, `@static` property, `@static` method (control)} × pattern arity {exact, short, long (SPY0363 control)}; joins `PatternConformanceMatrixTests`' head axis. No seam hypothesis.

### 3.11 P52 — declaration-order independence (Semantic binder; N1's type-member half; **R-KH: moved to row 6c⁶⁸ — directly after P80, before P51**; generator EXISTS — P77a's harness gains the declaring-type-order × member × position axes)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`; (b) lapsed at call 36 — the generator EXISTS since P77a).

**Call 36 (R-KH a):** this group is rule N1 (`docs/design/name-resolution.md`) applied at the binder's type-member pass — "declaration order never changes what a name means" — so it lands directly behind the block that writes N1's spec text, with its matrix generated by P77a (binding kind gains "a later-declared type's member", position gains the six below) instead of a second generator; P51 still follows it. Rejected: stay at row 11 (N1's spec sentence would carve out its own type-member half).

**Contract:** a member of a module-level type declared LATER in the module types identically to one declared earlier, in every position — declaration order never produces an Unknown. **Matrix:** declaring kind {class, struct, union, dataclass, interface, enum} × member {method, property, field, static method, constructor} × position {annotated store, match scrutinee, argument, condition, return, chained access} × order {declared before, declared after, in a later-listed project file}. **Lane / reach:** ice · program. **Standing harness:** none — the generator emits each cell twice (type before / type after its user) and asserts identical diagnostics and output, with an allowlist ratchet; `deliberately-permissive-allowlist.txt` is the related Unknown census (R-AZ). No seam hypothesis. Changes verdicts (SPY0908 → the ordinary SPY0220/SPY0203), not emitted layout.
- **#2249** (FIRST; tracker) — ice · program; measured @ 1e5d54c8f (`probes/m2249/`): `match Later().get(): case Some(v)` with `class Later` declared later is SPY0202 "Unknown type 'Some'" through a field receiver, a direct call, a match expression, and after binding to an UNTYPED local; `t: str = Later().get_int()` is SPY0908 CS0029 (untyped, not mistyped — the member call types Unknown); `x: bool? = Later().get()` then `match x` runs; a later FREE FUNCTION `get()` runs; `case 1:` and `is not None` on the same scrutinee run because they never read its type; the earlier-declared control runs. Reported from the HGSS demo (vibe-pokemon-sharpy C42).
- **Call 38 cell on #2249 (P77a's verify round @ fd925ddca):** a `T !E` annotation whose error type `E` is declared LATER — SPY0908 CS0029 with `E` below, SPY0260 with `E` above; the member axis gains the error-type position of a `T !E` annotation (declared before / after); the 3 name-resolution allowlist rows citing #2249 drain here.

### 3.12 P51 — narrowing authority (Semantic + CodeGen; R-EU; generator FIRST; after P52)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** ONE narrowing fact per condition position, read by the checker's member lookup, the store/argument checks AND the emitter — a value narrowed at a position has the narrowed type for every consumer, and a position that narrows for `if` narrows at every position the spec lists. **Matrix:** declared type {`T?` strict, `T | None` loose, union `A | B`, oblivious CLR reference} × position {if, elif, ternary, while, `and`/`or`, early-return, walrus, match arm guard (R-EU)} × member {wrapper-only (`unwrap`, `unwrap_or`, `is_some`), underlying-only (`upper`), on both} × consumer {checker verdict, emitted spelling}. **Lane / reach:** ice · program. **Standing harness:** none — generator FIRST (differential: each cell's checker verdict vs the compiled C#, allowlist ratchet). Acceptance by construction: the emitted C# of the `if`-narrowed `.upper()` twin does not move. No seam hypothesis. If the narrowing fact becomes a new node-keyed dictionary it joins `MergeFrom`.
- **#2248** (FIRST; tracker) — ice · program; measured @ 1e5d54c8f (`probes/m2248/`): `x.unwrap()` after `if x is not None` is SPY0908 CS1929 (`x.Unwrap().Unwrap()`) on `str?` AND on `str | None`, in `if` and in a ternary; `x.upper()` on the narrowed value runs; un-narrowed `x.unwrap()` runs; inside `case Some(v):` the subject is not narrowed and `x.unwrap()` runs. Recorded ≠ applied: the emitter applies the narrowing fact, the checker's member lookup reads the declared wrapper. Reported from the HGSS demo (C41).
- **#2246** (R-EU) — false-refusal · program; `case 2 if s is not None: t: int = s` is SPY0220 while the `if` twin narrows (#2237 chore @ b4d268dc1). **Ruling R-EU (a):** an arm guard narrows the arm body; a per-alternative guard never narrows (one rule: a guard that gates the WHOLE arm narrows; C#'s flow analysis honours `when x is not null` the same way). Lands AFTER P13b's #2240. `match_statement.md` gains the sentence and an executed example. Rejected: (a′) the per-alternative guard narrows when it is the sole alternative; (b) keep as is + spec sentence.

### 3.12b P57 — a no-return callee is a terminator (Semantic + one emitter arm; R-FM; generator FIRST; directly after P51)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (#2269's):** a callee that never returns is a terminator for every flow analysis — definite return (SPY0266), definite assignment (SPY0600), unreachable-statement warnings, `match` exhaustiveness, `try`/`finally` control-transfer checks — from ONE predicate with two sources: the CLR `[DoesNotReturn]` attribute discovered by reflection (CLR callees and Sharpy defs carrying the bracket attribute) and the Sharpy return-type spelling R-FM adds. **Measured @ 88345108f (issue):** `@[system.diagnostics.code_analysis.does_not_return] def panic(msg: str) -> None: raise …` is EMITTED with `[DoesNotReturn]` and runs, but a branch ending in `panic("missing")` is refused SPY0266 while the inline `raise` twin prints `3`. **Matrix:** fact source {Sharpy def `-> NoReturn`, Sharpy def with the bracket attribute only, CLR method carrying `[DoesNotReturn]` (`Environment.FailFast`), a def whose body is a bare `raise` with no spelling (NOT inferred — control), lambda/delegate (ignored)} × consumer {SPY0266, SPY0600, unreachable warning, `match` exhaustiveness, SPY0702 finally, `async def` caller} × call position {last statement of a branch, `else`, `match` arm, `with` body, loop body (does NOT terminate the function — control), expression statement vs operand `x = panic(…)`}. **Lane / reach:** false-refusal · program. **Standing harness:** none; P51's narrowing generator enumerates the position axis P57 reuses. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** (3 axes × ≥3 values) — differential: each (source × position) cell against its inline-`raise` twin, compiled and run, verdict and output equal; allowlist ratchet. Roslyn does NOT use `[DoesNotReturn]` for reachability, so an accepted program needs an emitter-materialized unreachable terminator after the call (node-keyed `SemanticInfo`, Rule 2 pattern b → `MergeFrom`) or CS0161 surfaces as SPY0908 — the generator's `run` cells catch that. **Class tracker:** #2269.
- **#2269** (R-FM; the group) — false-refusal · program. **Ruling R-FM (option a; owner's comment on the issue proposed `Never`, "the attribute seems a bit hefty"):** `-> NoReturn` — PEP 484's return-annotation spelling (`typing.NoReturn`) — is the Sharpy spelling; it lowers to `void` + `[System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.DoesNotReturn]`, and the attribute is CONSUMED on every callee, CLR or Sharpy, so the predicate has one definition and two sources; `Never` is not bound (SPY0202 with the steer "use `NoReturn`"), so no user-owned name is shadowed. A body that only raises is NOT inferred to be no-return (explicit over magic). Rejected: (b) `-> Never` alone (the 3.11 alias; the owner's shadowing concern); (c) both names (two spellings of one fact); (d) attribute only, as filed. Adding a second spelling later is additive (two-way). Spec: `function_definition.md`/`return_statement.md` gain the rule with an executed example; `decorators.md`'s bracket-attribute table lists `does_not_return` as compiler-consumed; `type_annotation_shorthand.md` the `NoReturn` row; python3 accepts the same program (`from typing import NoReturn` is implied — the plan decides whether the import line is required, accepted or refused, with python-fidelity on "which programs run" as the oracle).

### 3.12c P60 — function-value route parity (Semantic + CodeGen; R-FW; generator FIRST; directly after P57)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (#2277's):** a function used as a value either runs with python's result or is refused semantically with a named code — SPY0908 is never the verdict — and the value's TYPE is ONE recorded fact (the full signature, defaults erased, no `*args` tail, type arguments closed) that the checker's through-value call check and the emitter's delegate spelling both read, so the inferred local `f = g` reaches every verdict the annotated `f: (…) -> T = g` reaches. **Measured @ be1c16fcf (built binary, batching probes `probes/{g1,g2,d1,d2,d3,d4,p1}.spy`):** `f = first[int]` → SPY0335 (the explicit spelling is already refused; bare `f = first` reaches Roslyn CS0246 — the issue's cell); `m: (int, int) -> int = h` → warning SPY0486 and `function_types.md` §Default Parameter Erasure rules `k(1)` through the value SPY0277, while the inferred `k = h; k(1)` → SPY0908 CS7036 and `k(1, 5)` prints `6`; `m: (int) -> int = h` and `g: (list[int]) -> int = first` → SPY0220 (no arity adaptation, no inference from the target — today's behaviour, unchanged here); `f = s` for `def s(*xs: int)` → SPY0908 CS0123 (`Func<int, int>` — the tail spelled as its element type). The issue's stdlib cells (`statistics.mode`, `difflib.ndiff`, `struct.pack`, `csv.writer`, the `operator` page's SPY0599) are the same shapes. **Matrix:** signature shape {plain (control), generic, iterator-returning, defaulted, `*args` tail, CLR-typed parameter, overload set (SPY0336 control)} × callee kind {user def, spy-sourced stdlib, hand-written C# stdlib, CLR static} × position {local without annotation, `key=`, `map` argument, annotated local}. **Lane / reach:** ice · program. **Standing harness:** `StdlibDocFieldNameBindingTests.EveryDocumentedModuleFunction_BindsAMemberOfItsModule` prints `DOC-FN-EMIT` rows (29 @ 729bf1e7d) as `Output.WriteLine` ONLY — not a ratchet; the plan's first task makes it an asserted allowlist citing #2277 (drain on fix) before any cure. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** (3 axes × ≥3 values) — enumerate shape × callee kind × position, compile and run each cell against its direct-call twin (python3 output where the spec defers; the spec's SPY0277/SPY0486/SPY0335 verdicts where it rules), allowlist ratchet. The emit-family cells (the iterator return spelled from the Sharpy type; the unqualified `TextWriter`) join the #1683/#1802 emit-family completeness roster. By design NO snapshot moves (plain-signature C# is the control). **Class tracker:** #2277. Family note: a call-route residue beside P17b/c/d (a second callee speller in the emitter), scheduled here rather than as P17e because every refusal cell lands on an existing code (SPY0335, SPY0486, SPY0277) or R-FW's new one, so P17's sentinel adds nothing (R-GC).
- **#2277** (R-FW; the group) — ice · program. **Ruling R-FW (option a):** a function whose signature has a `*args` tail is not a value without a lambda — refused with a code in the SPY0335 family (the band is full; the next free in its overflow, plus a `DiagnosticExplanations` entry) and the steer "wrap it: `lambda a, b, c: s(a, b, c)`"; the same footing as R-BR (function types carry no names, defaults or tails; calls through a value are positional-only). Direction: SPY0908 → the new code (same program refused on a better channel). Rejected: (b) type the tail as one sequence parameter `(list[int]) -> int` (C# normal form; `f([1, 2, 3])` runs where python refuses and `f(1, 2, 3)` is refused — magic, one-way once written); (c) a `*T` spelling in function types with a per-arity variadic delegate family (a language feature; (a) → (c) later is additive). The generic and defaulted shapes need no ruling (SPY0335; SPY0486 + SPY0277 per the spec).

- **Call 22 cell on #2277 (2026-10-14; P53 prober @ f33b528f9, identical @ 8be4576fd):** the `from … import … as` ALIAS used as a value — `from lib import build as b; f = b; print(f("x").label)` → SPY0908 ×2 (CS0246 `Widget` not found, CS0407 wrong return type: the delegate spelled from the Sharpy signature names the return type unqualified through the alias) while `f = build` and `b("x")` run — the recurrence shape of closed #1588; *aliased from-import* joins the position axis.

- **#2419** (call 36) — ice · program; `run` @ df3eed258 (the issue): `def g() -> int: return 6`; in `main` `f = True; print((g if f else g)())` → SPY0524 "emitted C# tree inverts operator precedence -- unparenthesized ConditionalExpression operand of InvocationExpression (Receiver slot)" (python3 `6`); the same inside a static initializer (`(Host.sm if True else Host.sm)()`, @ 6d1c9021f too). **Contract (this group's):** a function VALUE produced by any expression form and called either runs with python's result or is refused semantically — the emitter parenthesizes every operand whose precedence is lower than its slot (SPY0524 is the internal guard that turned this silent inversion into an ICE). The generator's callee axis gains the EXPRESSION FORM {name (control), member, conditional, lambda, `a or b`, walrus, call result, index} × position {statement, argument, static initializer}; other low-precedence callee forms are probed by the generator, not hand-listed.
- **#2437** (call 38; R-KW **b**) — ice · program; `run` @ the issue's sha (P77a's verify round): `g: () -> None = lambda: 1` / `g()` → SPY0908 CS0201 (python3 prints nothing). **Ruling R-KW (owner, 2026-11-05, D9): (b) REFUSE** — SPY0220 "lambda body type 'int' is not assignable to expected return type 'None'" (the code the `-> int` twin draws for a `str` body); owner: "the lambda's body in Python is the return value and having just `1` would mean int, not None." The shape axis gains slot `() -> None` × body {literal, name, a call returning `T` (refused), a call returning `None` (control — runs), an expression-statement shape}. Direction: SPY0908 → SPY0220 (two-way; (b) → (a) is a relaxation). Rejected (the analysis's rec): (a) admit — type the lambda from the slot, evaluate the body for side effects and discard it as an expression statement in a `-> None` def is, block-body emission when the expression is not a C# statement expression.

**Contract (#2260's):** for every stdlib callable with a CPython twin, the call shapes {omitted, explicit `None`, value} give CPython's verdict — the same value or the same refusal — or the divergence is a `docs/deviations.yaml` row the audit cites (R-FA). **Matrix:** callable (every public Stdlib function / constructor / method with a defaulted parameter) × call shape {omitted, explicit `None`, in-domain value, the stand-in value itself} × source {spy-sourced (the 18 `MODULES` entries), hand-written C#} × CPython default kind {`None`, value, sentinel object, required, uninspectable}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · probe. **Standing harness:** `build_tools/spy_none_default_audit.py` + `spy_none_default_allowlist.txt` (#2236, 3e18a9b43) — its universe today is the spy-sourced `= None` defaults only. **Generator: REQUIRED, FIRST task** — widen the audit to every default and to the hand-written C# modules, with a hand table for the callables `inspect.signature` cannot read and a "recorded" verdict that cites a deviation-row id (today every allowlist row must cite an issue), red first; its first run IS the cell table; a candidate becomes a finding only after a RUN against python3 (a text diff is not enough — see `bisect` below). No seam hypothesis — the plan measures.
- **#2260** (R-FA, R-FB; FIRST; tracker) — measured @ 5942b6df8 (`probes/rep endpos hm hm2 standin`; python3 3.12.13 beside each): `itertools.repeat(1, -2)` and `repeat(1, -1)` loop forever (python `[]`); `p.search("xxa", 0, None)` searches to the end (python `TypeError`); `hmac.new(b"k")` and `hmac.new(b"k", b"m")` use sha256 (python `TypeError: Missing required argument 'digestmod'`), an explicit `"md5"` / `digestmod="sha1"` gives python's hex digest, and `digestmod=""` already raises `TypeError`. Posted cells: `p.search("xxa", 0, -5)` → unhandled `System.ArgumentOutOfRangeException` (python `None`); `itertools.permutations([1, 2], -1)` yields 2 permutations (python `ValueError: r must be non-negative`). **Correction to the issue:** `hmac` is not in `MODULES`; the default is in hand-written `Hmac/HmacModule.cs`, so two of the four cell families (`hmac`, `http.client`) are outside the spy source. `http.client`'s cell is code-read by the filer, NOT measured — the plan measures it. **Scratch count @ 5942b6df8** (every default instead of `= None`; not the audit): 102 defaulted parameters on public defs in the 18 spy-sourced modules, 10 of them `= None`; of the 81 other distinct cells 38 equal CPython's default, ~21 are candidates (15 where CPython's default is `None` and Sharpy has a value, 6 textual differences — string contents are masked by the parser, so unverified) and 22 are not mechanically comparable (8 uninspectable C callables, `repeat` among them); hand-written modules uncounted. `bisect.bisect_left([1, 2, 3], 5, 0, -1)` and `bisect_right` print `3` in both (CPython's C implementation uses `-1` as its own stand-in), so the six `bisect` `hi=-1` candidates are NOT findings. **Constraint:** Sharpy's `int` is 32-bit, so CPython's `sys.maxsize` literal is not spellable. **Ruling R-FA ("c — split"):** `endpos: int | None = None` ×5 stays and is ONE deviation row (the stdlib `T | None = None` convention; no valid CPython program changes meaning) — the five allowlist rows drain to the row's "recorded" verdict; `hmac.new` without `digestmod` is refused (lean: a compile-time refusal rather than a `""` default that leaves the check to runtime; the plan picks the spelling). Rejected: (a) record both; (b) promote both. **Ruling R-FB ("a"):** own group, widened audit first. Rejected: (b) chores for the named cells with the audit unchanged; (c) chores now, P55 later.

### 3.15 P49 — test-host marker-call parity (Semantic + one emitter arm; R-EI, R-EO; generator FIRST; after P38)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (#2174's):** a `unittest.assert_*` marker call lowers to the same semantics as the plain `assert` spelling of the same condition, from facts Semantic records for the call — never from the argument's syntactic shape or the callee's name in the emitter. **Matrix:** marker {`assert_true`, `assert_false`, `assert_is_none`, `assert_is_not_none`, `assert_greater`, `assert_less`, `assert_in`, `assert_not_in`; `assert_almost_equal`, `assert_raises`, `assert_regex`, `assert_count_equal` as controls} × operand kind {bool, non-bool truthy type, reference, `T | None`, `Optional` struct, CLR nullable value, list, tuple, dict, set, str, user `__contains__`/`__gt__`/`__bool__`} × direction {assert passes, assert fails}. **Lane / reach:** ice · program. **Standing harness:** `TestHostNoneAssertMatrixTests` and the R-EE 43-cell membership matrix (plain `assert` only). **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — differential: each marker call against its plain-`assert` twin (`assert_in(x, c)` ≡ `assert x in c`, `assert_true(v)` ≡ `assert v`, `assert_is_none(v)` ≡ `assert v is None`, `assert_greater(a, b)` ≡ `assert a > b`), executed under the test host in both directions, allowlist ratchet. **Class tracker:** #2174.
- **#2174** (R-EI, R-EO; FIRST) — ice · program; the marker cell: `unittest.assert_in` / `assert_not_in` on a tuple is SPY0908 CS1503 and on a dict CS0121 (chore agent @ ddb33a247). The emitter lowers all eight markers by name from the AST (`RoslynEmitter.Statements.cs:682–716`, read @ 21ca52b5d); only the two membership markers are measured. The `assert x in c` half landed @ ddb33a247 + a965bce55 (R-EE: `Assert.True(<ordinary membership lowering>)`, `#pragma warning disable xUnit2009, xUnit2017` in emitted test files). **Ruling R-EO (a):** the group covers the whole `assert_*` family, not the two markers R-EI names. Rejected: (b) only `assert_in` / `assert_not_in`, riding P37.

These are syntax facts on syntax nodes (Rule 3 is not in play). Row 6 drained with P40. **Cell 2026-10-15 (P63's roster RAW control; tooling · probe):** a conditional in the TEST position of a conditional unparses without parentheses when the AST has no `Parenthesized` node (synthesized ASTs only) → SPY0104 — **a chore under R-HP, directly after P63's close-out** (§3.0); it deletes the `KnownGeneratorPins` #2169 pin.
