### 3.19 P21d — binding-law residue (P21 residue; R-EA; **R-GV: AFTER P21c**; members #2150 #2153 #2151 #2144 #2155 #2125 moved to §3.22 — their bullets stay below because they define the matrix the bindings harness extends)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**R-GV (2026-10-14):** the owner's B1–B12 scope list moves #2150 (B2), #2153 (B3), #2155 (B10), #2151 (B9), #2125 (B9), #2144 (B5/B7) into the bindings plan (§3.22); this group keeps **#2154 #2156 #2152 #2206** and lands AFTER P21c (R-CN's premise — the codemod reads name-keyed `TargetBinding` facts that #2150/#2153 expose — is now internal to the bindings plan). Its generator is the bindings harness P21b/P21c leave behind. #2151's call cell (a type-argued call to a module function from a method body is CS0103 too — `ident[int]()`, aliased, from-imported, inside a deferred cycle; @ c245ece62 / 202a78e72 / 8be4576fd) goes with #2151: use-site kind {store, call, generic call} on its host matrix.

**Contract:** every use site (read, call, store, `out`, accessor/implicit parameter) and every validator (definite assignment, unused-binding, `@suppress`) resolves a name to the BINDING the checker bound — symbol-keyed, never by name — and every unpacking path checks values under the target slot. **Matrix:** use-site kind {read, call, call with defaults/keywords, plain store, augmented, tuple, star leaf, `out`} × local binder {parameter, annotated, bare-declaring, `let`, for target, with-as, except-as, walrus, implicit setter `value`/`self`, bare `_` discard (R-EA)} × shadowed binding {module variable, module const, def, from-imported name, implicit parameter} × host {function, block, method, static method, property setter, `__setitem__`, lambda} × slot type {`list[float]`, `list[object]`, `list[T?]`, `str`} × `_` target shape {plain, tuple leaf, star leaf, for target, with-as}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Standing harness:** `BlockScopeRedeclarationMatrixTests` (binding-law harness, P21a) — plants only `int` predecessors and only READ sites. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — extend that harness to enumerate use-site × binder × predecessor × slot type, EXECUTED with asserted values, allowlist ratchet. **Class tracker:** #2150. **Order inside the group:** #2150 and #2153 first (silent-wrong), then the ICEs, then the validators. No snapshot moves by design.
- **#2150** (FIRST) — silent-wrong · program; `f(1, 2)` inside `def call(f: (int, int) -> int)` calls the MODULE `f` (prints `-1`, expected `3`) for parameter / annotated / `for` / `let` binders; the emitted C# declares the local and calls `Module.F`.
- **#2153** — silent-wrong · program; `AccessorParamRewrite(<name>, "value")` and the `self` → `this` spelling are keyed on the NAME in CodeGen, so a `let value`/annotated `value`/`self` shadow reads or writes the parameter. Cure shape: the implicit-parameter fact is symbol-keyed on `CodeGenInfo` (Rule 2). Closed ancestors #1500 #1458 #1529.
- **#2151** — ice · program; a bare store to a module variable from a METHOD body emits the unqualified name (CS0103); the from-imported axis (`from math import pi; pi = 3.0` → CS0103, cross-file → CS0305) added by the prober.
- **#2154** — ice · program; star-path middle values are checked slot-free (`a, *rest = 1, 2, 3` into `rest: list[float]` builds `List<int>` → CS0029; was silent-wrong before P21a's write-through). The harness gains the slot-type axis; P37's generator carries this cell as an allowlisted row this group drains.
- **#2144** — false-refusal · program; SPY0600 misses `out` arguments and comprehension-filter walrus as binding sites, and blames a FRESH annotated/`let`/`out let` binding for its declared-but-unassigned predecessor (prober axis). Drains the `parameter_modifiers.md`/`variable_scoping.md` allowlist rows citing it.
- **#2155** — missing-refusal · program; SPY0451 is decided per NAME: a dead `let`/annotated shadow or a dead shadowed outer binding gets no warning (positive control `x = 1; print(2)` warns).
- **#2152** — false-refusal · program; `@suppress(SPY0451)` on a bare local suppresses nothing, on an annotated local is refused as a "module-level" declaration (SPY0322), `let` likewise. Call-29 cell (the P21e ledger, measured by the P21e verifier @ 8fc618b22): `@suppress` on a `type` alias statement is SPY0103 (the parser admits no decorator before `type`) — the host-kind axis gains `type` alias beside bare/annotated/`let` locals and nested defs; reach probe.
- **#2156** — false-refusal · program; `(c, d) = 3, 4` / `[c, d] = 3, 4` / `let (c, d) = 3, 4` are SPY0103 (the parser decides "tuple RHS" from the target's shape); python binds.

Rejected: python rebind (dies under const-only module scope anyway). Owner note: the issue's example is the pre-21c spelling; the ruling covers both spellings.
- **#2206** (R-EA) — false-refusal · program; measured @ 6b208066e: `_ = flag(); _ = items()` is SPY0220; `` _ = flag(); x = `_` `` prints `True`. **Ruling R-EA:** a bare `_` store target is a discard — no binding, any type each time, emitted as C# `_ = expr;`; only the escaped `` `_` `` is an identifier (its `__spy_underscore` spelling stands). Reverses plan-be89c1 D1 (a) for the BARE spelling only: `` a, _ = 1, 2; x = `_` `` stops compiling — direction record. Rejected: (b) keep the typed local and change SPY0480's steer to `let _ = …` (after P21c a bare `_ = f()` would be SPY0611).

### 3.20f P74 — a lambda argument is typed by the overload the call resolves to (Semantic — the call-site overload pass's contextual typing of a lambda argument; R-JP, R-JQ; **Gen REQUIRED FIRST**; **row 6c⁷: directly after P22c, before P69**)

**Tier:** full (a: a decision was open at batching (D1 → R-JP; the plan writes the rule into the spec); b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`).

**Contract (R-JP; `overload_resolution.md` §"Argument types are computed before candidates are considered" extended to lambdas, `lambdas.md` §Type Inference Requirements):** an unannotated lambda argument is typed from the overload the call resolves to, order-independently, or refused once — arity and the non-lambda arguments narrow the candidate set FIRST; if the survivors agree on the lambda's parameter types the lambda is typed from them and its inferred body type joins applicability (so overloads differing by the lambda's RETURN type resolve); if the survivors disagree the call is refused with ONE diagnostic (SPY0343 reused — "unannotated parameter types could not be inferred" — with the steer "annotate the lambda's parameters: `lambda n: int: …`"); the body never chooses an overload, and no cell's verdict depends on declaration order. A lambda under a single candidate, an annotated lambda, and a collection literal under the same overload set are the controls. **Matrix:** receiver {type name, `type` alias, instance, module function, CLR overload set} × distinguishing position {lambda parameter type, lambda return type, arity, a non-lambda argument} × lambda slot {on every overload, on a later overload only} × lambda body {uses a member of the parameter, operator only, opaque (`str(n)`)} × declaration order {forward, reversed}; positive controls: annotated lambda, single candidate, the `[]` twin. **Lane / reach:** ice · program (the later-slot cell), false-refusal · program (the narrowed cells, the return-type cell), order-dependent verdict · program (the parameter-type-only cells — `run` vs refused by declaration order). **Fidelity-scope row:** "which programs run vs are refused" — COMPARED against the spec; python3 has no overloads, so the spec is the oracle and the plan writes the sentence (R-JP) before measuring. **Standing harness / generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — `StaticOverloadReceiverMatrixTests` (the #2391 chore, 23 hand cells over receiver × static-ness × overload count × {plain, lambda}) becomes the warm control of a GENERATED theory over the five axes with an allowlist ratchet; the ratchet's first rows are the cells below; `ErrorInjector`/`TypedGeneratorRosterTests` gain a lambda-under-overload-set lane only if the roster has no row for it (check first). **Spec (R-BG: drain each file's allowlist rows first; R-FY: convert output-stating blocks):** `overload_resolution.md` gains the lambda paragraph beside the collection-literal rule (narrow first; survivors must agree; one refusal) with an executed example and an `error SPY0343` block; `lambdas.md` §Type Inference Requirements gains the overload-set row in both tables; `method_overloading.md` one sentence pointing there. `DiagnosticExplanations`: SPY0343's entry gains the overload-set sentence (no new code by R-JP unless the plan measures that SPY0343's identity cannot carry the steer — recorded in the commit). **Direction records (against the plan's base):** `C.apply("ab", lambda s: len(s))` SPY0354 → prints `2`; the later-slot `n.zfill(3)` cell SPY0908 CS1929 → prints `005`; the return-type cell SPY0220 → prints the `str` overload's result; the parameter-type-only `n + 1` cell `2` (int-first) / SPY0222 (str-first) → SPY0343 in BOTH orders; the `len(s.upper())` cell `3` → SPY0343 (the one running program R-JP refuses — C# accepts it; relaxable under R-JP's rejected (a)). **Snapshots:** none by design (verdicts and lambda parameter types; the emitted delegate types do not move for the cells that run today). **Blast radius:** Semantic → the whole gate (Compiler.Tests, Stdlib.Tests, Cli.Tests, `FrontEndParity`, the three sweeps); the `ErrorCompletenessPropertyTests` lanes that inject lambdas read the new verdict. **Class tracker:** #2394 (the cell table is on the issue).

### 3.20k P77 — N3: values ready before use (CodeGen value emission + Semantic cycle detection; R-KD, R-KI, R-KJ; **lands ALONE**; generator EXISTS (P77a); **row 6c⁶⁗: directly after P77a**)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `CodeGen/` and `Semantic/`, and the group lands alone — every module and `@static` value's emission moves).

**Contract (N3, #2422):** every non-constant module-level value and every class `@static` value is initialized BEFORE its first read on every path — bare, qualified, aliased, through a call, inside a lambda or generator, from another type or module; at load each module runs its initializers top to bottom, after the modules it imports and before `main` (Python's side-effect order for a Python-valid program); a forward read initializes its target first (Swift's lazy globals); an initialization CYCLE is a compile error where visible (SPY0278 generalized — every receiver spelling, cross-type) and a run-time circular-initialization error otherwise — NEVER a CLR default; a throwing initializer leaves the value uninitialized (the next read retries); literal consts stay C# `const`. **Emission (the design of record, measured on #2422 — M4 Max, .NET 10.0.14, BDN 0.15.8):** per value a backing field + an init-state byte + an accessor `get { if (!Swept.Done && Volatile.Read(ref s_state) != Ready) Init_x(); return s_x; }`, one `static class Swept { internal static readonly bool Done = RunSweep(); }` per module host (per class with `@static` values; one per closed generic type), the entry point touches each module's `Swept.Done` in import order before `main`; the folded flag runs at 0.99–1.01× the unguarded read (default JIT, PGO=0, TC=0); rejected shapes: a naive per-read check (1.47–1.77×, 2.0–2.5× without PGO), `static readonly` + cctor and holder classes (V0 speed but no cycle detection, no retry — a throwing cctor poisons the type), `Lazy<T>` (up to 3.5×). **Residue to state in the spec:** code JIT-compiled before the sweep and never re-JITted keeps the check (TC=0, R2R before tier-up, probably NativeAOT — unmeasured); a failed sweep leaves the slow path on for the process; generic-class statics initialize per closed type on first use. **Interop note:** a module var becomes a C# property on the module class (one-way for C# consumers that took its address — the plan counts them: none in the repo). **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Fidelity-scope row:** value of an expression — COMPARED (cells E01 `1 7` → `8 7`; E03/A07; A23 `1` → `5`; D02 `0 7` → `7 7`; D01 `0 0 5` → `5 5 5`; C49 `0` → `4`; A11 `main / init a / init b` → `init a / init b / main`; L20; the #2413 lambda/IIFE/constructor/cross-type/`@lru_cache` cells; the #2416 derived-receiver/parenthesized-callee/later-overload cells — all measured @ 3ef53c6ec or df3eed258 by their filers). **Standing harness / generator: EXISTS (P77a)** — its #2422 rows drain; `StaticInitializerOrderMatrixTests` (314 cells) is the warm control and flips by direction (forward cells SPY0618 → the initialized value; the 3 #2412 rows drain). **Retires:** `StaticInitializerOrderValidator` (Order 251) and SPY0618 (retirement note in `DiagnosticCodes`/`DiagnosticExplanations`); the `variable_scoping.md` L20 paragraph and the "temporary exception" text P75 wrote; the 2026-11-01 "narrow, stored only" generator ruling (subsumed). **Spec:** `variable_scoping.md` N3 section + executed examples (`compute / 14`) + an `error` block for a visible cycle; R-BG drain first; R-FY output markers. **Snapshot-sensitive: ALONE** — count `.expected.cs` hits first, regenerate in one commit; spy-stdlib C#, spy-test C#, stdlib docs regen EARLY (module values in `spy/` modules change shape).

### 3.20l P78 — N1/N2/N4/N7: scope decides meaning (Semantic only — symbol resolution order, the ambiguity refusal, the const rules, one code per read; R-KD, R-KF, R-KG; generator EXISTS (P77a); **row 6c⁶⁵: directly after P77, before P79**)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`).

**Contract (N1, N2, N4, N7 — #2423):** **N1** every module-level and class-level declaration (`def`, `class`, `const`, `let`, `var`, module variable, property, `@static` member) is visible throughout its scope regardless of textual position; the innermost scope wins (a class member beats a module name); Python's one scope rule is KEPT — class-body names are not visible by bare name inside methods, nested functions, lambdas, comprehension elements **and nested type bodies (R-KG)**: write `H.x` / `self.x`. **N2** if reading top to bottom (Python) and reading by scope (N1) give a name DIFFERENT meanings, refuse — the one place is a class-body initializer, parameter default or field default that reads a member at or before its declaration while an outer binding of the same name exists; steer "rename, or qualify (`Box.size`)"; ONE new Semantic code for "refers to a later declaration that is ambiguous here" (`DiagnosticExplanations` entry), SPY0200 only for "no such name in scope". **N4** a literal or computed `const` follows N1–N3 like every other declaration — foldability never changes what a name means or whether a read is legal. **N7** bare, qualified, aliased, lambda, generator and call spellings of one read get the same verdict and the same code (today a bare forward read is SPY0200 and the qualified one SPY0618). **Matrix:** P77a's (binding kind × position × order × spelling × outer binding) is the generator; `ClassBodyReadPositionMatrixTests` (host × 7 positions × 8 siblings + order/shadow) gains the scope axis {own body, nested type's body one and two levels, lambda in an initializer} and host {class, struct, interface, union} as the HAND control (P21j's matrix, carried; ### 3.20m P79 — N6: `global` / `nonlocal` (Lexer + Parser + Semantic + CodeGen; R-KD; generator EXISTS (P77a); **row 6c⁶⁶: directly after P78, before P80**)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/` and `CodeGen/`; a refusal by direction on 49 corpus sites).

**Contract (N6, #2424 — owner: "a hard pill to swallow … but it helps disambiguate the inability to target module level members and outer function scope things while being explicit about what scope is being targeted, rather than outright refusal or silent rules"):** a bare `=`, augmented assignment, tuple/star store or walrus assigns a binding of the SAME function (any enclosing block of it); writing a MODULE binding (variable or module PROPERTY — spelling never changes the verdict, N7) from a function, method, lambda or nested def requires `global x` in that function; writing an ENCLOSING function's local or parameter requires `nonlocal x`; a bare store that would cross a function boundary is a compile error with the steer "`let x = …` declares a new local; `global x` / `nonlocal x` writes the outer binding"; `global` on a module `const`/`let`, or `nonlocal` on an enclosing `let`, is refused as an immutable binding (SPY0225 family); Python's own errors are kept (`nonlocal` with no enclosing binding; a name used before its declaration in the same function); whether `global x` may CREATE a module binding is the plan's door (default NO — declarations are explicit); reads are unaffected. **Lowering:** `global` → a qualified static-field write (the module class) / the property setter; `nonlocal` → the by-reference closure capture C# already gives a local function or lambda. **Matrix (P77a's store axis):** store form {`=`, augmented, tuple/star, walrus} × target scope {same block, enclosing block, enclosing function, module} × host {function, method, `@static` method, nested def, lambda (cannot declare `nonlocal` → refused), comprehension} × module binding kind {var, let, const, property, from-imported name, star-bound name} × declaration {none, `global`, `nonlocal`}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program (B02 function `x = 5` writes the module var `5` where python3 makes a local `1`; M08 the same statement declares a local when the module var is BELOW — one statement, two meanings by order; B03/B04 `2` / `1 5` vs UnboundLocalError; D14 walrus `5 5` vs `5 1`; B08/D07 nested def stores to an enclosing local `2` / `9` vs UnboundLocalError / `1`; E09 walrus in a lambda `5` vs `1`; B34 `9` vs `1`; B24 `3`), ice · program (#2151: C36/E07/E08 method / `@static` method store to a module var → SPY0908 CS0103), false-refusal · program (A20 `global x` → SPY0134; python3 `2`). **Fidelity-scope row:** which programs run vs are refused — COMPARED against the spec (the spec adopts Python's rule). **Direction record:** 49 module stores in 25 files (fixtures 40, spec 7, stdlib `.spy` 2 — `random_module.spy:17` `_random`, `socket_module.spy:449` `_default_timeout` — at most 29 `global` lines; ### 3.20n P80 — S2: construct target reuse (Semantic only; R-KD; generator EXISTS (P77a); **row 6c⁶⁷: directly after P79, before P52**)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`; a refusal by direction).

**Contract (S2, #2425):** a `for` target, an `except … as` name and a `with … as` name are FRESH bindings scoped to their construct (N5; Rust, Swift and C# `foreach` agree) and a loop variable is fresh per iteration; when the target has the same name as a binding visible at that point (a local of the same function, an enclosing function's binding, a module binding) AND that name is READ after the construct, the program is REFUSED naming the outer binding and the later read — the one case where Sharpy's value would differ from Python's; steer "rename the target, or assign the outer name explicitly inside the construct"; a same-name target with NO later read stays legal. **Matrix (P77a's):** construct {`for`, `except as`, `with as`, comprehension `for` (control: already its own scope)} × outer binding {same-function local, enclosing function's local/parameter, module var, module let/const, none (control)} × later read {none, same block, enclosing block, after a nested def} × target shape {name, tuple, star}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program (B16 `x = "outer"; for x in range(3): pass; print(x)` → `outer`, python3 `2` — a spec example; D13 module `x = 100` + the loop in `main` → `100` vs `2`; B23 `except … as e` over an outer local `e` → `1` vs UnboundLocalError). **Fidelity-scope row:** value of an expression — COMPARED; the per-iteration closure cells B11/B12 (`[0, 1, 2]` vs python3 `[2, 2, 2]`) are the designed deviation — rows `loop-variable-fresh-per-iteration` and `construct-target-reuses-outer-name` landed @ 9957b3714 and are updated to the refusal. **Direction record:** 4 corpus sites (`basics/let_after_for_loop.spy:5`, `definite_assignment/with_as_binder_does_not_assign_outer_spy0600_1635.spy:13`, `definite_assignment/write_kinds_ok_1635.spy:40`, spec `variable_scoping.md:81`) + 6 same-shape sites with no later read that stay legal (census @ d2d5cbcab). **Spec:** `variable_scoping.md` / `comprehensions.md` examples (B16 is a spec example). **New SPY code:** the construct-target reuse refusal (Semantic overflow; `DiagnosticExplanations`). No snapshot moves by design.
- **#2425** (the group) — silent-wrong · program; cells @ 3ef53c6ec (the issue).
- **Call 38 arms (owner 2026-11-05; posted on #2427 and #2425) — each a refusal by direction, so the direction record widens (corpus census in the plan):** **R-KX (D10 a)** — a read above a same-scope declaration under an outer binding is REFUSED "`x` is declared later in this scope" — `x: int = 1` / `def main(): print(x); let x = 2; print(x)` → today the outer value `1 2` for every binder kind and every outer kind (var, let, const, def, class, enclosing local, builtin); python UnboundLocalError, Swift and C# (CS0841 / CS0136) refuse, Rust reads the outer (96 cells + B21/B22). Rejected: (b) keep the outer binding + a deviation row (the Rust reading); (a) → (b) is a relaxation. **R-KZ (D12 a)** — S2 extends to `match` captures and `as` captures: a capture is fresh per arm; no outer → SPY0200 after the arm stays; outer + a later read → refused like a `for` target; the failed-guard cells (`case x if x > 9` binds `x = 2` in python even when the guard fails; Sharpy reads the outer `5`) are covered (52 cells). Rejected: (b) builtins are not bindings + a deviation row. **R-LC (D15 a)** — a nested def reading an enclosing local declared below it is REFUSED "declared later", with and without an outer binding — L28/B09 `def g() -> int: return n` / `n: int = 3` / `print(g())` → SPY0200 today, and with a module `n` it silently reads the MODULE value (`5`; python3 `3` / `15` — the closure resolves at call time; 6 cells); D10's rule read through a closure. Rejected: (b) late binding (emission must hoist the local; a relaxation later). **R-LE (D17 a)** — a lambda-typed local reading itself (`f: () -> int = lambda: f()`; C# refuses `Func<int> f = () => f();`) stays refused with the steer "write a nested def" — message only (3 cells). Rejected: (b) nothing / drop block scope (reverses #1974) / hoist body bindings (one-way; python's footgun).


### 3.20i P21k — one name, one meaning at every consumer: residue (Semantic symbol resolution + the import-binding record + CodeGen's type and member spellers; P21h/P21i residue; R-JY, R-JZ, R-CZ binds; **Gen REQUIRED FIRST**; **row 6c⁹: directly after P21j, before P69**)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/`; d: a residue of groups (P21h, P21i) that passed a verify round and escaped).

**Contract (P21h/P21i's, carried to the consumers they missed):** a binding's RECORDED identity is what every consumer reads — (i) a Sharpy type's import clone (alias, re-exporter, aliasing re-exporter, two-hop) resolves to its DECLARATION for layout facts (nested types), type identity (assignability, argument passing) and materialized interfaces (warm builds), and is emitted under the declaration's qualified name in every position (#2402, #1928); (ii) a type-body member is spelled by its declaration's materialized `CSharpName` at every reference — instance and `@static` member access included (#2403; R-CZ's rule, P21i D5's module-kind cure applied to class members); (iii) an explicit import merged into a star binding of the same symbol is recorded EXPLICIT, so the module-variable verdict is SPY0204 in every order (#2408, R-JZ). **Matrix:** clone kind {alias, re-exporter, aliasing re-exporter, two-hop} × consumer {nested-type access, annotation, identity compare, isinstance, match head, static member, warm incremental} × route {run, project}; property form {auto, function-style} × receiver {instance, @static} × use {read, write, augmented} × site {same file, imported} × host {class, struct, dataclass}; provenance {same module, stdlib, star of a re-exporter + direct, package star + submodule, star + plain re-exporter, two stars (control)} × order. **Lane / reach:** ice · program (#2402 #1928 #2403); missing-refusal · program (#2408). **Standing harness:** `ModuleMemberQualificationMatrixTests`, `ModuleClassNameAuthorityMatrixTests`, `ImportBindingProjectRouteTests.TakeoverProvenanceCells` (pins today's outputs — flips by direction), `TypeDenotingReceiverMatrixTests` (P4b Family C). **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — emit-and-run over the three matrices against the direct-import / snake_case / explicit-only twins, allowlist ratchet. **Class tracker:** #2402. The facts the cure reads exist since P21i: `Symbol.DeclarationRoot` (the one `OriginSymbol` chain walker), the defining-module identity (R-JK), the materialized module-level member fact.
- **#2402** (FIRST; the tracker) — ice · program; lead-measured @ 60bd27738 (`run`/`project`): `core.spy` `class Box:` / `class Inner: v: int = 11`, `main.spy` `from core import Box as B` + `print(B.Inner().v)` → SPY0909 TypeSyntaxMapper "layout fact missing (#2102, Rule 2): no namespace recorded for type 'Box' (TypeSymbol, hasCodeGenInfo=False)"; also `from lib1 import Box; Box.Inner()` (re-exporter, when the entry sorts first), `import lib1; lib1.Box.Inner()`, the annotation `i: Box.Inner`; expected `11`. `from core import Box as B` + `from core import Box` + `c: Box = B()` (also `f(B())`, `c: core.Box = B()`) → SPY0220 "Cannot assign type 'B' to … 'Box'" in both orders; expected `8`. Warm: entry `from lib1 import Box, Impl` (lib1 re-exports an interface and its implementer) — cold OK, warm after touching lib1 OK, the next no-op build SPY0909 "TypeSymbol 'Impl' has 2 interface(s) but SemanticBinding.GetInterfaces() holds 1" (measured by the verification pass, not re-run by the lead; the zz-order and direct-only controls stay green). The one cell that moved with b7f365060: the dual import with the entry sorting first was SPY0204 and is now cell 1's ICE — the defect exists without the dual import. **Ruling R-JY (a):** this group; rejected (b) scatter to P16/P26.
- **#1928** (moved from P16 §3.33 at call 35) — ice · program; the EMISSION consumer: `lib.spy` `class P`, `main.spy` `from lib import P as Q` + `isinstance(v, Q)` → SPY0908 CS0426/CS0234 (the type is emitted under the ALIAS name in the aliased module's namespace); the 2026-10-30 cells @ 60bd27738: `B.make().v` (`@static` member access through the alias), `case B():`, `isinstance(x, B)`, on `run` and `project`, direct alias and through a re-exporter; the annotation, constructor, base class and `list[B]` run. The issue's original note that the static-member cell passes is refuted @ 60bd27738.
- **#2403** (R-CZ binds — the declaration governs every reference; one materialized `CSharpName` per symbol) — ice · program; lead-measured @ b9730a2ec: `class Klass:` + `@static` + `property CAP_Q: int = 4`; `print(Klass.CAP_Q)` → SPY0908 CS0117 "'Klass' does not contain a definition for 'CAP_Q'" (declared `CapQ` through the field speller, read through the casing rule); instance `property CAP_I` + `Klass().CAP_I` → CS1061; controls `property cap_q` → `4`, a static FIELD `CAP_X` read as `Klass.CAP_X` → `4`; the same on `from lib import Klass`, `lib.Klass.CAP_Q`, `a.Klass.CAP_Q`. The matrix (form × receiver × use × site × host) is a generator arm, never a hand table; `ModuleMemberQualificationMatrixTests` is the warm control.

Rejected: (b) amend the row (`module-binding-collision-refused`) so a star-also-bound name stays a takeover default beside an explicit import — star-dependent verdicts; admitting programs is the one-way direction.

### 3.21 P37 — slot-directed operand totality (Semantic; generator FIRST; **R-GX: moved to row 6f — directly after P70, before P16b**)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (R-W, slot-directed):** a `Some(v)` / `None()` / empty-literal operand against a typed other operand is slot-directed in EVERY position, not only inside a call argument. **Matrix:** expression {`x == Some(1)`, `x != Some(2)`, `x == None()`, `xs == []`, `d == {}`} × position {call argument (control), `assert`, `if`, `while`, ternary condition, bare assignment, under `not`, `and`/`or` operand, comprehension condition, match guard, return, conditional-expression branch against a typed other branch, no slot with a typed argument (`x = Some(3)`)} × operand side {left, right}. **Lane / reach:** false-refusal · program. **Standing harness:** none. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** (≥2 axes × ≥3 values) — enumerate expression × position × side, compile and run against the call-argument twin as the oracle, allowlist ratchet; the sibling slot cells #2146 (struct ctor args, P17d) and #2154 (star leaves, P21d) join this harness as allowlisted rows that their own groups drain — their placements do not move. **Class tracker:** #2162 (#2220 folded onto it: the two extra positions).
- **#2162** (FIRST; re-scoped) — false-refusal · program; the issue's `assert` framing is REFUTED — measured @ b1603d6a2 (`probes/c_*.spy`): `x = d.get("a"); x == Some(1)` compiles and prints `True` ONLY as a `print` argument; it is SPY0227 "Cannot infer type for 'Some()'" in `assert`, `if`, `while`, the ternary, `b = x == Some(1)` and `not (…)` (`CheckAssert` at `TypeChecker.Statements.cs:2394` is one of the positions, not the class). Cell (a) of the issue (the `@test` host `is None` lowering) was #2171 (chore, done). The `tagged_unions.md` SPY0227 sentence names the extra positions.

- **#2303 cell (folded by call 22, R-GX; the issue closed)** — ice · program; `Ok(1)` / `Err("e")` into a nullable-Result slot (`-> int !str | None`, `let x: int !str | None`) reach Roslyn CS0029 (`Result<int, object>` → `Result<int, string>?`) at return and local alike, while `-> int !str` and `return None` run (owner-measured @ fce79bf92, Debug `sharpyc run`). The matrix gains an operand-form value {`Ok(v)`, `Err(e)`} and a **slot-wrapper axis** {none, `| None`, `?`} × slot {return, annotated local, parameter, field initializer, collection element}; `(T !E)?` is `Optional[tuple[…]]` (parentheses in a type are tuple syntax, #2307) and is not a member. #2307's per-position gate (P16b) needs this cell drained — hence P37 directly before P16b.

### 3.21b P58 — target-typed implicit member access `.RED` (Parser + Semantic; R-FN; experimental flag; after P37, P23 and P13b; before P21b)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (#2267's; `overload_resolution.md` "Argument types are computed before candidates are considered"):** in an expression position whose contextual type `T` is RESOLVED, a leading-dot member `.NAME` denotes the static member `T.NAME` and resolves to the same symbol as the qualified spelling; it takes its type only from a resolved target — never from a candidate set — so under an overloaded callee it is refused with a qualify fix-it, exactly as an untyped collection literal is. One more member of the context-sensitive expression class; no new resolution engine; declaration-order independence by construction. **Measured @ 88345108f (issue):** `c: Color = .RED` is SPY0100 "Unexpected token: Dot". Lexer fact: `.` + digit is a float literal, so `.RED` is `Dot Identifier` — unambiguous. **Matrix:** member kind {enum member, `const`, static property, static field} × context {annotated init, typed-target store, typed `return`, parameter default, positional arg to a single-candidate callee, named arg, arg to an overloaded callee (REFUSED), `case .RED:` head, `==`/`!=`/`in` operand with a typed other side, slot-directed collection element (R-W), ternary arms, `match` expression arms} × contextual type shape {`T`, `T?`, `T | None`, `A | B` (refused unless exactly one alternative has the member), CLR enum, CLR static class, generic instantiation statics} × spelling {bare, backtick-escaped, nested chain `.Inner.A` (refused in v1), shadowed by a same-named local (the member wins)}. **Lane / reach:** design (no lane; a Python-invalid surface — `SyntaxError` — in the family of `[T]`, `t"…"`, `|>`). **Standing harness:** P37's slot-directed generator (expression × position × side). **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — P37's generator gains `.X` as an expression value and asserts each cell against its qualified twin (symbol identity via `emit ast` AND `run` output equal), the refusal cells with the qualified positive control; allowlist ratchet. **Class tracker:** #2267. Feature-lifecycle: experimental flag (Parser scope), ungated use → SPY0331, twin fixtures under `TestFixtures/experimental/`. LSP: completion after `.` in a typed slot lists `T`'s eligible statics; hover resolves the member. Formatter idempotent on `.RED`. Snapshot-neutral (gated; new syntax only).
- **#2267** (R-FN; the group) — design. **Ruling R-FN (option a):** v1 scope = enum members + `const`/static fields/static properties; static METHODS and constructors are EXCLUDED (they reintroduce overload resolution on return type — Dart's scope, revisit after v1); the refusal under an overloaded callee or an unresolved slot is a NEW SPY02xx "implicit member `.X` needs a resolved contextual type" (next free Semantic code at plan time; `DiagnosticExplanations` entry) with a fix-it that inserts the qualifier; `case .RED:` emits no SPY0468. Materialization: the resolved target is a node-keyed `SemanticInfo` fact (→ `MergeFrom`); the emitter only qualifies. Rejected: (b) Dart's scope including static methods/ctors; (c) scope as (a) but reuse SPY0227 (a "cannot infer" message with no fix-it). Scope is two-way (additive later); the new code is permanent. Spec: `expressions.md` "Member Access" section (or a new page), `match_statement.md` constant patterns, `overload_resolution.md` names the new member of the class; every block executed.

### 3.22 P21b → P21c — bindings under B1–B12 (`let` immutable / `var` / `const`; R-BI…R-BM, R-CN, **R-GV**; tracking #1974 + #2153 #2150 #2155 #2151 #2125 #2144 #2148; two plans, two gates)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Design of record (R-GV, 2026-10-14 — supersedes the 2026-09-21 model and ruling A; the B-rulings are the owner's words on #1974, 2026-10-13/14):** **B1** `let x = e` is a fresh IMMUTABLE binding, `var x = e` a fresh mutable one, `const` unchanged (`let` restricts the binding, not the value: `let p = P(); p.hp = 0` is fine); **B2** shadowing is unrestricted (`let`/`var` may shadow any binding in the same or an enclosing block, `var x = x` over a `let` or a parameter included; the emitter already versions names, so CS0136 does not apply); **B3** parameters are `let`-like (reassigning one is an error; the escape is a shadow `let s = s.strip()`); **B4** a construct-owned binding (`for` target, `with`/`except … as`, `case` capture, `out let`) is `let`-like — a write to a `for` target in the body is an error; **B5** walrus: on an existing `var` it writes; on an unbound name it introduces a `let` (in a `while` condition it rebinds each pass); on an existing `let` it is an ERROR with a "declare it with `var`" fix-it — never a silent shadow (amends R-BK); **B6** class and struct members require `let` (C# `readonly` field) / `var` (plain field) / `const`; keywordless `x: T` in a type body is retired (the codemod classifies it); `@final` on a FIELD is retired (`let` is the only readonly-field spelling; `@final` on a class unchanged, already refused on interfaces SPY0322); `@static let` / `@static var` combine; **B7** a `let` field with no initializer is assigned in every `__init__`; a `var` field likewise unless its type has a meaningful default (value type, `T | None`, `T?`) — mirrors `property init`; **B8** the spec states `let` field vs `prop get` (a field can be passed by `ref` and is serialized by Unity; a property can do neither; `[SerializeField]` on a `let` field is an error); **B9 (amended 2026-10-13)** module scope allows `let` (`static readonly`), `var` (a mutable static field on the module class — a function writes it with bare `=`, which STAYS; this diverges from python and the spec says so; no `global`) and `const`; a function cannot write a module `let`/`const` (the error steers to `var`); `const` narrows to what C# can make a `const`, anything else errors with the `let` steer; a lint for `public const` in library code; **B10** lints with fix-its: "`let x` is reassigned; declare it with `var`" (error), "`var x` is never reassigned; use `let`" (warning); **B11** `var` (and `produces`, `consumes`, `prop`) are hard keywords; backticks escape any keyword; **B12** in a STRUCT a `let`/`var` field of non-nullable reference type needs an initializer (`net10.0`: C# 10 struct field initializers) or a nullable type (`netstandard2.1`, C# 9); value-typed struct fields keep their zero default; B7's `__init__` rule applies to classes. Unchanged: the staging (21a DONE → 21b codemod → 21c flip), SPY0611 (R-BL), `auto` retired, R-BJ (ungated), R-BM (`out let`). **Codemod (21b, widened):** every declaration becomes `let` or `var` from its WRITE facts (written after declaration through `=`, augmented assignment or `:=` → `var`; else `let`); class fields written outside a constructor → `var`; module bindings written from any function → `var`, else `let`; no module binding is hand-rewritten (197 module-level annotated bindings in 98 files, 6 in spy-sourced stdlib — owner count @ fce79bf92). **Hypotheses for the plan author (not ruled):** B6's `let` field emits C# `readonly`, so 21b's "empty diff over regenerated C#" acceptance holds only if the modifier lands in 21c — the plan says which stage; B9's refusal of a function writing a module `let` turns today's measured write-through (`counter` prints 2 @ fce79bf92) into a refusal only where the codemod classified `let` — a direction record per module binding kind. **Members (R-GV):** #1974 (FIRST) · #2153 (B3: a local shadowing an implicit accessor parameter is rewritten as the parameter; `let value = value…` becomes the idiom) · #2150 (B2: a callable local shadowing a module function is ignored at the call) · #2155 (B10: per-binding, not per-name, unused tracking — the `var`-never-reassigned lint shares it) · #2151 (B9: writing a module `var` from a method body is CS0103 behind SPY0908; its generic-call cell rides along, §3.19) · #2125 (B9: module redeclaration keeps the first value silently — decided under `let`/`var`; R-CY's SPY0204 stands for the const twin) · #2148 (B6: `TypeBodyStatementValidator` — type bodies require `let`/`var`/`const`) · #2144 (B5/B7: definite assignment at binding sites; "a `let` field is assigned in every `__init__`" and the walrus sites read it). Adjacent, NOT in scope unless the plan finds a shared seam: #2206 #2156 #2154 #2152 (P21d residue, §3.19), #2147 (P30), #2213 (P26), #2268 (P59 — reuses the B-rulings), #1804 (parked; R-HA). **Every sentence below written for the 2026-09-21 model (module `const`-only, `let` mutable, `x: T = e` → `const`) is superseded where it conflicts with B1–B12; the seams, harness names and measured facts stand.**

**Previous design of record (2026-09-21; SUPERSEDED by R-GV above — kept for the R-BI…R-BM record):** #1974 body + its three owner comments (2026-09-21), summarized in memory `design_let_binding_model_1974` — `let <target> = e` is a fresh block-scoped MUTABLE binding, function/block scope only (shadows; same scope → new version); bare `<target> = e` is write-through to the nearest existing binding; `const NAME[: T] = e` is assign-once at module, class and local scope (C# `const` for literals, `static readonly` for runtime init); **module scope is `const`-only**; `auto` is fully retired (`x: auto` → `let x`; no `-> auto`; `auto` leaves the lexer); no `let mut`/`var`/`global`/`nonlocal`; staged rollout. Those parts are RULED on the issue — do not re-derive. **21a (Stage 1) is DONE** (plan-2003b4; `let` lands additive and ungated per R-BJ; `:=` unchanged per R-BK; `out let x` introduces, `out x` writes through per R-BM). #2207 #2201 #2204 were folded onto #1974 (R-EC: after P21c a bare first assignment is SPY0611 with the `let` steer, so SPY0606 never fires alone; the static-method cell and the missing `let` mention in the syntax skill are posted there).

**Contract:** one binding law in every binding position — `let` (and the positional introducers `:=`, `for`, `with/except … as`, `case`, parameters) introduce; every other store writes through; the law reads identically at every scope kind. **Matrix:** spelling {`let x = e`, `let x: T = e`, bare `x = e`, `x: T = e`, `x := e`, `out x` / `out let x`} × predecessor {none, same scope, enclosing block, enclosing function, module const, class member (SPY0606 control)} × scope kind {function body, each `BlockKinds.BodyKinds` row, module, class body} × stage {21a, 21c}. **Standing harness:** `BlockScopeRedeclarationMatrixTests` (block-kind × binder-axis matrix, #1560/#1647; gains the spelling axis; P21d extends it first); `ModuleLevelValidator` cells for module `let` / annotated-bare / `const`; `StoreTargetMatrixTests` gains the `let` store position.
- **Call 38 cells on #1974 (P77a's seeds and the harness's unruled arms; owner 2026-11-05; full text on #2427 and #1974):** **R-LD (D16 a)** — A12 `A: int = 1` / `A: int = 2` / `print(A)` prints `1` @ fd925ddca (python3 `2`) and B28 `def helper(f: int)` + nested `def f()` + `print(f)` prints `5` (the parameter; python3 the function) are same-scope re-declarations → SPY0204 (R-HJ's rule); rejected: last-wins store. **R-LF (D18 a)** — the same-block closure cells `x: int = 13` / `g: () -> int = lambda: x` / `let x = 2` (or `x: int = 2`) / `print(g())` → `13` (python3 `2`): a same-block re-declaration seen by an earlier closure is refused SPY0204, or a store the closure must see — the B-rules decide which, never `13`; the block-scope deviation row (a binder in an `if`/`for`/`while`/`match`/`with` body is block-scoped: `1`, python `2`) widens to every binder {annotated, const, def, lambda-typed}; rejected: drop block scope.

### 3.23 P25 — import-binding authority (R-CX, R-DJ; generator FIRST)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** `from m import X`, `from m import X as Y`, `import m` + `m.X`, wildcard and re-export bind the symbol module `m` DECLARES, with its declared type, in every position, on every route {run, project, LSP, warm}, whatever the alphabetical order of modules — one resolution rule, no case-insensitive fallback; and every consumer of an import fact reads the store its producer wrote (Rule 2). **Matrix:** import form {from, from-as, wildcard, import-qualified, re-export, alias of alias} × member kind {class, struct, enum, union, interface, function, variable, module alias, submodule, builtin decorator via `functools`, bracket attribute} × declaring-module order {before, after the same-named other module} × route {run, project, warm}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Standing harness:** `ModuleMemberQualificationMatrixTests` (emit spellings, flat modules only), `ColdWarmBuildDifferentialTests`. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — a differential harness that generates two-module projects over the matrix, runs them and compares against the single-file twin (the oracle), allowlist ratchet. **Class tracker:** #2097. Before P28 (the warm harness replays import bindings).

The spec is silent on name case (`module_resolution.md`, `import_statements.md`, `module_system.md`, grep-only @ 3f16f261e), so the surface is "which programs run vs are refused" where the spec defers to Python — COMPARED. **Ruling R-HH (a):** module FILE names match by exact case on every OS — SPY0300 for `GREETER`, the directory-entry case check at the one module-path resolver; `module_resolution.md` gains the sentence; #2132's contract "a name resolves by case where Python's does not — never" gains the axis {member fallback, module file}. Rejected: (b) OS-native acceptance with a `deviations.yaml` row (compilability would depend on the host filesystem; one-way in practice). (a) is a refusal, relaxable later.

- **#2406** (call 35; placed by the P21i P6.6 ruling — the b9730a2ec forwarder rename REVERTED @ 481cae5ea; the `__init__` forwarder-layout tracker; **R-KC** on cell 9) — ice · program; lead-measured @ 92609edcf (`run`): `pc/core.spy` `property CAP_P: int = 7`, `pc/__init__.spy` `from pc.core import CAP_P`, `import pc; print(pc.CAP_P)` → SPY0908 CS0117 "'CoreModule' does not contain a definition for 'CAP_P'" (the declaration is `CapP`; cell 1); an alias beside the package's own same-named def (`from other import ov as helper2` + `def ov(x: object)`) → CS0117 'OtherModule' has no 'Helper2' (cell 2); two forwarders reaching one C# name → CS0117 (cell 3); renamed def `main`/`MainFunc` (cell 4, #2065); aliased def read qualified with the entry sorting AFTER `pc/` (cell 5); one declaration re-exported in TWO statements → CS0111/CS0102 (cell 6); overloaded def — one overload forwarded (cell 7); the package's own `cap_p` beside the forwarder for `CAP_P` → CS0102 (cell 8; forwarders are outside the module-level collision check's kind set); a store through a forwarder → CS0200 (cell 9; #2116's store cell). **Contract:** the forwarder's OWN name follows the package BINDING (cells 2, 5), its TARGET is the declaration's materialized name on the DECLARING module's class, the dedupe key includes the target class, every overload is forwarded, forwarders join the collision check. **Hazards measured on b9730a2ec (reverted):** a declaration-named forwarder made cells 2–3 SILENT WRONG (`other` / `11`) — the generator's negative controls. **Ruling R-KC (a):** a store whose target is a re-export binding is REFUSED — a new Semantic code (Semantic band; `DiagnosticExplanations`) with the steer "assign through the declaring module (`pc.core.level = 3`)", a `docs/deviations.yaml` row beside `module-binding-collision-refused` ("Sharpy refuses rather than rebinds"); reads stay live-delegating. Rejected: (b) write-through to the declaration (`pc.level = 3` changes `pc.core.level`; python3 keeps `7` — a silent divergence on a COMPARED surface); (c) Python's copy semantics (the forwarder becomes a field; reads diverge from today's delegation). (b)/(c) fix a runtime value — one-way; (a) is relaxable.

### 3.24 P26 — emitted-name & layout authority (P14 residue; R-CR, R-CZ, R-DH; lands alone; generator FIRST)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (the #2013 one):** one function computes each emitted C# identifier, namespace and module path, and every declaration, reference, collision check and entry-point choice reads it; the entry point is a compilation OPTION (`WithMainTypeName`, since P14c), never a spelling rule; every runtime/BCL name the emitter writes is `global::`-qualified; a package-level refusal names only a real C# clash in the final layout; a file NAME is not program text. **Matrix:** python spelling {`main`, `Main`, `` `Main` ``, `_x`, `` `q` ``, keyword-named, SPY0453-cased, `_`} × position {declaration, same-module reference, from-import reference, qualified reference, value reference, collision check (SPY0523/SPY0526/SPY0615), entry-point choice} × module kind {entry, non-entry exe module, library module, `__init__.spy`, module named `sharpy`/`system`/`builtins`} × route {run, project, C# consumer}; plus file-name characters {`:`, `\`, `"`, space, leading digit, unicode} × consumer {assembly name, `#line` path, module class, namespace segment, mirrored output path} × route {run, project, `--emit-cs-to`, LSP}. **Lane / reach:** ice · program. **Standing harness:** `ModuleClassNameAuthorityMatrixTests`, `ModuleClassMergeMatrixTests`, `MemberEnclosingTypeCollisionMatrixTests`, `LayoutFactConsumerScanTests` (chore #2102). **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — enumerate spelling × position × module kind, emit and Roslyn-compile, compare the identifier at every position against the one authority; allowlist ratchet. **Class tracker:** #2065. **Snapshot-sensitive:** R-CR moves every snapshot holding a non-entry `main`. **Order inside the group:** #2127 (the one path authority) before #2160 and #2170; the group before P43, P29 and P36.
- **#2065** (R-CR; FIRST) — ice · program at filing; measured @ db8e05bf1: the CS0117 is GONE (P14c materialized the rename — declaration and references both spell `MainFunc`; `from util import main as um`, `util.main()`, `f = util.main` print `4 3 3 3`). Re-scoped by the ruling. **Ruling R-CR (option c′ — owner: "why doesn't option (c) emit def main() as Main()? it would be legal no and thus consistent with def Main() and def `Main`()"):** RETIRE the `MainFunc` rename — every function emits its mangled name (`main` → `Main`) in every module kind, and the exe's entry point is the compilation option P14c already sets (`AssemblyCompiler.cs:154` `WithMainTypeName` from `ProjectCompiler.Utilities.cs:27`); a library needs no entry point and Roslyn accepts a `static Main` in one. Direction record: `Util.UtilModule.MainFunc()` → `Util.UtilModule.Main()` (one-way for C# consumers of a Sharpy library's non-entry `main`; tiny surface). Rejected: (c) keep `MainFunc`; (a) refuse `Main` by emitted name; (b) rename every emitted `Main` (breaks backtick = verbatim). Cells: {`def main`, `def Main`, ``def `Main` ``} × {non-entry exe module, library module, entry control} × {run, project, C# consumer}; #2094's predicted `exe_app × def Main` ICE did not materialize — pin it green.
- **#2335** (R-HR; the entry-point SHAPE tracker, retitled at call 26) — ice · program. **Contract:** `program_entry_point.md:14-15` ("takes no parameters", "implicit `None` return") is checked by Sharpy for the ENTRY file's `main` and never for a library's `def main(...)`, which is a plain function under R-CR (every module's `main` emits `Main`; the entry point is the compilation option). **Measured (the issue @ f10b8381f and @ 27bf064bb, built CLI):** `def main(args: list[str])`, `def main(x: int)`, `def main() -> str` → `emit diagnostics` clean, `run` SPY0908 CS1558; the only entry-point refusal today is a missing `main` (SPY0403). **Matrix:** presence {present, absent (SPY0403 control)} × parameters {none, scalar, `list[str]`, defaulted} × return {implicit None, explicit `-> None`, other} × module kind {entry file, non-entry exe module, library} × route {run, project, build}. Cure: one Validation refusal beside SPY0403 naming the spec rule (code at the plan's call — Validation overflow range), fired from the entry-point choice P26 owns; direction record CS1558 → the refusal; mutation. Cells 4–5 of the issue (access decorators on a class) are #2074's (§3.45, R-HS) and are struck from this tracker. Rejected (R-HR): (b) fold cells 1–3 into #2065 (R-CR's rename retirement is a different contract); (c) a new group "declaration-site rules left to Roslyn" (a meta-class — the SPY0908 net — whose members #2143 #2212 #2021 already have groups). Two-way.
- **#2098** — ice · program; `class Sharpy` / `class System` / a module `sharpy.spy` or `system.spy` re-binds the emitter's unqualified `Sharpy.`/`System.` names (CS0426/CS0234); sibling: SPY0615 compares module namespaces against spelled .NET types but not the runtime types the EMITTER injects (`Sharpy.Builtins` shadowed by RootNamespace `Sharpy` + `builtins.spy`). Totality scan over emitted C# beside those four shadows, positive control + mutation; the injected-runtime-type set joins SPY0615.
- **#2086** — false-refusal · program; SPY0526 refusal 2 compares emitted identifiers and refuses `X: int` in `__init__.spy` beside `x.spy`; under P14c only an `__init__` TYPE clashes with a child namespace segment (the #2039 comment records the layout). Narrow to real clashes: {type, function, variable} × {same python name, same emitted name only}; direction refused → runs.
- **#2046** (R-CZ) — ice · probe; measured @ db8e05bf1: class field `` `q` `` + `v.q` → SPY0908 CS1061, ``v.`q` `` → `4`; method `` `do_it` `` + `V().do_it()` → CS1061; enum `` `red` `` + `Color.red.value` → `1` (declaration governs since plan-0ca7b7 ruling 3). **Ruling R-CZ (option a):** the declaration governs every reference for class members too — one materialized `CSharpName` per symbol, every reference reads it, the backtick only changes what is emitted; `EscapedDeclarationOnly_StaysCs1061_TheClosed478Contract` is re-pinned to the run. Rejected: (b) SPY0203 with a backtick steer (enum members and class members would answer the same question differently unless ruling 3 were reversed).
- **#2127** — tooling · probe; `CompilationUnitFactory.ComputeModulePath` is a THIRD module-path derivation beside `ModuleIdentifiers`/`ComputeSourceRootPath` (`ProjectCompiler.Utilities.cs:87` roots at the common directory of all sources; `ProjectCompiler.Parsing.cs:202/267/418` call `ComputeModulePath(file, config.ProjectDirectory)` which strips leading `..`); route it through the one authority; the two generator-assembly compile-error sites stay path-less and SPY0554 carries `Phase=Unknown` (#2032 contract). Cell posted 2026-10-04b from the chores round.
- **#2160** (R-DH) — silent-wrong · program; measured by the filer @ db8e05bf1: `proj/ext/lib.spy` and `../ext/lib.spy` both become `ext.lib`, "Build succeeded.". **Ruling R-DH (option a):** derive through the one authority relative to the source root, and refuse any two units that still map to one dotted name (`a.b.spy` beside `a/b.spy`; the #2170 characters) with a new Validation code naming BOTH files. Rejected: (b) refuse out-of-project sources (contradicts the common-root design and misses the sibling spelling); (c) deterministic rename (one-way for C# consumers, leaks into `import` spellings). Lands after #2127.
- **#2170** — ice · probe; measured by the filer @ b1603d6a2: a filename containing `:` or `\` → SPY0908 CS8203 (invalid assembly name), `"` → SPY0599 (a `#line` path written unescaped); `a b`, `a,b`, `a=b`, `a'b`, `a*b`, `a?b`, `a<b`, `1abc`, `a-b`, `a.b` all print `1` (controls). The assembly name and every path written into generated C# go through the one authority (SyntaxFactory's literal escaping for paths; invalid assembly-name characters mapped). **Call 24 cell (P54 isolation-sweep lane @ 486c9a88a; ice · API):** the programmatic API with `OutputAssemblyPath` set and `AssemblyName` unset names the assembly `AssemblyName ?? RootNamespace` = `""` → SPY0908 CS8203 "Name cannot be empty" (`SyntheticProject.BuildConfig` + `AssemblyCompiler`, the filer's reading); the CLI always passes a name. The route axis gains {API}; the authority names the assembly from the entry stem or refuses naming the missing option.
- **#2213** (tracker) — ice · probe; a module-level `const _` has no C# spelling (SPY0599); symbol kind × read matrix on the issue (locals/parameters are `__spy_underscore` since #2166).

### 3.25 P43 — entry independence: per-file emit ≡ project (R-DP, R-DQ; lands alone; generator FIRST; after P26)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** a module's dotted name, C# namespace, import resolution and cycle verdict are functions of the source root and the file set, never of which file is the entry or of file-name order (`module_system.md`: "the layout is the same in every mode"). **Matrix:** entry choice {each file of the set as the entry, the project} × layout {flat, package, nested package} × root namespace {none, `-n X`} × observation {namespace per unit, import resolution, cycle verdict, diagnostic location}. **Lane / reach:** silent-wrong · program. **Standing harness:** none — `ModuleMemberQualificationMatrixTests` and the P25 generator run one entry per fixture. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — for every multi-file fixture, emit each file as its own entry and compare each unit's namespace, references and diagnostics with the project route; allowlist ratchet; it asserts the search-path behaviour chore #2233 landed. **Class tracker:** #2192. R-EQ (b) — compiling the import closure in project mode — stays open here.
- **#2192** (R-DP; FIRST) — measured @ 6b208066e (`probes/ns/`): `pad.spy` as the entry → `namespace Pad`, as a dependency → `SharpyApp.Pad`; with `-n Demo`, `src/eng/a.spy` as the entry → `Demo.A`, as a dependency → `Demo.Eng.A`. **Ruling R-DP:** the dependency form everywhere — `SharpyApp.<path from the source root>` when no `-n` is given. One-way for C# consumers of single-file entries; the plan counts the snapshot churn first.
- **#2216** — false-refusal · program; a dotted import inside a package resolves relative to the entry file, not the `-m` root (filer, 0.21.0). Its failing import is covered by chore #2233 (R-EL); it stays open here for the entry-independence generator and closes there. Reads #2127's path authority.
- **#2215 · #2219** (R-DQ) — the cycle verdict depends on the entry (#2215) and on file names, classifies only the deferred edge, and counts uses inside the declaring module (#2219, with the owner's correction comment). **Ruling R-DQ:** the "proposed" annotation-only rule in `module_system.md` becomes normative — a cycle compiles only when every symbol imported on every edge of the cycle is annotation-only; the verdict is independent of entry and file names; the diagnostic points at the offending import USE. Programs that compile today by accident are refused — verified by direction.

- **Ruling R-GY on #2219 (owner, 2026-10-14; the P53 implementer asked):** a constructor call through a type-argued generic reference and ANY expression-position type argument naming the deferred symbol (`Box[int]()`, `Box[B]()`, `list[B]()`) is RUNTIME use — the RFC's "constructor calls" and "any expression-position usage" clauses (`module_system.md:263-269`), normative under R-DQ. Today `Box()` → SPY0308 but `Box[int]()` runs, `list[B]()`/`Box[B]()` run, `isinstance(o, B)` → SPY0308 (@ c245ece62, f33b528f9), because P53 (ebf7c9cd0) kept the verdict unchanged with `CircularImportUsageValidator._genericReferenceNames` + fixture `imports/circular_import_type_argument`. When P43 lands: the skip is deleted, the fixture flips to `.error` (direction record: runs → SPY0308); cells spelling {bare call, explicit type args, type-argument position, `isinstance`} × declaring side {own module, importer}. Rejected: (b) type arguments in expression position as annotation-like (diverges from the written rule); (c) keep the spelling-dependent skip. Refusal, relaxable.

### 3.26 P36 — stdlib namespace layout (R-DD; lands alone; after P26)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** the Stdlib's C# layout follows the SAME module-as-namespace rule user code gets (P14b/P14c) — no generator special case, no stem/namespace map — and the python module name is the one authority the hand-written partial declares. **Tracking issue #2047. Ruling R-DD (option c, FULL — owner: "why not just c? … could c include moving the modules under a stdlib/ plus stdlib/__init__.spy directory+init as module to make it not magic?"):** (1) the spy sources move to `src/Sharpy.Stdlib/spy/stdlib/` with an empty `stdlib/__init__.spy`, and the 18 `_module` stems are renamed to their python names (`socket.spy`, `math.spy`, …) — the stem existed only to keep the module CLASS from colliding with `System.Math` under the old module-as-class layout; (2) with RootNamespace `Sharpy` the P14c rule alone emits `namespace Sharpy.Stdlib.Socket { [SharpyModule("socket")] static partial class SocketModule { … } [SharpyModuleType("socket", "error")] class Error … }`; (3) the python name: the compiler derives `stdlib.socket` from the path, so the regen script strips the `stdlib.` prefix — ONE documented rule replacing the 18-entry `MODULES` python-name map (the map shrinks to a file list) — and asserts agreement with the hand-written partial's `[SharpyModule]`; (4) the 65 hand-written modules move to `Sharpy.Stdlib.<Module>` in the same plan (one convention), which is where the 14 files using bare `Math.` need `global::System.Math` (measured: `String.`, `new Random(`, `Threading.`, `Xml.`, `Collections.`, bare `Socket`: 0 files); code that stays in `namespace Sharpy` (Core) never sees `Sharpy.Stdlib.Math` as `Math`. **One-way:** the whole Stdlib C# surface renames once for C# consumers (`Sharpy.Json.Loads` → `Sharpy.Stdlib.Json.Json.Loads` — the plan fixes the class-name spelling per module and records it in `module_system.md`); every fixture snapshot naming a stdlib type regenerates in one commit; `StdlibModuleNamespaceStampTests` and `check_spy_staleness.sh` are the gates. Rejected: (a) keep stems + permanent rewrite (two conventions, the doubled `SocketModuleModule`); (b) rename stems without the `Stdlib` segment (`Sharpy.Math` shadows `System.Math` for all 224 hand-written files); (c) half (spy-sourced 18 only). Rejected mechanism for the python name: teaching `ModuleRegistry` that `stdlib` is an implicit root (magic in the compiler). Lane tooling · probe. Before P39.

### 3.27 P39 — stdlib API collection-type rule (Core + Stdlib; R-DI; signatures change once; after P36)

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract (CLAUDE.md Design Anti-Patterns):** no public Core/Stdlib parameter or return type is a raw `System.Collections.*` collection — phase 1 containers, phase 2 (#2172) the protocol interfaces once they have Sharpy spellings. **Matrix:** type family {`List`, `Dictionary`, `HashSet`, `IList`, `ICollection`, `IDictionary`, `ISet`, `IReadOnlyList/Collection/Dictionary`, `KeyValuePair`} × position {parameter, return, property, field, event} × assembly {Core, Stdlib}. **Lane / reach:** tooling · probe. **Standing harness:** none; `OptionalParameterSpellingSweepTests` (52db74195) is the shape. **Generator: REQUIRED** — a reflective sweep over public Core/Stdlib signatures with a synthetic positive control and a commented exemption list (the protocol interfaces), red first; its first run IS the cell table (grep-only @ b1603d6a2: `IList<` 9, `Dictionary<` 8, `KeyValuePair<` 4, `IReadOnlyDictionary<` 4, `IReadOnlyList<` 2, `IReadOnlyCollection<` 2, `ICollection<` 2, `IDictionary<` 1, `HashSet<` 1 — against 223 `IEnumerable<`). **Class tracker:** #2164. **Snapshot/regen-sensitive:** stdlib docs, spy-sourced modules; every signature change is one-way for C# consumers.
- **#2164** (R-DI; FIRST) — tooling · probe. **Ruling R-DI ("a then b later"):** phase 1 = container types only; the protocol interfaces (`IEnumerable<T>` = `__iter__`, `IComparable<T>`, `IEquatable<T>`, `IFormattable`, `IDisposable`) are exempt by a commented list; `KeyValuePair<K,V>` → `tuple[K, V]`. Rejected as phase 1: (b) every `System.Collections.Generic` interface (223 `IEnumerable<T>` signatures would move before a Sharpy `Iterable[T]` exists).
- **#2172** (phase 2, LATER — filed by R-DI) — tooling · probe; removes the `IEnumerable<T>` exemption (and rules the other bounds per interface) after phase 1 drains; needs a Sharpy `Iterable[T]` Core type or a ruling that `Iterable[T]` keeps spelling to `IEnumerable<T>` — the plan author decides and records it. Not scheduled before phase 1's gate.

### 3.28 P28 — warm ≡ cold

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies; promoted in flight if the seam moves into `Semantic/`).

**Contract:** a warm (incremental, cache-served) build reports the same diagnostics and binds the same symbols as a cold build of the same sources AND references; the cache key covers every input; the codec round-trips every legal path; no symbol is re-bound by NAME after a restore. **Matrix:** change kind {source, referenced assembly (member removed / type changed), layout (new subpackage), none} × served unit {entry, dependency, cache-served with a warning} × observation {diagnostic set, binding, emitted C#}. **Lane / reach:** ice · program. **Standing harness:** `ColdWarmBuildDifferentialTests` (the generator; extend its axes). **Generator:** existing — the cells join it. **Class tracker:** #2114. After P25.
- **#2114** (FIRST) — ice · program; a changed referenced assembly does not invalidate the cache (warm SPY0908 CS0117 vs cold SPY0301); referenced-assembly identity (MVID/hash) joins the key. A red warm `@lru_cache` cell from #2165 (P25) lands here. **Call 24 cells (P54 verify prober @ 045ce6b58 = @ 486c9a88a; the compile SERVER is a warm instance):** (1) a referenced dll rebuilt IN PLACE between `run --server -r` requests → every later request on that path `Unexpected error: Invalid assembly public key (0x8013141E)`, exit 3, sticky per path (ice · program); (2) an ATOMIC replace (`mv`, new inode) → request 2 SPY0203 "no member greet2" (analysis stale) while the deployed binary is the NEW one (silent-wrong · program); (3) code-read: lazily loaded dependencies and the prior reference's actually-loaded copy are outside the `ReferenceLoadContext` key (`Discovery/ReferenceLoadContext.cs` ~:109 keys on path/length/mtime of each reference and of the PRIOR references by disk identity at key time). The served-unit axis gains "compile server across requests", the change-kind axis {rebuilt in place, replaced atomically, dependency rebuilt beside an unchanged reference}; the key covers the loaded copy's identity; P54's `InProcessIsolationSweepTests` + `CompileServerE2ETests` are the instruments to extend. The earlier "the compile-server probe stayed correct" note is refuted.
- **#2084** — message · probe; a refused warm build (SPY0526 before analysis) replays cached warnings the cold build never produces; either suppress replay under a pre-analysis refusal or keep analysing past it — the same answer both ways.
- **#2123** — silent-wrong · probe; the `user`/`generic` codecs write the origin path raw while decoders split on `, [ ( ! ->`; escape at the writer, a hostile-path codec cell.
- **#2050** — tooling · probe; retire the ten `udt.Symbol ?? Lookup(udt.Name)` fallbacks now that #2027's relink pass landed, one mutation per site proving it unreachable (a name-keyed lookup can bind the wrong symbol, #1325).

### 3.29 P24 — referenced-library route parity

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract:** a Sharpy library consumed as a DLL (`-r` / `<Reference>`) exposes the surface the source route has — union cases (fields, constructors, patterns), enum `.name`/`.value` (P23's table), package wrappers, merged file==class modules — and a standing harness builds a library project and consumes it from Sharpy AND from C#. **Matrix:** declaration {union, enum, class in package, merged module, nested type} × consumer {Sharpy `-r`, Sharpy `<Reference>`, C# `using` two module namespaces} × {cold, warm}. **Lane / reach:** false-refusal · program. **Standing harness:** `LibraryReferenceMatrixTests` (cells a–d, plan-0ca7b7 Phase 5; #2061 records its gap row in `gap-discovery-contracts.md`). **Generator:** existing harness — extend the declaration axis. **Class tracker:** #2115.
- **#2115** (FIRST) — false-refusal · program; a referenced union has no case fields/constructors (SPY0363/SPY0369/SPY0362/SPY0230); a referenced int enum's `.value`/`.name` is SPY0203; the same declarations across modules of ONE project work. A red "Stdlib assembly recorded only when imported" cell from #2165 (P25) lands here. **Call 28 cell (measured @ be4664e8f, `probes/ref/`):** a generic class in a referenced library (`sharpyc build lib.spy -t library`, then `-r ref/out/lib.dll`): `Box[int](5)` runs, `Box(6)` → SPY0237 "Cannot infer type arguments for 'Box'" — while the same class defined locally or imported from a sibling module infers (`Box(6)` prints 6): constructor type-argument inference is lost on the `-r` route; `LibraryReferenceMatrixTests` gains construction {explicit type args, inferred} × route. The band agent's "`Box[int](5)` via `-r` → SPY0342" did NOT reproduce (ledger line only).
- **#2061** — tooling · probe; the harness-gap row; measured by hand @ acd1d40a2: `from pkg.lib import lib_fn` → CS0234 (discovery drops the package wrapper — re-measure after P14b/P14c), merged module → SPY0300.
- **#2119** — tooling · probe; a Roslyn-compiled C# consumer with `using Lib.Thing; using Lib.Other;` calling both members classes and a sibling type, 0 diagnostics (the P14c X3 no-CS0104 claim is unmeasured).

### 3.30 P42 — reference-assembly loading & target surface (R-DN, R-DO; after P24)

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract:** a `-r` assembly contributes every type that can be described and never aborts the compile; a facade's forwarded types are reachable; everything the emitter writes on its own compiles on every target `Sharpy.Core` ships for. **Matrix:** assembly kind {ordinary, one unloadable type, unresolvable transitive reference, type-forwarding facade, facade whose target is unloadable} × use {nothing used, one loadable type used, an unloadable type named} × command {run, emit csharp, project}; and emitter-synthesized API × target {net10.0, netstandard2.1}. **Lane / reach:** ice · program. **Standing harness:** `InteropConformance` sweep (no hostile-assembly row). **Generator:** for #2191, the emitted fixture corpus compiled against netstandard2.1 references with an allowlist ratchet; the loading axis is a hand matrix over synthetic assemblies built in the test (five closed values). **Class tracker:** #2182. #2182 before #2183.
- **#2182** (R-DN; FIRST) — ice · program; one unloadable type aborts the compile for a file that uses nothing from the assembly (filer, sharpyc 0.21.0; the 2026-10-03 comment adds the "could not load file or assembly" variant for a missing transitive reference). **Ruling R-DN ("a now, b later"):** phase 1 stays on runtime reflection and tolerates — skip types that fail to load AND referenced assemblies that cannot be resolved, with a warning naming what was skipped; phase 2 (#2222) moves discovery to metadata-only loading. **Call 23 cell (P46 verify prober @ 10b43ea0b; pre-existing by code-read @ 27e261684; ice · program) + R-HI:** a `.dll` that exists but is not a managed assembly (`echo "not an assembly" > Fake.dll` beside a resolving `Greeter.dll`) is `SPY0908 internal error CS0009: PE image doesn't contain managed metadata`, exit 2, on `compile x.spyproj`, `project`, `compile main.spy -r`, `run -r` — the reference-state axis gains `not-an-assembly` beside `resolves`/`missing`/`unloadable`. Read @ 3f16f261e: `ModuleRegistry.LoadReference` records SPY0306 for three arms (`FileLoadException` `:123`, `BadImageFormatException` `:128`, access denied `:133`); P46's door helper `MissingReferenceDiagnostics()` (`:52`) filters to SPY0305 only because SPY0306 also fires on the long-lived-process "already loaded a same-named assembly from another path, then succeeds against the loaded copy" leak (fa7e8aa63 reds) — #2250's compile-server cell seen from the registry. **Ruling R-HI (a):** SPY0306 keeps ONE meaning, "a reference that cannot be loaded as given", and every door refuses every SPY0306; the already-loaded-elsewhere case stops being a diagnostic because P54 removes the leak (an isolated registry per compilation) — P54 is row 6, P42 row 30, so the order already holds and P42 re-measures the fa7e8aa63 run-route cells after P54. Rejected: (b) a new code for not-an-assembly with SPY0306 kept for the leak (two codes for one axis; a filtered diagnostic survives); (c) a SPY0306 sub-reason the helper keeps filtering (infrastructure for a case P54 deletes). A refusal on a cell that is an ICE today; two-way. **Call 24:** R-HI's P54 dependency is satisfied (32ea5428f: one SPY0306 meaning, every registry error reaches every door, the SPY0305-only filter deleted) — P42 re-measures the fa7e8aa63 run-route cells; the dependency cells posted here 2026-10-15 moved to #2322.
- **#2322** (R-HN; directly after #2182, before #2183; the second tracker — P54's F3 dependency arm) — missing-refusal (cell 1) · ice (cell 2) · silent-wrong (cell 3, code-read) · program; identical @ 486c9a88a and 045ce6b58: (1) `-r <a lone copy of Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.dll>` + `from microsoft.code_analysis.c_sharp import CSharpSyntaxTree` / `CSharpSyntaxTree.parse_text("class C {}")` compiles, then exit 134 `FileNotFoundException: Microsoft.CodeAnalysis, Version=5.9.0.0` at run — the dependency binds through the load context's Default fallback to sharpyc's own copy and the C# step references the whole host TPA (the issue names the mirrored sites `ReferenceLoadContext.Load` / `AssemblyVisibility.LoadDependencyOfOwn` and `AssemblyCompiler.GetMetadataReferences` — the plan measures); (2) `-r O.dll -r A.dll` in separate directories (O's signatures use A's `Greeter`) → SPY0909 `FileNotFoundException 'Tablegreeter'` + crash bundle during type checking, server and fresh process alike; `-r A -r O` runs; same directory runs; (3) a user dll sharing a FRAMEWORK simple name is silently replaced by `AssemblyVisibility.FindFrameworkAssembly`'s copy. **Ruling R-HN (a):** P42 as the second tracker — R-DN phase 1's "skipped with a warning" diagnostic is the door cells 1–2 land behind; the reference-state axis gains {dependency host-only, dependency order, framework-named user reference}. Acceptance: ONE resolution set `{reference directory} ∪ {module paths}` (∪ the framework for framework names) for BOTH the load context (no Default fallback for a non-framework name) and the C# metadata-reference set (framework + the registry's references + their resolved dependencies, never the host TPA); cell 1 → a compile-time diagnostic naming the unresolved dependency, cell 2 → the same regardless of `-r` order, no SPY0909, cell 3 → a diagnostic or a documented substitution; `HostAssemblyVisibilityE2ETests` gains the dependency arm (cell 1 the fixture; positive control: the dependency beside the reference compiles and runs); `ProcessWideResolutionScanTests` names instance ALC loads (`GetLoadContext(x).LoadFromAssemblyName/Path`) and `AssemblyLoadContext.All` as sites and scans `src/Sharpy.Lsp` + `src/Sharpy.Cli`; direction record over `ReferenceDeploymentMatrixTests`, `CompileServerE2ETests`, `HostAssemblyVisibilityE2ETests`; mutations (restore the Default fallback → cell 1 red; restore the whole-TPA set → cell 1 compiles again, red). Rejected: (b) pull P42 forward to the current band (P24's `LibraryReferenceMatrixTests` is P42's generator and R-DN's warning plumbing does not exist yet).
