### 3.31 P22c — string-literal grammar totality (R-CO; **R-HY: moved to row 6c‴ — directly after P63b, before P69; the #2339 chore lands first**)

**Tier:** light (no §12 letter applies).

**Contract:** ONE string-prefix/escape table drives the lexer's main loop, the hole tokenizer, the dedent arm and the editor grammar, and it is python's: prefixes `f t r b d dr df` plus `rf/fr/rb/br/tr/rt` in either order, case-insensitive; `{{`/`}}` inside a format spec; backslash escapes in spec text (non-raw); backslash-newline continuation inside non-raw literals; the PEP 822 dedent rules (`d`/`dr`/`df`) applied by one arm. **Matrix:** prefix × case × quote {single, triple} × content {plain, hole, spec with brace escape, spec with backslash escape, backslash-newline, CRLF} × dedent shape {shallow content line, close not on its own line, N=0 close at column 1, ordinary} (the last axis for the `d` family). **Lane / reach:** false-refusal + missing-refusal · program (#2044 cell 1 and #2337 run and print ALTERED text — silent-wrong in effect; #2048 tooling). **Fidelity-scope row:** "which programs run" — COMPARED against the spec; R-CO makes the spec say python's table. **Standing harness:** `TemplateSurfaceMatrixTests` + the P22 lexer cells + **P68's `FStringArmMatrixTests` (three `Skip=` cells cite #2337 — drain on fix), `LexerCorpusTwinTests` and the scanner-vs-lexer differential**; the editor grammar has none. **Generator:** not required (the prefix table IS the enumeration; the harness iterates it). **Class tracker:** #2045. Blast radius: FrontEndParity (semantic tokens read the lexer) + the TextMate grammar.
- **#2045** (R-CO; FIRST) — false-refusal · program; measured `rf"…"`/`F"…"` lex as Identifier + String (SPY0104). **Ruling R-CO:** python's full table, one place, the editor grammar generated from it.
- **#2044** — false-refusal · probe; `f"{x:{{}}>4}"` is SPY0021 at lex time; python parses the spec `{}>4` and raises `ValueError` at runtime — under R-CO the escape is read and the existing static twin (SPY0609) refuses the resulting spec (direction record: SPY0021 → SPY0609, same program refused, better channel). **Three P68 cells (comment 2026-10-21, measured @ 96d7a2792's binary):** `f"{x:{{>4}|"` with `x = 5` RUNS printing `{{{5|` (python 3.12: SyntaxError — missing-refusal · program); `f"{x:\"^5}"` → SPY0609 "Unknown format code" (the `\"` escape never decoded in spec text — #2129's cell seen from the lexer; false-refusal); a newline inside a single-quoted f-string's spec → SPY0609 `Unknown format code '\xa'` where python 3.12 drops the newline and 3.13+ refuses (message lane; pin to 3.12 at plan time per Rule 6). All three are rows of the content axis; no ruling needed.
- **#2337** (R-HX; filed by the P68 implementation round @ 96d7a2792) — missing-refusal · program. **Measured (the issue):** `df"""…"""` skips BOTH SPY0029 refusals the `d` path applies (a content line shallower than the close's indentation; a close not on its own line) and runs printing the altered text; N=0 (close at column 1) keeps both newlines where `d"""` drops them (silent-wrong). **Read (grep-only, the issue's):** the `d` path refuses in `Lexer.Literals.cs` (~:302, :339); the `df` path has its own dedent code in `Lexer.FStrings.cs` (`PrescanDedentedTripleFString`, `SkipDedentWhitespace`) with the newline rules gated on `DedentAmount > 0` and no SPY0029 site — the parallel-site meta-class, R-CO's "one table drives the lexer" applied to the dedent arm. Cells: prefix {`d`, `dr`, `df`, `dt` if lexed} × shape {shallow line, close not alone, N=0, ordinary} × quote; the three `Skip=` cells in `FStringArmMatrixTests` and the +3 skips in the 81093/0/7 gate drain on fix; direction record (ran printing altered text → SPY0029, same message as `d`). Rejected (R-HX): a new P73 residue group; all three P68 issues into P22c. Two-way placement; the refusal is relaxable.
- **#2129** — false-refusal · program; `f"{x:\"^5}"` passes the raw `\"^5` to the validator (SPY0609; python centers with fill `"`); backslash-newline inside a non-raw literal is SPY0004 (python: continuation). Cells × {single, triple} × {f, t, plain} × CRLF.
- **#2048** — tooling · probe; the VS Code TextMate grammar is a THIRD prefix table; generate its prefix alternation from the lexer's table (or a test comparing the two sets). Lands inside this group so the table has one consumer roster.

### 3.32 P15 — producer & protocol ring on hosts (R-BB, R-BT, R-CU, R-DX part 2)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** (a) presence, route, synthesis and every consumer read ONE producer decider (`IterableElementDecider`); (b) a loose `T | None` wrapper never changes HOW a protocol dispatches (`ProtocolReceiverMatrixTests`); (c) `self` inside a generic host is the host's own instantiation; (d) the super-dunder route reads the same dunder→member-shape table the class-member emitter uses; (e) `len(x)`, `bool(x)` and every truth position answer from ONE reflective `__len__`/`__bool__` fact (which includes a BCL `.Count` and a CLR implicit conversion to `bool`); (f) one hashability predicate at `hash()`, dict-key and set-element positions. Extends P10's matrices (`SynthesizedInterfaceVisibilityTests`, `ConstructedGenericHostDunderMatrixTests`, `IterableProducerAnnotationMatrixTests`, `SuperDunderHostMatrixTests`) and `TruthinessConformanceTests`. Before P16.
- **#1912** — `Box[T].me() -> Box[T]: return self` is SPY0260: `self` is typed as the open declaration. Type `self` as the self-instantiation in every position {return, store, argument, identity `is`, `isinstance`}; unblocks the generic own-iterator column (`__iter__ -> Box[T]: return self`) and the non-generator `__iter__` synthesis on generic hosts.
- **#1914** (+ #1930 cell 2) — a class synthesizing both `IEnumerable<T>` and `IReverseEnumerable<T>` makes `Builtins.Reversed` ambiguous (CS0121), generator and non-generator alike; two `.spy` fixtures under `generics/` are measured reds. Do NOT make `IReverseEnumerable<T>` extend `IEnumerable<T>`. The receiver's `CodeGenInfo` already records both synthesized interfaces; record the reverse route there and let the emitter cast to `IReverseEnumerable<T>` at the `reversed()` call. Cells: host {non-generic, generic} × `__iter__` {generator, non-generator} × `__reversed__` {generator, non-generator}.
- **#1915** (R-BB; + #1930 cell 1) — a non-generator `__iter__` whose DECLARED type is not a producer (`-> int: return 0`) reaches Roslyn (CS0117); one whose BODY returns a non-producer under a producer annotation reaches Roslyn (CS0029). Two seams: declaration shape → `DunderSignatureValidator` under its existing code; body mismatch → SPY0260 at the return slot, worded against the synthesized member's return type. The `for` route's presence check shares `SynthesisAnalyzer`'s producer gate.
- **#1916** — an unannotated generator's element stored into `b: bool` is CS0266 → SPY0908: the loop variable rides a permissive channel instead of carrying the decided `object` element. The loop variable's type is always the decider's answer, never Unknown. Cell: annotation {absent, `-> T`, `-> Iterator[T]`} × consumer {store into unrelated slot, `e: IEnumerable[object]`}. #2241 cell 3 (untyped async generator in an async comprehension → `void`) is its async twin.
- **#1918** — `v(1)` with `v: Bag | None` is SPY0230: `TryResolveCallableObject` handles `UserDefinedType`/`GenericType` only; the call route is the one protocol route that does not unwrap the loose wrapper. Route it through the shared receiver unwrap; add the `call` row to `ProtocolReceiverMatrixTests` for every receiver kind.
- **#1927** — `super().__len__()` emits `base.Count()` (CS1955), `super().__getitem__(i)` emits `base.GetItem` (CS0117). Rows `__len__`, `__getitem__`, `__setitem__`, `__contains__`, `__bool__`, `__iter__` added to `SuperDunderHostMatrixTests`; the super route emits `base.Count` / `base[i]` from the member-shape table.
- **#1931 (sum/any/all cell)** — `sum(Bag())` SPY0354, `any(Bag())` SPY0237 while `for`, `list()`, `sorted`, `max`, `enumerate` accept the same host: those three builtins do not consult the synthesized `IEnumerable[T]` row. Add them to the builtin-consumer roster P9 established.
- **#1994** — `ClassifyTruthiness` (`TypeChecker.Utilities.cs:157–228`) has no arm for a `BuiltinType` carrying a `ClrType`, so `range(0)` (`RangeIterator : ISized`) is SPY0220 at all nine truth positions while `len`/`bool` work. Cure: the classifier reads the CLR type from every `SemanticType` shape that carries one and routes it through `ClassifyClrTruthiness`; rows `range` + a reflective row per bridge-registered `BuiltinType` in `TruthinessConformanceTests` × nine positions × {empty, non-empty}. `SizedProtocolSweepTests` checks the interface list, not the verdict — this is the verdict guard.
- **#1982** (R-BT) — `ClassifyClrTruthiness` (`:240`) reads `IBoolConvertible`/`ISized` only, while `HasClrProtocol(__len__)` (`ProtocolMembership.cs:294–306`) also admits non-generic `ICollection`, `ICollection<T>`, `IReadOnlyCollection<T>` — so `len(q)`/`bool(q)` run and `if q:` is SPY0220 for `Queue[int]`. **Ruling R-BT:** a BCL collection with a `.Count` HAS `__len__` for truth: one reflective predicate shared by `len`, `bool` and the truth classifier; a new `TruthinessLowering.CountNotZero` (emit `.Count != 0` through the interface) for shapes without `ISized`; spec sentence in `dotnet_interop.md` (executed). Cells: {BCL `Queue<T>`, `HashSet<T>`, `List<T>` via `System.Collections.Generic`, `Stack<T>`} × nine positions × {empty, non-empty}; a CLR type with neither interface stays refused (positive control). Under P18b the collapsed interface shapes keep CLR identity and reach the same arm.
- **#2198** (R-DX part 2) — false-refusal · program; `if obj:` is SPY0220 "has no falsy case" and `bool(obj)` goes through `Builtins.Bool` (measured on `Version` @ 6b208066e). **Ruling R-DX part 2:** a CLR implicit conversion to `bool` (or `op_True`) joins the ONE reflective truthiness predicate (R-BT). The `iter(gen)` / `next(it, None)` cell of #2196 is a producer-ring cell of this group. (Part 1, #2221, landed as a chore and was then lifted for CLR types by R-ES.)
- **#2072** — ice · program; `b: bool = B(3).me().x` on `def me(self) -> Self` is SPY0908 CS0029 with a SILENT `b: bool` probe (untyped, not mistyped): a `SelfType` receiver must resolve members through its declaring type at every member-access seam — contract (c)'s self-instantiation carrier (#1912) is the same seam. Cells: `-> Self` result × {member read, call, index, store probe} × host {non-generic, generic}.
- **#2121** — false-refusal · program; `abs(complex(3, 4))` is SPY0354 (python `5.0`): `abs` does not consult the complex type's `__abs__` spelling (dunder table, rung 2); joins the builtin-consumer roster the `sum/any/all` cell extends.
- **#2081** (R-CU) — missing-refusal · program; `hash([1, 2])` and `hash(([1, 2], [3, 4]))` compile and print reference hashes; `builtin_functions.md` §Hashing Tuples says ERROR (block not error-asserted, so the sweep stays green); python3 `TypeError: unhashable type: 'list'` (verified). **Ruling R-CU (option a):** ONE hashability predicate (types without `__hash__`: `list`, `dict`, `set`, `bytearray`, and any tuple/record containing one) read at `hash()`, dict-key and set-element positions — static refusal (SPY02xx, Semantic overflow) + the runtime `TypeError` twin in `Builtins.Hash`; the spec block becomes error-asserted. Rejected: (b) runtime only (Type Safety over Python syntax; the spec already promises a refusal).
- **#2241** (tracker: a generator construct runs or is refused) — ice · program; measured @ 1e5d54c8f (`probes/m2241/`): `g.send(10)` AND `g.frobnicate(10)` on `g = gen()` are SPY0908 CS1061, while `g: Iterator[int] = gen()` is SPY0220 (`IEnumerable[int32]` to `Iterator[int32]`) — the receiver IS typed; its mapped-collection member surface admits any name (the plan locates the seam; P18's bridge family is the candidate, R-AP's "mapped collections route to silent Unknown"). Cell 4 (`await` in a genexpr's first iterator → SPY0524) joins the consumer axis. Cells 1–2's cure is SPY0203 with the `no-generator-send-throw` steer (a `docs/deviations.yaml` row). Observation, not a cell claim: `g: Iterator[int] = gen()` is refused while python's generator IS an Iterator — the plan decides whether `Iterator[T]` names the producer.

- **#2315** (call 24; tracker for the context-manager protocol on a CLR-route host) — silent-wrong · program as labelled (the ordinary spelling `from threading import Lock` is correct; the failing spelling is the CLR-namespace door `from sharpy import Lock`); measured by P54 Phase 3 @ 486c9a88a: `with lk:` under `from sharpy import Lock` → `using (lk) { … }` — `Enter()` never called, `Dispose` disposes the SemaphoreSlim at block exit, the lock NEVER held; under `from threading import Lock` → `__ctx.Enter(); try { … } finally { __ctx.Exit(); }` (python3 prints `True` / `False`); the spy-sourced stdlib itself carried the bug until ff610198f. Read grep-only @ f8c37f986: `TypeChecker.Statements.cs:2343` checks the dunder protocol through `GetTypeSymbolFromSemanticType`, which only the module-route symbol factory satisfies (`Stdlib/Threading/Lock.cs` declares `__enter__`/`__exit__` AND `Enter`/`Exit` AND `Dispose`); the CLR-namespace route has no such symbol and falls to `IsDisposableType`. Class: contract (a) — one protocol decider for every host factory (`__enter__`/`__exit__`, `__aenter__`/`__aexit__` join the ring), keyed on CLR identity (`ProtocolMembership.HasClrProtocol`, rung 3), never two symbol factories answering differently for one `System.Type`. Cells: Stdlib type with `__enter__`/`__exit__` × route {module import, `from sharpy import`, alias, module-qualified} × {`with`, `async with`} + a `-r` type declaring dunder-spelled methods; guard: sweep every Stdlib context-manager type across the routes, one lowering; mutation (key on the symbol kind again → red). P27 (R-CT, order 44) later refuses `from sharpy import <Stdlib type>` for module types, so the surviving member after P27 is the `-r`/CLR host; the lowering fix lands first. Ride-along: `src/Sharpy.Stdlib/spy/random_module.spy:4-5` documents this bug citing #2250 — retarget to #2315. Deployment twin (`FileNotFoundException: Sharpy.Stdlib` at run) is #2313 cell 4's sixth arm (P46b).

### 3.33 P16 — type-denoting reference totality

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** every spelling of a type reference {bare, type alias, import alias, nested chain, nested type under a constructed generic, CLR nested type via a collision generic, imported union case, type-parameter constraint} reaches the same verdict in every position {annotation, constructor, `isinstance`, value, static member, qualifier, match-pattern head, argument, enum member read `.name`/`.value`}; one denoted-type carrier the emitter can spell. Extends `TypeDenotingReceiverMatrixTests` (P4b Family C) with a spelling × position grid; #1909's DP allowlist row (`TypeChecker.Expressions.cs 1`) drains as acceptance. Before P16b and P17. Snapshot-sensitive (nested-type spelling `G<int>.Inner`).
- **#1941 + #1909** — ONE seam: both nested-type arms (`CheckMemberAccessCore`, `TryResolveTypeMemberAccess`) carry `!ReferenceEquals(memberAccess, _currentMemberAccessQualifier)` (11 sites across 4 files; the guard was added in 2249d4dcb for #1303 with no explanation), so a nested access that is itself a qualifier is untyped (`Outer.Inner.zzz` → CS0117 SPY0908), and `GenericReferenceResolver` keys on that cached Unknown to tell a type qualifier from an instance receiver. Cure: switch the resolver to a symbol-kind signal; add a materialized carrier for "nested type under a CONSTRUCTED generic" (a `GenericType` whose definition is the nested symbol with the OWNER's arguments, or a node-keyed fact in `MergeFrom`); then lift the guard in both arms and delete `ignoreQualifierGuard`. Measured trap: lifting the guard alone regresses `G[int].Inner.V` from 9 to CS0305.
- **#1894** — a nested type referenced inside its generic host drops the host's arguments (`Box.Inner`, CS0305), on the annotation route, the construction route (`return Outer[T].Inner()`) and the outside-qualified spelling (`s: Outer.Shape = Outer.Shape.Circle(2)`, no working spelling). Same carrier: the nested segment carries the owner's arguments — open `T` inside the host, closed outside.
- **#1886** — `List[int].Enumerator` (CLR nested type through a Sharpy-collection-mapped generic) is CS0119: the receiver denotes the wrapper, whose surface lacks the CLR nested type. Same carrier + the nested-CLR arm reaching through the wrapper's mapped CLR type.
- **#1926** — `case Environment.SpecialFolder.Desktop:` is SPY0203 while annotation, `isinstance`, store, argument and callee positions resolve the chain. Route `CheckMemberAccessPattern`'s dotted head through the same nested-CLR classifier; match-pattern column added.
- **#1925** (+ #1908 folded: the constructor cell) — `type A = P` then `A()` / `isinstance(o, A)` / `print(Id)` are CS0103 → SPY0908; `type A = G[int]` then `A()` is SPY0230. `CheckFunctionCallCore`'s construction arm gates on `symbol is TypeSymbol`, which a `TypeAliasSymbol` never satisfies. Cure at the alias-resolution seam; cells {ctor, isinstance, value, static member, annotation} × {non-generic, generic alias}.
- **#1971** — `RoslynEmitter.Patterns.cs:880–932` `BuildUnionCaseTypeSyntax`: the two generic arms emit a bare `GenericName(unionCSharpName)` and only the non-generic arm goes through `BuildNestedTypeName`; `case Tree<int>.Leaf` on the project route is CS0246 → SPY0908 (filer @ 44cf5dddc). Emit family of the #1683/#1802 class. Cure: every arm spells the union from the symbol-keyed qualified-name fact (Rule 2); cells generic × {single-file, project} × {class pattern, annotation control} in `ModuleMemberQualificationMatrixTests`; the family joins the #1802 emit-family completeness roster.
- ~~**#1928**~~ (MOVED to P21k §3.20i at call 35, R-JY — its cells are #2402's emission consumer; P16's import-alias × position arm is drained by P21k's generator) — was: `isinstance(v, Q)` with `from lib import P as Q` is CS0426 in project mode: the `isinstance` arm hands `BuildQualifiedTypeName`/`QualifyFromSymbol` the alias identifier, not the resolved symbol. Cells: import alias × {annotation, ctor, isinstance, static member, match pattern} × {single-file, project}.
- **#2038** — measured @ acd1d40a2: `Outer.C.RED.value` on a nested int enum → CS1061 → SPY0908; the top-level twin prints `1`. The emitter's `IsEnumInstance` (`RoslynEmitter.Operators.cs:1289`) reads the object's semantic type, which the `_currentMemberAccessQualifier` guard leaves Unknown for a nested chain, so the enum arm at `RoslynEmitter.Expressions.Access.cs:1710` is skipped. Same seam as #1941/#1909. Cells `.value/.name` × {int, string} enum × {top-level, nested in class, nested in struct, imported}; direction record SPY0908 → `1`.
- **#2082** — ice · program; `Outer.Inner.Blue` (a nested enum member through its declaring chain) is Unknown: `b: bool =` probe silent, `.name` → CS1061 behind SPY0908; an annotated `x: Outer.Inner` twin prints `Blue`. The qualifier class beside #2038 — every enum fact the checker records (P23's table) is missing for this receiver; cells enum {top-level, nested in class, nested in struct, imported nested} × read {bare, `.name`, `.value`, probe, f-string with spec}.
- **#2202** — ice · program; `isinstance(ev, BattleEvent.LevelUp)` for an imported union emits the case type without its nesting (CS0234); the `match` route is correct. Position `isinstance` × origin imported.
- **#2212** (tracker) — ice · program; constraint types of an uncalled generic are never resolved (`[T: Nope]` is CS0246 behind SPY0908, `` [T: `notnull`] `` compiles as the C# keyword), and a violated constraint at a call is CS0315/CS0310 behind SPY0908. Both halves stay on this tracker: position "type-parameter constraint" × {function, class, method} × {unknown, escaped soft keyword, valid}, and call-site × {notnull, `new()`, base class, interface}. **Call 26 cells (P63 round, @ f8c37f986 / @ 27bf064bb, built CLI):** the call-site half gains callee kind {module function → SPY0237 (the roster's one YInjected spelling), method `Util().biggest(Plain(), Plain())` → SPY0908 CS0311, class instantiation `SortedBox[Plain](Plain())` → CS0311 (P63's `KnownRedRefusal("#2212")` pin, drains when the refusal lands)} × argument kind {user class → SPY0237, builtin `biggest(1, 2)` → SPY0908 CS0315}; explicit instantiation of a constrained FUNCTION → SPY0237 (control). Two probe-reach notes: constraint handling is keyed on the type NAME `Comparable` (read only — a rung-4 name-keyed alias, the #1933 meta-class; the cure reads the interface's members or CLR identity, never the spelling); SPY0237 is declared `CannotInferGenericType` but its message states a constraint violation (message · probe; the plan names the code by what it reports).
- **#2397** (call 34; R-JW — by class; no ruling) — ice · program; `run` @ 9aae96977 (the issue) and @ the 04f9138b3 tree (measured at call 34, `probes/n2.spy`): `class C:` / `class N: tag: int = 3` / `def m(self) -> int: return N().tag` → SPY0908 CS1955 "Non-invocable member 'C.N' cannot be used like a method" — the bare nested type as a CALLEE is emitted as a member invocation, not `new N()`; the field-initializer position the same; the parameter default SPY0401 (correct). **Control (measured):** `C.N()` and `n: C.N = C.N()` inside the method print `3 3` — the Python-habit spelling runs (Python's class scope is invisible in methods, so Python programmers write `C.N()`); the failing spelling is C#'s. **Contract (this group's):** a nested type's bare name inside its enclosing body denotes the TYPE in every position, the callee position included — the callee-kind axis gains `nested type, bare, from the enclosing body` beside #1894's generic-host cells (one carrier). The cells agree across positions, so the axis is callee kind, not P21h's read position (the issue's own axis change).
- **#2401** (call 34; R-JW — by class; no ruling) — ice · program (+ one LSP tooling cell); `run` @ 13747484f = 9aae96977 (the issue): `type S = IShape` + `property IShape.area` + `property S.area` → SPY0908 CS0246 (one key or an alias resolution expected); `interface IBox[T]: property v: T` + `class B(IBox[int]): property IBox[int].v: int = 4` → SPY0908 CS0305 (expected `4`); an interface declared in another module (`class C(m.IShape)` / `from m import IShape` + `property IShape.area`) → CS0246; ``property IShape.`area` `` → CS0539; `CompletionHandler.cs:207` lists `IShape.area` as a member `area` (tooling). **Contract (R-JH extended into this group's):** the explicit-interface qualifier is a TYPE REFERENCE position — it resolves through alias, generic instantiation, import and escaped spelling like every other position in this matrix, and the member key `(Name, ExplicitInterface)` compares the RESOLVED interface, never its spelling; the position axis gains `explicit-interface member qualifier`. The emitted-name collision cells are #2399 (the call-34 band).

Snapshot-sensitive (`.expected.cs` for typed generics).

### 3.35 P17 — call-route parity: sentinel, constructor ring, shadowing, coercion forms (R-AZ, R-AY, R-BV)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Phase 1 (R-AZ): the error-sentinel type — #1946.** Today every refusal site returns `SemanticType.Unknown` after reporting (e.g. `Calls.cs:418`), and Unknown doubles as the deliberately-permissive channel; a second independent check on the same node reports again. Add `SemanticType.Error`: accepted by every check, never reported on, never assignable-tracked by the DP allowlist. Refusal sites return it; guard = a totality scan that every reporting site returning a `SemanticType` returns Error (anchored to `AddError` call sites), plus a positive control. Side effect: Unknown then means only "deliberately permissive", which makes the DP drain honest. #2243 folded here: `any(undefined)` is SPY0200 + SPY0237, `sum(undefined)` is SPY0200 + SPY0354 (@ b4d268dc1); the 36 two-diagnostic cells in `AsyncGeneratorExpressionRefusalMatrixTests` drop to one as acceptance. Every later P17/P17b/P17c/P17d cell asserts exactly one diagnostic per site. Reuses SPY0326/SPY0320/SPY0336/SPY0203; new: the `dict()` argument refusal (Semantic overflow) and SPY0612 (R-BV).

Some : None` (flags enums: decide at plan time, python `Color(5)` raises for undefined); `__explicit__` called, a throw → `None`. ### 3.36 P17b — params-tail binding parity (after P17's sentinel)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** a Sharpy argument list binds a CLR/Core `params T[]` tail exactly as C#'s EXPANDED form would — element-wise, never as the array itself — and the tail's own parameter name is not keyword-bindable from Sharpy source; every Core/Stdlib `params` callee, not `str.format`. **Matrix:** argument shape {lone `None`, lone typed value, sole `T[]` actual, spread `*xs` of `list[T]`, spread + trailing positional, keyword naming the tail, keyword with did-you-mean near the tail's name} × callee {`str.format`, `Builtins.Print`-class, a Stdlib `params` method, a user CLR `params` import} × T {`object`, `string`, `int`}. **Standing harness:** none — the plan adds a reflective roster of `params` callees (Core + Stdlib public surface) and the cells above; direction records for the ICE cells. **Seams:** emitter `RoslynEmitter.Expressions.Access.Calls.cs:702/915` (`IsVariadic`), `:938/:1015` (spread); checker `FindKeywordParameter` (`Calls.cs:3866`) admits the tail's name; `ReportUnknownKeywordArgument`'s did-you-mean ranks it. #2078 was a duplicate of #1985; #2106 (`print(*xs)`) folded into #1990.
- **#1985** — `"[{}]".format(None)` → C# normal-form binding (`args == null`) → `IndexError` at runtime (python `[None]`); two-argument call works. Cure: emit the expanded form for a lone `null`/`None` from the recorded binding, never by inspecting the AST in the emitter.
- **#1990** — `"{:=5}".format(*ns)` with `ns: list[int]` passes the `int[]` as ONE `object` element (prints `System.Int32[]`; python `'    1'`); spread + trailing arg → CS1929 `Concat` → SPY0908. Cure: element-wise boxing into the tail's element type from the recorded parameter type.
- **#1992** — `"{}".format(args=1)` binds the keyword to the `params` parameter and prints `1` (python `IndexError`); `"{}".format(arg=1)` → SPY0234 whose did-you-mean names `args` — steering INTO the hole beside the R-BE steer. Cure: the ONE keyword binder never binds a `params` tail by name; the did-you-mean excludes it; sweep every `params` method.
- **#2011** — measured @ acd1d40a2: `print(tp.strings)` (a CLR `string[]`) prints `  b`, `str(tp.strings)` prints `['', ' b']`. A `T[]` sole argument binds `Print(params object?[])` in C#'s NORMAL form (array covariance beats `Print(object?)`), so no Core overload can cure it — the emitter boxes the argument from the RECORDED argument type when a `params` tail receives a single array-typed actual. Spelling: the array's own `str()`; python's tuple spelling of `Template.strings` is a P11d surface deviation recorded in `template_strings.md`. Cells: callee {`print`, `str.format`, a Stdlib `params` method} × actual {`string[]`, `int[]`, `object[]`, `list[T]` control} × arity {sole, with a trailing positional}.

### 3.37 P17c — pipe-route parity (after P17's sentinel; independent of P17b)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** `x |> m.f(y)` ≡ `m.f(x, y)` — the pipe route resolves the callee symbol, records it, binds arguments and keywords, runs every symbol-carried check (static format-spec twin, deprecation, keyword names) and spells the callee EXACTLY as the direct call does, for every callee spelling {bare identifier, import alias, module-qualified `m.f`, aliased module `b.f`, `_` placeholder partial}. **Matrix:** callee spelling × check {SPY0609 twin, deprecation warning, unknown keyword SPY0234 with steer, keyword-already-positional, emitted C# byte-equal to the direct call}. **Standing harness:** none dedicated — the plan adds `PipeRouteParityTests` (the direct call is the oracle) and extends the format-spec differential's route axis with the pipe route. **Seams:** `CheckPipeForwardWithFunctionCall` (`TypeChecker.Expressions.Operators.cs:990`): symbol resolved only for `Identifier` callees (`:1019`), keywords bound by verbatim `p.Name == kwarg.Name` (`:1098`) with its own messages; `GeneratePipeCallTarget` (`RoslynEmitter.Expressions.Operators.cs:389`) spells an `Identifier` through `NameCasing.ResolveMethod`, never from the resolved symbol. Snapshot-sensitive (pipe fixtures' C#).
- **#1999** — `s |> builtins.format("=5")` / `s |> b.format("=5")` fall to the `FunctionType` fallback: no `RecordResolvedCallTarget`, no SPY0609 (runtime ValueError). Second cell: the `_` placeholder partial types the placeholder from the formal (`object?`), so the operand kind is Unknown and the twin skips. Cure: resolve a qualified callee's symbol the way the direct qualified route does and call `RecordResolvedCallTarget(call, symbol, implicitFirstArgument: binOp.Left)`; the placeholder lambda's parameter takes the piped operand's static type. The `fstrings.md:290` "runtime-only" sentence is rewritten when this lands (#1997 stays runtime-only, R-BW). **Call-30 cell (P63b, comment 6381508391):** `super().__init__(a)` in a derived `__init__` has NO recorded call target (50/50 generated samples @ 04f0c72a5; the route types the callee through `ValidateSuperMemberAccess`); `RoslynEmitter.ClassMembers.Constructors` falls back to named arguments, so no wrong program — a recording-totality cell for P17 phase 1's guard (every resolved call records its target or carries a documented exemption; route axis gains `super().<m>()`), kept on this issue because the contract is the same. Lane message · probe.
- **#2000** — `s |> format(format_spec=">5")` is a false SPY0234 while `format(s, format_spec=">5")` runs: the pipe route binds keywords by verbatim CLR name. Cure: bind through `FindKeywordParameter`/`ClassifyKeywordBinding` with the piped value counted as the first positional; report through `ReportUnknownKeywordArgument`/`ReportKeywordAlreadyPositional`; delete the route's private messages.
- **#2001** — `from math import sqrt as sq; x |> sq` emits `Sq` (CS0103 → SPY0908); `x |> math.sqrt()` runs. Cure: `GeneratePipeCallTarget` reads `GetCallTarget(call)` and spells through the same qualification path `GenerateCall` uses (#1683/#1802 emit family). Cells: alias × {builtin, module function, user function} × {`x |> f`, `x |> f()`, `x |> f(y)`}.

### 3.38 P17d — argument-binding totality: synthesized constructors, struct parameterless ctor, explicit-type-arg CLR ctor, ref/out modifiers (after P17's sentinel; independent of P17b/P17c)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** the checker binds every call against EXACTLY the signature and modifier set Roslyn will bind against — every constructor the emitter synthesizes has a semantic signature, a struct's constructor set always contains the parameterless one, the explicit-type-argument route and the inferred route run the same overload check, and an argument's call-site modifier is compared with its parameter's. SPY0908-as-a-net meta-class throughout. **Matrix:** host {class without `__init__`, dataclass, struct, struct with explicit `__init__`} × route {inferred, explicit `T[...]`, module-qualified} × argument shape {unknown keyword, extra positional, missing required, keyword naming a defaulted auto-property, wrong type, empty literal, `None` for a value-type formal} × modifier {none, `ref`, `out`, `in`} × callee kind {user function, method, constructor, CLR method, CLR generic constructor, overloaded generic method group with explicit type arguments on `self`}. **Standing harness:** none — the plan adds `ConstructorSignatureTotalityTests` (a reflective roster: every type the emitter synthesizes a constructor for has a checker-visible signature with the same parameter list) and extends `StoreConversionMatrixTests`/the call-argument matrix with the modifier axis. New carrier: a semantic signature for every synthesized constructor (the emitter's roster consumes it).
- **#2021** — measured @ acd1d40a2 on ALL THREE hosts: `H(bogus=5)` (struct), `C(bogus=1)` (class), `D(x=4)` (dataclass `property x = 3`) → CS1739 → SPY0908. `GenerateStructAutoConstructors` (`Constructors.cs:381`) synthesizes in the emitter with no checker twin; `DataclassSynthesis.SynthesizeMembers` (`:145`) does not cover the defaulted-auto-property parameter. Cure: ONE signature-synthesis seam in Semantic that the emitter's roster reads (Rule 2), so a bad keyword/arity/type is SPY02xx. Also decides whether a defaulted auto-property is a constructor parameter on the dataclass host, as #1938 ruled for structs. Posted cell (from #2161): `dict[str, int].fromkeys(["a"], None)` → CS1503 (None admitted for a value-type formal while `od.get("x", None)` is SPY0229). **Call 26 — the `@dataclass` POSITIONAL arm (P63 verify class-cure auditor @ 27bf064bb):** `Config(1, 7)` → CS1503, `Config(1)` → CS7036, `Config(1, "a", 3)` → CS1729, all clean at `emit diagnostics` (expected SPY0220 / SPY0224 / SPY0224); the host axis {struct, class, dataclass} × argument form {keyword, positional} × defect {wrong type, too few, too many, unknown keyword}. Instrument note: `DecoratorPropertyTests.DataclassDecorator_GeneratesCorrectMembers` stays green under a generator mutation constructing a `str` field from an `int` until this class lands. **Call-30 cells (P63b verify @ b955e592f):** the IMPLICIT constructor of a class with no `__init__` (`class Factory(IProducer[bool])`, `Factory(0)`) → CS1729 behind SPY0908, expected SPY0224 — GenVariance 30/30, MatchExpressionExhaustive 21/21 and DecoratedFunction 18/30 samples construct such classes; `@dataclass` one argument short (`Config(1, "val_1")`) → CS7036, pinned `KnownRedRefusal("#2021")` by `ErrorInjector.InjectDataclassArity` (166e4e181) beside the CS1503 pin — both drain when the synthesized-constructor signature lands; the host axis gains {implicit constructor}.
- **#2023** — measured SPY0224 for `Vector()` on a struct with an explicit two-parameter `__init__`; `structs.md` promises the implicit parameterless constructor (Axiom 1). Contract: a struct's constructor set as the checker sees it always includes the parameterless zero-initializing one; declared `__init__` overloads are ADDED. Change the checker, not the spec (Rule 7); the `structs.md::ad235aa4cc` allowlist row drains as acceptance.
- **#2042** — measured `list[int]([1], 2)` → CS1729 → SPY0908 while `list([1], 2)` is SPY0224. `Calls.cs:1170–1215`: the explicit-type-argument arm validates against `__init__` overloads through its own `initMethods.Count == 1` / `> 1` branches instead of the bare route's resolver. Cure at the shared constructor-resolution seam; cells {list, dict, set, deque, user generic class, interop generic} × {too many positional, unknown keyword, missing required} × {explicit, inferred}. Posted cell (from #2197): `TaskCompletionSource[bool]()` unresolvable. **Call 24 cell (P54 final verifier @ 486c9a88a and 7ae6ed761; ice · program):** `from system.threading.channels import Channel` + `Channel.create_unbounded[int]()` → `run` SPY0908 CS0021 "Cannot apply indexing with [] to an expression of type 'method group'" — the `callee[T](...)` route reaches the emitter as an index expression on a method group; callee-kind value "CLR generic static method with explicit type arguments" beside #2184's row. **Call 28 cell (measured @ be4664e8f, `probes/arity*.spy`):** `from system import Tuple; Tuple[int, int](1, 2)` → SPY0346 "'Tuple' is abstract and has no constructor" — explicit-type-argument construction binds the non-generic static `Tuple` class, not the arity-2 generic of the same CLR name (the arity group ``Tuple`2``); control: a local `class Box[T]` constructs as `Box[int](5)` and `Box(6)`. P17d's explicit-type-arg CLR ctor arm gains the arity-group axis {non-generic sibling present, one arity, several arities}.
- **#2146** — false-refusal · program; `S({})` / `S([])` for a struct with a `dict`/`list` field is SPY0227 while the same literal at a `def` or class `__init__` argument is slot-directed (R-W): the synthesized signature #2021 gives the constructor makes the argument a slot like any other. Cells: empty literal × callee kind {def, class `__init__`, struct synthesized ctor, dataclass ctor, keyword argument}. **Sibling cell posted (@ db8e05bf1):** `max([])` — an untyped empty literal at a GENERIC builtin's argument reaches the emitter as an open `T` → SPY0908 CS0246 (`max(ys)` with `ys: list[int] = []` raises python's `ValueError` correctly); expected SPY0227/SPY0237 at the argument. P37's generator carries this cell as an allowlisted row this group drains.
- **#2145** — false-refusal · program; `def process(value: int)` + `def process(value: ref int)` → SPY0204 (modifiers are not signature identity; spec and C# say they are); `SwapFunc = (ref int, ref int) -> None` → SPY0104 (function types cannot spell modifiers). Joins #2025's modifier axis: modifier {none, ref, out, in} × position {overload pair, function type, delegate assignment, call site}; drains the `parameter_modifiers.md` overloading row and the `error SPY0104` marker.
- **#2025** — measured `inc(x)` for `c: ref int` → CS1620 → SPY0908. `CheckModifiedArgument` (`TypeChecker.Expressions.cs:243`) types a modified argument; nothing compares an argument's `Modifier` with the bound parameter's modifier. Cure at the one argument-binding seam: {ref, out, in} parameter × {argument without modifier, wrong modifier, `in` with none = allowed control} × callee {module function, method, constructor, CLR method}; the `structs.md` `ref T` example's allowlist row drains.
- **#2184** — false-refusal · program; measured @ 6b208066e (`probes/d2184.spy`): `Array.empty[int]()` runs, `Activator.create_instance[int]()` is SPY0336 — the failing axis is likely "overloaded method group + explicit type arguments", not the receiver; the plan measures receiver {`self`, type, instance} × overload set {generic only, generic + non-generic}.

- **#2317** (call 24; tracker) — ice (cell 1) + false-refusal (cell 2) · program; measured by the P54 final verifier @ 486c9a88a and 7ae6ed761: (1) `from system.security.cryptography import SHA256` + `SHA256.hash_data(bytes([1, 2, 3]))` → `run` SPY0908 CS1503 "cannot convert from 'Sharpy.Bytes' to 'byte[]'"; (2) `from system.text.json import JsonSerializer` + `JsonSerializer.serialize(5)` → SPY0224 "expects 2 to 4 arguments but got 1" while the CLR has `Serialize<TValue>(TValue, JsonSerializerOptions? = null)`. Contract (P17d's verbatim): bind every CLR call against exactly the signature Roslyn binds — a Sharpy Core value converts where C# accepts it, a generic method's type arguments are inferred from the arguments, else a check-time refusal; never SPY0908, never a false SPY0224. Axes join P17d's matrix on callee kind "CLR method": argument shape {`bytes`, `list`, `dict`, `str`, `tuple` against a CLR formal {array, `IEnumerable<T>`, `ReadOnlySpan<T>`, `IDictionary`}}, callee kind {generic method with inferable type arguments × optional parameters}; the `InteropConformance` corpus gains the rows (no hand matrix). Cell 1 reads R-BH when P18b lands: a `bytes` in a `byte[]` slot is an explicit conversion unless `Sharpy.Bytes` declares an implicit operator C# would use, in which case the checker discovers `op_Implicit` by reflection (rung 2) — the plan measures which. Cell 2 is the generic-METHOD inference twin of #1951's constructor inference (§5 finding 6).

### 3.38b P59 — `ref` bindings (Parser + Semantic + CodeGen; R-FO; experimental flag; generator FIRST; after P21c and P17d)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (#2268's):** a `ref` binding aliases a storage location — `ref r = <lvalue>` and `for ref v in <seq>` — and every write through it lands in the element; C#'s ref local / ref iteration variable (`ref var r = ref arr[1]`, `foreach (ref var v in span)`), which Sharpy has no spelling for; closes the struct-element mutation gap (`structs.md` "Copy-on-Assignment": mutating a struct element of an array in a loop mutates a copy today). Borrowed from Mojo's `ref` bindings (manual @ 33cd4694b). **Measured @ 88345108f (issue):** `ref r = xs[1]` → SPY0103 at the name (`ref` is already a keyword — the parameter-modifier spelling); `for ref v in xs:` → SPY0104. **Matrix:** binding form {`ref NAME = <lvalue>`, `for ref NAME in <seq>`, `-> ref T` return (optional in v1), `ref in` readonly (optional)} × storage {array element, struct field `self.pos`, nested struct field, `Span[T]`/`ReadOnlySpan[T]` element, `ref`/`out` parameter, Sharpy `list[T]` element (REFUSED, R-FO), `dict[K,V]` value (REFUSED — no stable location), property (REFUSED — not a location), local} × element type {primitive, struct (the motivating case; the required positive control), class reference, `T?`, generic `T`} × use {read, compound write, plain write (writes THROUGH, never rebinds; a second `ref r =` is a redeclaration error), pass as `ref` argument, lambda capture (REFUSED — C# ref locals cannot be captured), escape via return/yield/async (REFUSED — ref safety), `let`/`const` interplay (a third binding kind, not a `let` modifier)}. **Lane / reach:** design (no lane; Python `SyntaxError`). **Standing harness:** none. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** (4 axes) — enumerate form × storage × element × use, run each accepted cell and print the container after the write (a struct cell distinguishes copy from alias; a primitive cell does not), refused cells carry a Sharpy diagnostic and never SPY0908/CS8xxx (Roslyn's ref-safety errors are refused by Sharpy's own validator FIRST); allowlist ratchet. Lowering: `ref var r = ref <lvalue>` / `foreach (ref var v in <span>)`, a binding-kind fact on the symbol's `CodeGenInfo` (Rule 2 pattern a). **Class tracker:** #2268. Feature-lifecycle: experimental flag (Parser scope), SPY0331 twins. Spec: `variable_declaration.md`, `for_statement.md`, `parameter_modifiers.md` (if `-> ref T` is in). Snapshot-neutral (gated).
- **#2268** (R-FO; the group) — design. **Ruling R-FO (option a):** v1 REFUSES `ref` over a Sharpy `list[T]` element (and `for ref` over a list) with a steer naming the array/span alternative — a span over a growable list is unsafe under `append`/`pop` in the loop body and the compiler cannot refuse that statically; v1 scope = arrays, struct fields, spans, ref/out parameters, locals. Rejected: (b) a net10-only `AsSpan` on `Sharpy.List<T>` behind `#if NET10_0_OR_GREATER` (bifurcates the Core surface by TFM and ships the aliasing hazard). Two-way: (b) stacks later as an additive relaxation.
- **Call 31 note (from #1952's P21f triage @ 980d58bd0, posted on #2268):** `events.md::511f0d2735` (`def set_property[T](self, field: ref[T], value: T, name: str)`, ~line 355) and `::f6007db8e3` (`def increment(self: ref[Counter])`, ~373) spell a by-ref PARAMETER as `ref[T]` — SPY0202 "Generic type 'ref' not found". Design input for this group: the spec already carries a `ref[T]` TYPE spelling for parameters beside `parameter_modifiers.md`'s `ref`/`out` modifier; P59 decides whether `ref[T]` is retired for the modifier spelling or becomes the binding's type form, and rewrites both blocks; their allowlist rows stay on #1952 (P20) until then.

### 3.39 P18 — CLR bridge totality & module resolution (R-BO, R-BU, R-BA, R-CV, R-DV; own measured gate)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (R-BO):** the CLR type bridge (`Discovery/ClrTypeBridge.cs`, the one component translating reflected .NET types ↔ `SemanticType`) never answers `object` for a CLR shape it can name, and the checker's chosen overload is the one Roslyn binds; module resolution follows python's `sys.path` precedence. **Phases:** 1 = #1921 (by-ref unwrap; the refusal carrier for genuinely inexpressible shapes) → 2 = #1975 (retire the `object` fallback; #1973 is its `ITuple` cell; #2197) → 3 = #1969 + #1987 (one reverse-mangled member surface; #2195 #2196) → 4 = #1898 + #2113 + #2175 (resolution order). Every phase turns silent acceptance or SPY0908 into refusals, so P18 lands on its own measured gate with direction records. **Measured @ 048a15574 (q20–q32):** the `object` fallback bites only on REFLECTED signatures — `ArrayList().add_range(42)` (formal `ICollection`) → SPY0908 CS1503; `b: bool = Environment.get_environment_variables()` (return `IDictionary`) silent → SPY0908 CS0029 — while the annotation route is already honest (`def f(x: IFormattable)` + `f("s")` → SPY0220). Positive controls that run today and must keep running: `xs.add_range([1, 2])`, `env.count`, `env["HOME"]`, `for k in env.keys`, `o: object = env`, `env: IDictionary = …`, `"abc/".trim_end(Path.directory_separator_char)`, `Convert.to_int32(finfo.attributes)`. Writer-visible change of phase 2: SPY0908 cells become SPY0220 at the Sharpy line; nothing that runs stops running. Unrecognized GENERICS already keep CLR identity (`:831`, #1640); non-generic classes/structs/enums/delegates already get a real symbol (`:333–:355`). `dotnet_interop.md` gains "a CLR interface value is that interface; a CLR value never reads as `object` unless declared so"; `module_resolution.md` states the order with executed examples. New warning code in SPY0450–0489 (R-BA shadow, default-ON) + explanations entry. Before P48 and P18b.
- **#1921** (phase 1) — `ClrTypeBridge.MapParameterType` maps `V&` (by-ref) to `object`, so `d.try_get_value("a", out v: str)` on `dict[str, int]` is CS1503 → SPY0908 and `out v: auto` binds `object`; the CLR call route strips by-ref locally (`Calls.Clr.cs`, `GetElementType()`). Unwrap once at the bridge; the by-ref-ness travels as a modifier; delete the local workaround. **Blast radius is real** (silently-accepted mismatches become refusals): matrix receiver kind {Sharpy builtin, CLR type, generic instance, extension} × {`ref`, `out`, `in`} × argument shape {typed, `auto`, mismatched, missing `out`}; totality over CLR type shapes {by-ref, pointer, generic parameter, array, nested generic}.
- **#1975** (R-BO tracking; phase 2) — the class arm at `ClrTypeBridge.cs:333` is guarded by `IsClass || IsValueType` with no stated reason while `GetOrCreateClrDefinitionSymbol` (`:1004`) already knows `def.IsInterface ? TypeKind.Interface`; every non-generic interface falls to `return new UnmappedClrType` (`:356`), whose record (`SemanticType.cs:1422`) is object-like in both directions. Consumers to touch: `TypeSyntaxMapper.cs:69`, `Calls.Clr.cs:251`/`:814` (the permissive exemptions), `CoercionPossibility.cs:163`, `Access.cs:550`/`:791`, `SymbolSerializer.cs:1461` + cache version bump, 7 `IsObjectLike` readers; one NEW piece: a `Type.IsAssignableFrom` arm in assignability for interface satisfaction by CLR-typed arguments. Matrix and acceptance on the issue.
- **#1973** (cell of #1975) — `MapParameterType` (`:230`) → `UnmappedClrType` for `ITuple`, so the overload index selects `Bool(ITuple)` for `bool(C())` while Roslyn binds `Bool(object?)` (commit 5f98ccbc3's inverted positive control). Closes as the `ITuple` cell; its overload-selection parity sweep (checker's member ≡ Roslyn `GetSymbolInfo`) is #1975's class guard; the TupleBool guard's inverted control becomes a true positive control.
- **#2197** (phase 2) — false-refusal · program; an imported CLR `Task[T]` is not the identity `await` recognises (SPY0274, and SPY0260 "`Task[bool]` … expecting `Task[bool]`"). The `TaskCompletionSource[bool]()` cell is posted on #2042 (P17d).
- **#1969** (phase 3) — `SharpyReceiverSpelling.cs:134` `ReverseMangleNamesAMethod` enumerates `Public | Instance | FlattenHierarchy` methods, while the resolver (gate caller at `TypeChecker.Expressions.Access.cs:1303`) admits explicit-interface (`IndexOf`) and underlying-only (`LastIndexOf`, `AddRange`, `Find`) names the emitter cannot bind (filer @ 44cf5dddc: `f = xs.index_of` prints a delegate past the R-AP gate; `xs.last_index_of` CS1061 → SPY0908 in value AND callee position; 17 public reverse-mangled names correctly SPY0336, 16 absent names correctly SPY0203). Cure: ONE reverse-mangled member surface = what the emitter can bind (public members + the explicit-interface map spelled through the interface cast the emitter already writes), shared by the resolver, the R-AP gate and the SPY0203 arm; cells member kind {public, explicit-interface, underlying-only, absent} × position {value, typed store, callee} in `SharpyReceiverSpellingMatrixTests`. P18b then removes the collapsed-collection arm from this surface (R-BH).
- **#1987** (R-BU; phase 3) — `str.format("{:>5}", "ab")` reaches `TryResolveTypeMemberAccess`'s static arm (`Access.cs:2538`), which on `str` is `System.String.Format` → runtime `FormatException`. Measured @ d17ddb956: `C.m(c)` on a user class is SPY0290 while `str.upper("ab")` is SPY0908 (CS1501), because `TryRefuseInstanceMemberViaTypeName` lets CLR-backed symbols through. Collision set = python `str` methods ∩ `System.String` statics = {`format`, `join`}. **Ruling R-BU (b):** extend SPY0290 to CLR-backed builtin types; when a same-named CLR static exists, the steer names the escape ("`format` is an instance method of `str`; call it on a value, or write `` str.`Format` `` for the CLR static"). Cells: python instance method × {no CLR static, same-named CLR static} × spelling {bare → SPY0290, backtick → the CLR static runs} × type {str, int, float, list, dict}; direction records `str.format` runs-wrong → refuses, `str.upper` SPY0908 → SPY0290. Rejected: python unbound-method semantics; keep.
- **#2195 · #2196** (phase 3) — false-refusal · program; special-name accessor methods (`set_active`) take part in member lookup and collide with mangled names; generic interface member hiding is ignored (`IEnumerable[T].get_enumerator()` ambiguous, `IEnumerator[T].current` typed `object`). The `iter()`/`next()` cell of #2196 is P15's.
- **#1898** (R-BA; phase 4) — `ImportResolver.TryResolveNetModule` asks `IsNetNamespace` FIRST (`ImportResolver.ModuleLoading.cs:908`); that answers true unconditionally for `system`, `system.*`, `sharpy`, and for any other name by a PascalCase probe against every loaded assembly (`ModuleRegistry.MapModuleToNamespace`) — so the collision set is open-ended. **Ruling R-BA (option c):** search-path (user) modules resolve BEFORE CLR namespaces (python's precedence); the CLR namespace stays reachable through the existing backtick spelling; a warning at the module's definition site in project mode (and at the import site under `run`) says "module 'system' shadows CLR namespace 'System'; use `` `System` `` to reach the namespace". Cells: name {`system`, `system.io`, `sharpy`, a loaded-assembly namespace, a NuGet namespace, a non-colliding name} × mode {run, project} × spelling {bare, backtick}.
- **#2113** (R-CV; phase 4) — silent-wrong · program; `src/math.spy` defining `sqrt` loses to the stdlib (`from math import sqrt` prints `2.0`, python `42.0`) with no diagnostic; a local `thing.spy` vs a referenced library's `thing` binds by ROUTE (`project` 100, `run` 7, `run -r` 100); and `IsNetNamespace` scans `AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies()`, so any assembly loaded into a long-lived process shadows a local module. **Ruling R-CV (option a):** the R-BA order extended — a search-path (local) module resolves BEFORE stdlib modules, referenced libraries and CLR namespaces alike; the R-BA default-on warning names the shadow at the definition site; ONLY the compilation's referenced assemblies count — the loaded-assembly scan is deleted. Rejected: (b) refuse a local module that shadows a stdlib/referenced module. Cells: local name {stdlib module, referenced-library module, CLR namespace, loaded-but-unreferenced namespace, non-colliding} × mode {run, project, `run -r`, LSP} × spelling {bare, backtick}.
- **#2175** (R-DV; phase 4) — missing-refusal · program. **Ruling R-DV:** every dotted CLR name that no import binds is refused with the import steer (`from system.text import StringBuilder`); the fallback-namespace prefix probe goes with the loaded-assembly scan (R-CV). Rejected: accept the fully qualified spelling from referenced assemblies (a second way to name a type). Relaxable.

- **Call 22 cell on #2175 (phase 4; 2026-10-14):** the lazy CLR-type lookup `ModuleRegistry.TryResolveNetType` (`Type.GetType` / `assembly.GetType`) applies no public-surface filter where `GetNamespaceTypes` keeps `IsPublic` only — `` x = `System`.`SR` `` → SPY0908 CS0122, `import system; system.SR` → SPY0908 CS0234 (expected SPY0203; @ 8be4576fd). Lane ice · probe. Same R-DV cure: one lookup path, one public filter.

### 3.40 P48 — platform nullability (R-EH, R-DW write half, R-EN; lands alone; generator FIRST; directly after P18)

**Tier:** full (b: `Generator: REQUIRED FIRST`; c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (R-EH):** a reference type written `T` is non-null statically and at runtime; a CLR reference from an assembly without nullable annotations has UNKNOWN nullability — usable as `T`, testable for None, `maybe`-wrappable — and is null-checked when it is stored into a slot declared `T`; a None test on a declared non-null `T` is refused; on write, oblivious members and parameters accept `None` (R-DW write half). **Matrix:** origin {Sharpy-constructed, annotated-CLR non-null, annotated-CLR nullable, oblivious-CLR, oblivious generic argument / array element / out parameter / event argument} × slot {annotated local, inferred local, parameter, field, return, collection element, tuple element, closure capture} × use {member access, `is None`, `is not None`, `maybe`, truthiness, pass to declared-`T` parameter, pass to oblivious parameter, property set, field set, argument with `None`}. **Lane / reach:** missing-refusal · program. **Standing harness:** none. **Generator: REQUIRED FIRST** — origin × slot × use over a synthetic assembly pair (one annotated, one oblivious) built in the test, each running cell executed with a null and a non-null source, allowlist ratchet. **Class tracker:** #2230. The plan's first task answers the issue's four open questions (whether the bridge distinguishes oblivious from annotated, propagation through generics and comprehensions, helper call vs inline throw, the diagnostic identity of the always-false None test). Interaction: R-EJ makes `is None` a reference null check everywhere, and R-ES allows `== None` / `!= None` on every .NET reference type running the type's `==`; R-EH's wider point lives here. Posted cell (#2245): a chained `a == None == s` between two different CLR types keeps a bare null for the middle `None` — derived from the #2238 lowering @ 4fb77ebae, NOT measured; the plan measures before designing a per-link comparand fact.
- **#2230** (R-EH; FIRST) — missing-refusal · program; design tracker, lands alone (new refusals; a one-way runtime null check at the declared-`T` boundary).
- **#2199** (R-DW write half; moved from P18 phase 4 by R-EN (a)) — false-refusal · program; first phase of this group, so the annotation-state fact is built once. **Ruling R-DW:** members and parameters from assemblies without nullable annotations accept `None` on write and read as `T` (C#'s rule for oblivious types) — the READ half is superseded by R-EH. Matrix: annotation state {annotated non-null, annotated nullable, oblivious} × position {property set, field set, argument, return read} × value {`None`, value}. Rejected (R-EN): (b) #2199 stays in P18 and P48 goes late; (c) P48 before P25.

### 3.41 P18b — explicit CLR-collection bridging (R-BH; tracking #1965, symptom #1960; lands alone, own gate)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract today (violated):** a CLR interface sequence/mapping shape is collapsed onto the Sharpy builtin at discovery (`ClrTypeBridge.cs:481` IList/ICollection, `:593` IReadOnlyList/IReadOnlyCollection, `:650` IEnumerable; `MapsToSharpyBuiltin` `:424` — eleven collapse arms at `:464–656`, "mapped to list for simplicity"), so the checker admits the whole Pythonic surface in every position, but the emitted C# carries it only where #1251's `RecordSequenceMaterialization` (`TypeChecker.Utilities.cs:1753`, consumed at `RoslynEmitter.Expressions.cs:53`) inserts `new Sharpy.List<T>(src)` — declaration, reassignment, `return` to a Sharpy slot, argument to a Sharpy-native parameter; bare reads and CLR-mapped parameters are deliberately NOT converted, which is exactly where the divergence lives. **Measured @ 44cf5dddc:** store `xs = e.inner_exceptions` copies; direct chain `e.inner_exceptions.index(...)` CS1929→SPY0908; `def push(xs: IList[int]): xs.append(9)` emits `xs.Append(9)` (LINQ, discarded) and `push(l); print(l.count)` prints `1` (silent-wrong); `ConcurrentDictionary[str,int]().keys.count("a")` CS1929→SPY0908. **Ruling R-BH — option (d):** CLR interface shapes keep CLR identity (reverse-mangled surface; `len`/`for`/`in` via the interfaces; indexer where present); the Sharpy collection is reached ONLY by the explicit `list(x)`/`dict(x)`/`set(x)`; #1251's implicit slot materialization is RETIRED — a CLR sequence in a `list[T]` slot is a type mismatch with the `list(...)` steer. See #1965 for the acceptance list (bridge-arm totality guard, position × shape × member matrix, deletion of the `SequenceMaterialization` fact and emitter arm incl. `MergeFrom`, snapshot/fixture/spy-stdlib regen, `dotnet_interop.md` rule with executed examples). Direction records for every ICE cell above. #1960 closes as the symptom cell; #1966 parks the future bridge-keyword door. **Consequences for other groups:** the R-AP gates (`IsExplicitlyMappedCollectionReceiver`, `IsSharpyBuiltinSpellingReceiver`) lose the collapsed-collection arm; `CoreGenericTypeMapping` guard rows move; #1951 axis (iii) is re-measured after P18b; every spy-sourced stdlib module that relied on the collapse is rewritten with `list(...)` and regenerated (P21b regenerates once more later). New steers, no new codes: SPY0203 with the CLR-spelling steer and SPY0220 with the `list(...)` steer.

Narrowing facts inside a conditional expression inside a clause apply exactly as in statement position (python3 verified).

Direction record: runs → SPY0202 with the import steer; the plan counts fixture hits first. Rejected: keep.

New code: #2167's explicit-interface access refusal (Semantic range) + explanations entry; R-CW reuses SPY0283.
- **#2074** (FIRST) — `FindMemberSymbol` searches the receiver's OWN methods/fields only (no base walk, no properties/events), then falls back to the underscore convention — `@public def _m` via a subclass and `@public property _p` are SPY0283 while emitted public; the reverse (`@protected m` from outside → CS0122 behind SPY0908) is predicted, measure first.

- **#2131** (R-CW) — silent-wrong · probe; `from lib import __w` → SPY0283 but `lib.__w` runs; an escaped module variable loses its escape on export; an aliased escaped type keeps it; root: NameResolver records module functions `Public` while ModuleLoader/emitter use `AccessLevelConventions.FromName`. **Ruling R-CW (option a):** `_x`/`__x` module variables are module-private exactly like module functions — SPY0283 on BOTH routes, the escape carried on the exported symbol (cells 2–3 read P26's spelling authority). Rejected: public module variables.
- **#2167** (spec-ruled, no decision) — ice · program; `property IFoo.x: int = 3` on `class C(IFoo)` + `c.x` on a class-typed value → SPY0908 CS1061 (filer @ 4e0369898). `properties_inheritance.md` § Explicit Interface Implementation already rules it: `holder.value` is an ERROR, `secret.value` through an `ISecret`-typed value works. Rule 7: refuse semantically with a steer ("`x` is implemented explicitly for `IFoo`; access it through an `IFoo`-typed value"). The receiver axis gains explicit-interface member × {class-typed, derived-typed, interface-typed, `self` inside the class} × member kind {auto-property, function-style property, method}; the spec block gets its error marker and an executed interface-typed twin.

The `@abstract`-class cell joins #2067's abstract-pair cells. **Call 35 (2026-10-31):** #2409 (the module-host SPY0407 collision — a duplicate of this issue's cell 4, filed by the P21i sibling-cell prober @ 2988266a1) folded here and CLOSED; **R-KB (a):** its cure is a band chore riding P75's gate (§3.0) — the module host joins the one `ValidateBackingFieldCollision` arm, host {class, struct, module} × form {mixed, observer, lone set half}, mutation = drop the module arm; the cell drains from this tracker with the chore; cells 1–3, 5–6 and the 2026-10-31 rows (write-only read / read-only write on the module host, `property init` on a static host, abstract property overridden without `@override` → SPY0248) stay P30's.
- **Call-34 cells recorded here (the P21h rounds, 2026-10-29; placements unchanged):** **#2111** — `interface I: @static property get a: int` + `class C(I): property get a: int = 3` runs `3` with the interface's `@static` silently dropped (an instance abstract property; silent-wrong · program) — the member-kind twin of its CS0120 method cell: an interface member's `@static` reaches the emitted C# (`static abstract`, which needs a `@static` implementer) or is refused, for every member kind; `@static` interface member storage → CS0120; a `@static` interface property default reading a `@static` sibling → SPY0200 with no member description. **#2147** — `@static property get a: int = 1` + `C().a` → SPY0908 CS0176 (the property-kind sibling of its const/static-field cells). **#2369** — `--enable-feature property_observers`: an auto half carrying `before_set` beside a function-style getter prints `7` with the observer silently dropped (`GenerateGroupedProperty` honours `Observers` for a one-half group only — the comment's reading); silent-wrong · probe; stays #2369's. **#2383** — `interface IA: property v: int` + `class C(IA): pass` → SPY0908 CS0535 (a SPY0325-shaped refusal expected; `InterfaceImplementationValidator` checks methods only — the comment's reading); the two-interface `property IA.v` + `IB.v` unimplemented twin the same; R-JN stands (no stopgap).

- **#2417** (call 36) — ice · program; `run` @ df3eed258 (the issue; same at the 0.22.1 proxy): `class Host: @static property sp: int = 4`; `b: str = Host.sp` → SPY0908 CS0029 (expected SPY0220); `print(Host.sp.bit_length())` → CS1061 `BitLength` (expected `3`); `(lambda: Host.sp)()` → CS0149 (expected `4`); `sum(x for x in [Host.sp])` → an extra SPY0354 `Iterator[<?>]`; every property shape (auto, computed, no-default), in `main` and in a static initializer alike; the same read of a `@static` FIELD, a `const` or a `@static` method IS typed. **Contract (this group's):** every read of a member through any receiver is TYPED by the one member-shape table — receiver {type name} × member kind {`@static` property} is the untyped cell beside #2147's receiver {instance} cells (CS0176); the oracle is a deliberately wrong annotation (`b: str = Host.sp` → SPY0220 — silence at the checker means untyped; `print(x)` is never a type probe). Joins the generator's receiver × member-kind grid.
- **#2434** (call 38; R-KT a) — ice · program; the issue's eight cells @ its sha (P77a's verify round): `struct H:` / `x: int = 3` / `def r(self) -> int:` / `f: () -> int = lambda: self.x` / `return f()` → SPY0908 CS1673 (a lambda stored / immediately invoked / passed, a nested def, an accessor get, an `__init__` lambda, a comprehension inside a lambda, a method call through `self` — all CS1673; `class` and `@dataclass` twins run). **Contract (one more host cell of P30's table):** host {struct} × closure body × `self` use is decided in Semantic — refused as C# refuses it (a lambda capturing `this` in a struct would copy a value), never SPY0908. **Ruling R-KT (a):** REFUSE with a named code and the steer "copy `self` to a local first" (what C# programmers write: `var s = this; () => s.x`). Rejected: (b) lower by copying `self` to a local before the closure (every read runs; a store through the copy, or a mutating method call, silently writes the copy — one-way once written); (c) (b) for read-only bodies, (a) for bodies that store or call (a call cannot be proven non-mutating). (a) → (b)/(c) is a relaxation later. Not probed (the plan's matrix): `@dataclass struct`, generic structs, a body that STORES through `self`.
- **#2435** (call 38; R-KU a) — silent-wrong + ice · program; `run` @ the issue's sha (P77a's verify round): `class H: @static x: int = 2` / `def f(self): self.x = 5` / `h.f(); print(H.x); print(h.x)` → `5 5` (python3 `2 5` — an instance attribute); `H.x = 5` on a derived `H(B)` writes `B.x` (python: a new attribute on `H`); `self.sp = 9` on a `@static property` → CS0176 behind SPY0908; a CONST store `H.K = 9` / `self.K = 5` / through an alias / a derived type / a struct `self` → CS0131/CS0176 behind SPY0908. **Contract (the STORE side of #2147's receiver matrix):** a store names ONE member through every receiver spelling and reaches one verdict — a const store is SPY0225 in every spelling (the spec: a constant cannot be assigned; N7: spelling never changes the verdict — no ruling needed); a `@static` field or property store through `self` or a derived type is REFUSED because python's meaning cannot be matched. **Ruling R-KU (a):** refuse with the steer "write `H.x = …`" — the one rule the principle sets: never a different meaning. Rejected: (b) write through to the shared field (today's behaviour for fields, extended to properties — a C# habit giving a Python-valid program a different meaning; one-way for programs that rely on it). Matrix: member kind {const, `@static` field, `@static` property} × receiver {type name, `self`, alias, derived type, struct `self`} × host {class, struct, dataclass} × body {method, `@static` method, nested def, lambda}; the attribute-STORE target axis joins P77b's generator (#2433).

### 3.47 P31 — CLR-base subclassing totality (R-EG)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract (the #2138 ruling):** the override, abstractness, base-constructor, member-lookup and interface-implementation rules read a CLR base exactly as they read a Sharpy base — an unimplemented abstract CLR member, a CLR base without a parameterless constructor, a module-qualified annotation, a nullable-spelled interface parameter, or a member declared in the class body that shares a source spelling with an inherited CLR member is decided in Semantic with python-spelled member names, never CS0534/CS7036/CS0535 behind SPY0908 and never a silent rebinding. **Matrix:** base kind {Sharpy abstract, CLR abstract class, CLR generic abstract (`Comparer[int]`), CLR interface (`IFormattable`), CLR class with required ctor args, `System.Object`} × member state {implemented, missing, arity mismatch with `@override`, nullable-parameter spelling, same source spelling as an inherited member} × member kind {field, property, method} × access {`self.x` read, write, call} × route {`from m import T`, `import m as alias` + `alias.T`, namespace route}. **Lane / reach:** ice · program (#2200 silent-wrong · program). **Standing harness:** `ClrBaseOverrideRouteMatrixTests` (extend the base-kind and member-state axes). **Generator:** existing harness — the cells join it. **Class tracker:** #2143. `dotnet_interop.md` states the CLR-base rules.
- **#2200** (FIRST) — silent-wrong · program; `self.audio` binds the inherited CLR `audio` instead of the class's own field `audio` (emitted `Audio`). Contract: a member declared in the class body wins over an inherited CLR member of the same source spelling.
- **#2143** — `class Rev(scg.Comparer[int])` with a wrong-arity `@override compare` → CS0534; the checker refuses a Sharpy abstract base's missing member but not a CLR one; report once, python spelling (`compare(x: int, y: int) -> int`). **Call 28 cell (R-IE; measured @ be4664e8f):** `class MyList(list[int]): pass` → SPY0325 "Class 'MyList' does not implement interface method 'IList.count_of'"; `class MyDict(dict[str, int]): pass` → SPY0325 'IDeepCopyable.deep_copy' (python3: `class MyList(list)` runs) — the base is `sealed` (`Partial.List/List.cs:10`, grep-only) and the checker reads a sealed CLR base's interface implementations as missing: the same CLR-base reading as the CS0534 cell. The honest refusal is a sealed-base diagnostic with the composition/protocol steer (a new Semantic code + `DiagnosticExplanations`); the base-kind axis gains {sealed Core collection, sealed CLR class}; the ledger row `no-subclassing-core-collections` lands NOW (§3.0, R-IE a; rejected (b) unseal — one-way Core surface; (c) leave SPY0325). **Call-30 cells (P63b verify @ b955e592f):** interface IMPLEMENTATION arms on a Sharpy interface — `class Factory(IProducer[int])` implementing `def produce(self) -> str` → CS0738 (return type; generic and non-generic interface alike), a parameter type differing from `IShape.method_0(self, arg0: str, arg1: str)` → CS0535, all clean at analysis; the base-kind axis gains {Sharpy interface, Sharpy generic interface} × mismatch {return, parameter}; the roster rows for decorators.md's CS0508/CS0534 pins name `InjectOverrideSignatureMismatch` / `InjectMissingAbstractImplementation` (6b258e58c) and flip when the semantic refusal lands.
- **#2142** — `class E(sharpy.JSONEncoder)` via the namespace route → CS7036 (implicit `base()` against a required `indent`); refuse ("subclass must call `super().__init__(…)`") or bridge optional parameters as defaults — the plan decides by measuring the `from json import JSONEncoder` route.
- **#2141** — false-refusal · program; SPY0445 compares annotation TEXT (`sg.GeneratorContext` ≠ `GeneratorContext`); signature validators compare resolved types (the bd2dd0a01 `__format__` fix is the same class). #2117 cell 2 (`IFormattable` implementation with `str?` parameters → CS0535) is posted here.
- **#2229** (R-EG; after #2200) — false-refusal · program; `C().get_type()` is SPY0203 while `o: object = C(); o.get_type()` runs (filer @ 6b208066e). **Ruling R-EG (a):** `System.Object` members bind on a Sharpy class instance, scheduled WITH #2200's group (a user member named like an inherited CLR member joins its matrix), not as a chore; until then the refusal stands. Rejected: (b) bind only `get_type` (name-keyed); (c) keep the refusal and document the `to object` cast. One-way once landed.

### 3.48 P32 — typed-load doors (R-DE; after P11g)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** the typed json/yaml/toml doors share ONE naming rule (source ↔ member) and ONE message channel (python-spelled type names, python-shaped errors), and accept the same target shapes. **Matrix:** door {json, yaml, toml} × target {dataclass, `list[dataclass]`, nested, field `x_`/`_x` affixed names} × failure {missing field, wrong type, malformed} × observation {`Err` text, bound value}. **Lane / reach:** message · program. **Fidelity-scope row:** type names in messages (COMPARED, R-CF); message text beyond the name is DEVIATION (R-CI) — the door's cells assert the type name and the python exception TYPE, not the library prose. **Standing harness:** `TypeNameMessageSourceScan` (cannot see BCL-runtime text), the typed-load tests in Stdlib.Tests. **Generator:** not required (three doors × four targets; the plan enumerates by hand). **Class tracker:** #2136.
- **#2099** — `TypedLoadContract.cs:212` spells `{target.Name}` (`AppCfg` for `app_cfg`); every Stdlib/Core message naming a user type goes through `PyFormat.PyTypeName`; widen the scan to `System.Type` receivers with a positive control on this site.
- **#2135** — the doors wrap `System.Text.Json`/YamlDotNet `ex.Message` verbatim (``Sharpy.List`1[J1.AppCfg]``, `System.String`); translate the library exception (type + path) into a python-named message.
- **#2136** (R-DE) — silent-wrong · probe; three copies of the reverse member→snake rule (`TypedLoadContract.ToSnakeCase`, STJ `SnakeCaseLower`, YamlDotNet `UnderscoredNamingConvention`), none the inverse of R-CG's forward rule; affixed names (`x_`, `_x`) are the cells. **Ruling R-DE (option b):** `[SharpyName]` stamped on members whose mangled name differs from the source name (R-CF's attribute-when-different rule at member level, the same stamp R-DG puts on methods), read by ONE custom naming policy shared by the three doors; a missing-field message reports the stamped name. Rejected: (a) a Core `ToSnakeCase` inverse (lossy for affixed names).
- **#2117** — ice · program; cell 1 stays here: `toml.loads[Cfg]` for a `@dataclass` → CS0310 (`new()` constraint) while json's typed load handles the same shape. Cells 2 and 3 are posted on #2143 (P31) and #2051 (P30).

### 3.48b P62 — executable grammar: generation + differential parse (Parser tests + a grammar artifact; R-FX; the sentence generator IS the generator; after P59 and P21c, directly before P45)

**Tier:** light (e: owner's call (R-JR: a keyword codemod / a tests-only instrument with no door) — the §12 letters would say full).

**Contract (#2287's):** one grammar artifact is executable and the others are derived from or checked against it — the CsCheck generators' syntactic coverage comes from the grammar, and a production the parser accepts that the grammar lacks (or vice versa) is a red, not a stale file. **Matrix:** construct (every production of the grammar written FROM the parser — Sharpy-only syntax included: type parameters, `let`/`const`, `match` reification, backtick identifiers, d-strings, bracket decorators, `as!`/`as?`, `maybe`/`try` expressions, `|>`, `.X` (P58), `ref` (P59)) × check {a grammar-generated sentence (bounded depth) parses with the hand-written parser; a reference parser generated from the grammar vs the hand-written parser on the generated corpus + `TestFixtures/*.spy` + the spec blocks — biconditional acceptance (the contract `CPythonDifferentialParseTests` already asserts against CPython), skeleton agreement where both accept}. **Lane / reach:** tooling · probe. **Standing harness:** `CPythonDifferentialParseTests` (#1037; the Python-shared subset only), `ParserRoundTripPropertyTests` / `AmbiguityHotspotPropertyTests` (self-consistency only). **Generator:** the grammar-driven sentence generator; an allowlist of known grammar/parser divergences citing issues, drain on fix; the fixtures and spec blocks are the positive control (a too-narrow grammar is red on day one). Toolchain (lark or ANTLR4) chosen by the plan by which also drives generation. **Class tracker:** #2287. Step 1 (delete `grammar.ebnf.txt`) is the chore in §3.0.
- **#2287** (R-FX; the group) — tooling · probe. **Ruling R-FX (option a):** delete the stale EBNF now as a chore (read by nothing @ be1c16fcf; the two test-harness design docs that call it "hints only" drop the input or point at P62; the memory records the deletion); P62 writes the executable grammar FROM the parser. Rejected: (b) measure first by translating the stale file to lark (most of a fix's cost, for a number the owner's 2026-07-02 ruling already predicts); (c) fix in place as ISO EBNF (no executing toolchain exists for it; a second hand model that drifts). Two-way: git keeps the file; P62 may mine its production names.
- **Backtick-identifier content cell (P68 verify round, measured @ 96d7a2792's binary; posted on #2287 at call 27):** the matrix row "backtick identifiers" has no content validation today — `` `` `` (empty) lexes as Identifier `''` and `run` dies on `Debug.Assert` at `Parser/Ast/Expression.cs:506` (exit -6; ice · probe); `` `a b` `` and `` `1x` `` reach Roslyn (SPY0599). The grammar's backtick production states what content is legal (C# identifier syntax after the escape); the lexer refuses the rest with one code, and the sentence generator's backtick arm draws from that production. No ruling; a cell of the construct axis.
- **One-seam rows from P63 (close-out 2026-10-21, out of P63's universe by the plan's own scope):** `Parser/AmbiguityHotspotPropertyTests.cs` and `ParserRoundTripPropertyTests.cs` use `passed > total / 2`; `CodeGen/ILCompiles|RoslynParseable|NormalizationIdempotence` skip on `!result.Success`; the Metamorphic lanes skip on `!rN.Success` — different generators, so they are P62's (the grammar-driven generator replaces their syntactic coverage; their oracles migrate to the P63 seam or a sink then).

### 3.49 P45 — loop lowering (R-DU; lands alone; last)

**Tier:** full (c: the seam is under `Semantic/`/`CodeGen/` or the group lands alone).

**Contract:** `for x in range(a, b[, literal step])` costs a counted loop. **Lane / reach:** tooling · program. **Generator:** a differential run of the old and new lowering over the fixture corpus (stdout identical); the snapshot churn is counted and regenerated in one commit. **Class tracker:** #2194.
- **#2194** (R-DU) — measured @ 6b208066e (`probes/d9.spy`): `foreach (var __loopVar_0 in global::Sharpy.Builtins.Range(4))` and `global::Sharpy.ArrayHelpers.SetItem(xs, i, i)`. **Ruling R-DU:** lower the `range` header to a C# `for` (bounds evaluated once; the loop variable is a per-iteration copy). Direct array indexing stays a cell on the issue until there is a proof rule for a non-negative index. Rejected: (b) direct array indexing as well; (c) park.
