## 4. Rulings (owner; posted as issue comments — do NOT re-ask)

Every ruling of this round, R-AW…R-KL, in sequence. "Rejected" names the alternatives that must not be re-proposed; "two-way"/"one-way" records the door type. Rulings on DONE groups stay here because they bind later work (e.g. R-CF/R-CG/R-CH shape P11g; R-BZ/R-CR shape P26). A struck ruling is SUPERSEDED — the strike-through names the ruling that replaced it; the text is kept verbatim.

| Ruling | Issue | Content |
|---|---|---|
| R-AW | #1871 | **Refuse by name** a member whose PascalCase equals its enclosing type: new SPY052x sibling of SPY0523, steer = rename or backtick-escape. Rejected: deterministic verbatim fallback (leaks into the C# interop surface; b→a later is breaking, a→b is not). |
| R-AX | #1932 | **Phase a now:** refuse `dir == module filename` by name in project mode with a rename steer + the subdirectory axis on the qualification matrix. **Phase c next (P14b):** emit package directories as C# namespaces (fully qualified names unchanged; `using Simple.Lib` becomes possible). Rejected: collision-only wrapper rename (renames the wrapper for every sibling module when a colliding file is added). |
| R-AY | #1892 | **Option c:** `as?` lowers per `CoercionPossibility.Verdict` kind with a python-matching runtime test — enum→backing always `Some`; backing→enum `IsDefined ? Some : None`; `__explicit__` throw → `None`. Totality guard over `Verdict` anchored to literals: every Possible kind has both lowerings. Rejected: unconditional `Some((T)v)` (silently wrong for undefined enum values; a→c later changes runtime values); refuse `as?` for statically-total kinds (option b, would have been two-way but was not chosen). |
| R-AZ | #1931 | **Error-sentinel type** (`SemanticType.Error`): accepted by every check, never reported on, distinct from the deliberately-permissive Unknown; every refusal site returns it; totality guard over reporting sites. P17 phase 1. Rejected: first-error-per-span suppression (hides distinct errors; allowed only as an interim net, not needed if the sentinel lands first); per-pair reordering (cell fix). |
| R-BA | #1898 | **User modules first + explicit escape + warning:** search-path modules resolve before CLR namespaces; the namespace stays reachable via the backtick spelling; a default-on warning (SPY045x) at the definition site names the shadow and the escape. Rejected: refuse-by-name (the CLR namespace probe is open-ended — adding a NuGet reference would newly refuse existing user modules); silent Python precedence with no warning. |
| R-BB | #1915 | **Two seams, two codes:** a non-producer `__iter__`/`__reversed__` DECLARATION is refused by `DunderSignatureValidator` under its existing code; a BODY returning a non-producer under a producer annotation is SPY0260 at the return slot, worded against the synthesized member's return type. Rejected: SPY0260 for the declaration shape (no return-slot mismatch exists); SPY0320 at the `for` site (misleading). |
| R-BC | #1911 | Stdlib docs render a module-defined type as `StatResult` on its own module's page and `os.StatResult` cross-module. |
| R-BD | #1939 | **Order: prelude → fragment → per-bucket issues.** (1) per-file `spec-sweep: prelude` block prepended to every fenced block, drain the rows that now compile; (2) bulk `fragment` markers for the genuinely partial residue; (3) one tracking issue per remaining first-error bucket. The gate's glob (`build_tools/*allowlist*.txt`) and a comment-roster regex widen immediately. Rejected as FIRST step: bulk fragment marking (forecloses the drain — a fragment never re-executes). |
| R-BE | #1955 | **Keep refusing, add the steer** (2026-09-21, option a): `str.format` keyword fields stay SPY0234 with "use an f-string or `format_map`". Rejected: accepting keywords via a name-keyed lowering into a synthesized dict + a new Core `Format(kwargs, args)` overload (rung-4 magic no CLR surface can express; SRP-0002 already rejected dynamic kwargs; users would depend on it — one-way). Two-way door: can be relaxed later. |
| R-BF | #1950 | **As designed** (2026-09-21, option a): a bound method / CLR-identity method group in value position is a TYPED delegate (measured SPY0220 on a `bool` store); no refusal. One spec sentence + executed example, then close. Rejected: extending SPY0336 to every untargeted value position on every receiver (refuses the common `f = obj.m` idiom for nothing typed; a→b later is a one-way tightening); refusing only the repr position (position-keyed, partial). |
| R-BG | #1952 | **Dedicated last group P20 + standing drain-as-you-touch rule** (2026-09-21, option a). Rejected: standing tracker only, opportunistic (never drains); per-bucket chores now (a blind mass-marking hides real bugs). |
| R-BH | #1960 | **No implicit collapse; explicit conversion** (2026-09-21, option d — owner: "have the programmer do explicit conversions that they are aware of … as little magic as possible … maybe later we can consider a bridge syntax/keyword but not now"): CLR interface sequence/mapping shapes keep CLR identity; `list(x)`/`dict(x)`/`set(x)` is the only conversion; #1251's implicit slot materialization is retired for a mismatch + steer. Tracking #1965 (P18b); parked door #1966. Rejected: (a) copy at every position (uniform but silently loses mutation through CLR collections, copies per read); (b) #1517's precedent for mutable shapes only with read-only shapes still snapshot-copied in slots (leaves an implicit copy); (c) Core view adapter (largest work, leaks the view type into the C# surface). All three neighbours are one-way relative to each other for code written against the other surface. |
| R-BI | #1974 | **P21 last, three sub-phases** (2026-09-22, D1 option a): P21 follows P20; 21a Stage 1 → 21b codemod + migration → 21c Stage 2, each its own plan and measured gate. Rejected: 21a now as a chore-sized slice with 21b/21c last (two declaration spellings in the corpus through P11b–P20 for no gain until the codemod runs); everything now before P11b. |
| R-BJ | #1974 | **Stage 1 ungated** (2026-09-22, D2 option a): `let` lands as an additive unconditional keyword, NOT behind a `feature-lifecycle.md` flag — the design is ruled, not under evaluation, and the ~2,450-file migration cannot run behind a gate; a keyword add is reversible by deletion. Rejected: experimental flag (would need a `.features` sidecar on every migrated fixture). |
| R-BK | #1974 | **`:=` unchanged** (2026-09-22, D3 option a): declare-if-new, rebind-if-existing — measured today (`x = 1; if (x := 2) > 0` prints `2 2`) and python3 agrees; `:=` remains the sole expression-position introducer after Stage 2. The issue's §2 sentence "`:=` already introduces a fresh binding" is corrected by this ruling. Rejected: fresh-always (diverges from Python; one-way once code depends on it). |
| R-BL | #1974 | **One new code + SPY0340 reuse** (2026-09-22, D4 option a): Stage 2's "assignment to undeclared name '<x>'; use `let`" is a new Semantic-overflow code (next free SPY0611); module-scope non-const bindings (`x: T = e` without keyword, `let x`) are SPY0340 with the steer "module-scope bindings are `const`" (they ARE executable statements at module level once annotation stops declaring). ## 5. Class findings still open (grep/measured at the sha named; hypotheses until the plan re-measures)

Only findings whose group is still open are kept; findings for done groups were retired with them.

1. **Qualifier-position typing** (c1bb9f7ca) — `_currentMemberAccessQualifier` / `ignoreQualifierGuard` at 11 sites in `GenericReferenceResolver.cs`, `TypeChecker.Expressions.Access.cs`, `TypeChecker.Expressions.cs`, `TypeChecker.cs`; the guard was added in 2249d4dcb (#1303) with no explanation (P16: #1941 #1909 #2038 #2082).
2. **`as?` lowering has no conversion arm** (c1bb9f7ca) — `RoslynEmitter.Expressions.Operators.cs:1090–1160`: null mode = numeric arms then a type pattern; throw mode = numeric arms then a catch-all cast. Every `Verdict` kind that is a conversion is a CS8121 candidate under `as?` (P17 #1892 #1947).
3. **Unknown doubles as permissive AND already-refused** (c1bb9f7ca) — refusal sites `return SemanticType.Unknown` (e.g. `Calls.cs:418`); the DP allowlist counts Unknown-returning sites; no error sentinel exists (P17 phase 1 #1946).
4. **CLR-namespace precedence** (c1bb9f7ca) — `ImportResolver.ModuleLoading.cs:908` checks `IsNetNamespace` before the search path; `ModuleRegistry.MapModuleToNamespace` hardcodes `system`/`sharpy` and probes every loaded assembly for other names (P18 phase 4 #1898 #2113 #2175).
5. **Position-dependent CLR-sequence conversion** (44cf5dddc, measured) — `RecordSequenceMaterialization` converts in slot positions only while the bridge's collapse types every position as `list[T]`; direct chains and CLR-mapped parameters emit the Pythonic name against the raw CLR type (LINQ `Append` silent no-op; CS1929 ICEs). Retired by R-BH (P18b).
6. **Constructor inference declines by shape, not by evidence** (44cf5dddc, measured) — `TryInferConstructorTypeArgs` requires exactly one `__init__`; no `params` arm; producer actuals (`str`) never bind producer formals; `Iterable[T]` ≠ `IEnumerable[T]` for unification (P17 #1951).
7. **The `auto` type name reaches Roslyn** (048a15574, measured) — the parser accepts `auto` as a type NAME (`Parser.Types.cs:186`) that the checker maps to Unknown, so `-> auto` alone reaches Roslyn (CS0246 → SPY0908) while `-> Foo` is SPY0202; dies with P21c.
8. **The bridge's two dishonest arm families** (048a15574, grep + measured) — (i) eleven collapse arms at `ClrTypeBridge.cs:464–656` map CLR interface collections onto Sharpy builtin names — retired by R-BH (P18b, #1965); (ii) the `object` give-up: the class arm's `IsClass || IsValueType` guard (`:333`) excludes interfaces, so non-generic interfaces, pointers and by-ref reach `UnmappedClrType` (`:356`), object-like in both directions — retired by R-BO (P18 phase 2, #1975). The `object` arm bites only on reflected signatures; the annotation route was already honest.
9. **Reverse-mangled surface ≠ emitter-bindable surface** (048a15574, grep) — `ReverseMangleNamesAMethod` enumerates public instance methods only, the resolver admits explicit-interface and underlying-only names (P18 #1969).
10. **Generic union case pattern does not qualify** (048a15574, grep) — `BuildUnionCaseTypeSyntax` generic arms (`Patterns.cs:894–909`) emit a bare `GenericName`; only the non-generic arm reaches `BuildNestedTypeName` (P16 #1971).
11. **Truthiness classifier misses two CLR shapes** (d17ddb956, grep) — no `BuiltinType`-with-`ClrType` arm in `ClassifyTruthiness` (`Utilities.cs:157–228`); `ClassifyClrTruthiness` (`:240`) reads two interfaces while `HasClrProtocol(__len__)` reads five (P15 #1994 #1982 #2198).
12. **`()` and bare `tuple` share one AST** (d17ddb956, grep) — `Parser.Types.cs:513` vs `:263`; `DecideBoundTypeTest` (`Utilities.cs:571`) cannot tell them apart; `TryResolveExpressionAsType` (`Access.cs:3299`) reads four node kinds of the whole type grammar (P16b).
13. **Instance-via-type refusal exempts CLR-backed types** (d17ddb956, measured d6a/d6b) — `TryRefuseInstanceMemberViaTypeName` refuses user classes (SPY0290) and lets CLR-backed symbols fall to the static arm at `Access.cs:2538` → a same-named CLR static or SPY0908 (P18 #1987, R-BU).
14. **Pipe route is a second call checker and a second callee speller** (d17ddb956, grep) — `Operators.cs:990–1135` resolves only `Identifier` callees, binds keywords verbatim (`:1098`) with private messages; `RoslynEmitter.Expressions.Operators.cs:389` spells names without the resolved symbol (P17c).
15. **`params` tail bound in normal form** (d17ddb956, read) — lone `None`, typed spread, the tail's own name and a sole `T[]` actual all reach the CLR `params` parameter as C# normal form would (P17b); no `params` roster exists.
16. **Synthesized constructors have no semantic twin** (acd1d40a2, measured) — `GenerateStructAutoConstructors` (`Constructors.cs:381`) and the class/dataclass auto-constructors are checked by Roslyn only; the struct's parameterless constructor is absent from the checker's set (#2023); the explicit-type-argument route runs its own overload branches (`Calls.cs:1170–1215`, #2042); no modifier comparison exists at the argument-binding seam (#2025) (P17d).
17. **The entry point is already a compilation option** (db8e05bf1, measured) — `AssemblyCompiler.cs:154` `WithMainTypeName(entryTypeName)` from `ProjectCompiler.Utilities.cs:27`; non-entry `def Main`/``def `Main` ``/`def main` all run on both routes, so the `MainFunc` rename has no remaining job (R-CR, P26).
18. **Two name-keyed rewrites in CodeGen** (db8e05bf1, filer-measured) — `AccessorParamRewrite(<name>, "value")` at six sites in `RoslynEmitter.ClassMembers.Properties.cs`/`Events.cs` and the `self` → `this` spelling; and the call-site resolution that reaches `Module.F` past a callable local (#2150) — "the emitter decides by name what a symbol-keyed fact should carry" (P21d).
19. **The stdlib's python-name rewrite is a generator special case** (db8e05bf1, read) — `regenerate_spy_stdlib.sh` `MODULES` carries `<stem>:<python>:<path>` and post-processes `[SharpyModuleType("<stem>", …)]`; R-DD replaces it with the directory rule + one prefix strip (P36).
20. **`max([])` reaches the emitter as an open `T`** (db8e05bf1, measured) — an untyped empty literal at a generic builtin argument is SPY0908 CS0246; the typed twin raises python's `ValueError` (cell on #2146, P17d).
21. **`Some()` is slot-directed only inside a call argument** (b1603d6a2, measured `probes/c_*.spy`) — `x == Some(1)` runs as a `print` argument and is SPY0227 in `assert`, `if`, `while`, ternary, bare assignment and under `not`; `CheckAssert` (`TypeChecker.Statements.cs:2394`) is one position of the class (P37 #2162).
22. **Two module-path authorities disagree on the root** (b1603d6a2, read) — `ProjectCompiler.Utilities.cs:87` roots at the common directory of all sources; `ProjectCompiler.Parsing.cs:202/267/418` call `CompilationUnitFactory.ComputeModulePath(file, config.ProjectDirectory)` which strips leading `..` (P26 #2160, the #2127 cell).
23. **Memoization is name-keyed in five files** (b1603d6a2, grep) — `DecoratorNames.LruCache` read in `TypeChecker.Definitions.cs`, `DecoratorValidator.cs`, `DecoratorValidator.Caching.cs`, `RoslynEmitter.TypeDeclarations.cs`, `RoslynEmitter.ClassMembers.LruCache.cs`; the siblings `NameCacheInfo`/`NameCacheClear` are emitted (`:383`) with no checker member (P25 #2165, R-DJ).
24. **The test-host marker family is lowered by name from the AST** (21ca52b5d, read) — `RoslynEmitter.Statements.cs:682–716` lowers all eight `assert_*` markers by callee name (P49 #2174).
25. **Narrowing is recorded by the emitter and not read by the checker's member lookup** (1e5d54c8f, measured `probes/m2248/`) — Recorded ≠ applied (P51 #2248).
26. **Spec-block allowlist** — 586 rows citing #1952 (44cf5dddc); the prelude mechanism landed; the reviewed per-bucket split is P20.
